0010414:                                 u32 prior_alignment =
0010415:                                     result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ? codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_alignment(prior_type) : 8;
0010416:                                 // A memory-class value's stack image is its own
0010417:                                 // rounded size; its part count may be the one
0010418:                                 // register it never actually takes.
0010419:                                 u64 prior_stack_image = prior_memory && prior_type->layout.resolved ? ((prior_type->layout.size + 7) & ~(u64)7)
0010420:                                                                                                     : (u64)prior_parts * 8;
0010421:                                 prior_stack_bytes = codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_offset(prior_stack_bytes, prior_alignment) + prior_stack_image;
0010422:                             }
0010423:                         }
0010424:                         u32 part_count = 1;
0010425:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &part_count);
0010426:                         CodegenCanonicalAbiValue argument_aggregate_abi =
0010427:                             codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, false, false);
0010428:                         // How many eightbytes the argument occupies if it came
0010429:                         // in on the stack, which is its size and not the number
0010430:                         // of registers it would have taken: one zmm holds a
0010431:                         // 64-byte vector, but its stack image is still eight.
0010432:                         IrType* argument_stack_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0010433:                         u32 stack_part_count =
0010434:                             argument_stack_type && argument_stack_type->layout.resolved ? (u32)((argument_stack_type->layout.size + 7) / 8) : part_count;
0010435:                         bool argument_in_registers = codegen_canonical_x64_abi_value_in_registers(&argument_aggregate_abi, &target);
0010436:                         if (argument_aggregate_abi.part_count && !argument_aggregate_abi.memory && !argument_aggregate_abi.indirect)
0010437:                         {
0010438:                             aggregate = true;
0010439:                             if (argument_in_registers)
0010440:                             {
0010441:                                 part_count = argument_aggregate_abi.part_count;
0010442:                             }
0010443:                         }
0010444:                         IrType* memory_aggregate_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0010445:                         if (argument_aggregate_abi.memory && memory_aggregate_type &&
0010446:                             (memory_aggregate_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || memory_aggregate_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION ||
0010447:                              memory_aggregate_type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY))
0010448:                         {
0010449:                             // A memory-class aggregate (one whose SysV walk hit a
0010450:                             // MEMORY field) reads back from the incoming stack
0010451:                             // area like any other stack aggregate.
0010452:                             aggregate = true;
0010453:                         }
0010454:                         bool windows_indirect = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS && argument_aggregate_abi.indirect;
0010455:                         IrType* argument_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0010456:                         bool argument_contains_f80 = codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type) &&
0010457:                                                      !(result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V &&
0010458:                                                        codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_bytes_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type));
0010459:                         if (argument_contains_f80)
0010460:                         {
0010461:                             if (result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V || !argument_type || argument_type->layout.size != 16 ||
0010462:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type) ||
0010463:                                 !argument_aggregate_abi.memory)
0010464:                             {
0010465:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0010466:                                 return result;
0010467:                             }
0010468:                             u64 argument_stack_offset = codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_offset(prior_stack_bytes, 16);
0010469:                             s64 source_displacement = (s64)16 + (s64)argument_stack_offset;
0010470:                             if (source_displacement > INT32_MAX ||
0010471:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0010472:                                                                       codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(
0010473:                                                                           &buffer, source_displacement, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset),
0010474:                                                                       X64_REGISTER_RBP, result_displacement, &x87_stack_depth))
0010475:                             {
0010476:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0010477:                                 return result;
0010478:                             }
0010479:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0010480:                             continue;
0010481:                         }
0010482:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && argument_type && argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0010483:                         {
0010484:                             if (argument_type->bit_width != 32 && argument_type->bit_width != 64)
0010485:                             {
0010486:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0010487:                                 return result;
0010488:                             }
0010489:                             if (float_register_index < 8)
0010490:                             {
0010491:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand float_store_operands[2] = {
0010492:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)argument_type->bit_width,
0010493:                                                                           result_displacement),
0010494:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(float_register_index, (u16)argument_type->bit_width),
0010495:                                 };
0010496:                                 String8 float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0010497:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0010498:                                         &buffer, argument_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD"), float_store_operands,
0010499:                                         BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_store_operands),
0010500:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = float_features,
0010501:                                                                          .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features)}))
0010502:                                 {
0010503:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0010504:                                     return result;
0010505:                                 }
0010506:                             }
0010507:                             else
0010508:                             {
0010509:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_argument_load_operands[2] = {
0010510:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0010511:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0010512:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0010513:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(
0010514:                                             &buffer, (s64)16 + (s64)codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_offset(prior_stack_bytes, 8),
0010515:                                             canonical_x64_frame_base_offset)),
0010516:                                 };
0010517:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), stack_argument_load_operands,
0010518:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_argument_load_operands)))
0010519:                                 {
0010520:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0010521:                                     return result;
0010522:                                 }
0010523:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0010524:                             }
0010525:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0010526:                             continue;
0010527:                         }
0010528:                         u16 windows_float_width = 0;
0010529:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS && register_index < register_count &&
0010530:                             codegen_canonical_x64_windows_float_argument(program, argument_type, &windows_float_width))
0010531:                         {
0010532:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand float_store_operands[2] = {
0010533:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, windows_float_width,
0010534:                                                                       result_displacement),
0010535:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(register_index, windows_float_width),
0010536:                             };
0010537:                             String8 float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0010538:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0010539:                                     &buffer, windows_float_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD"), float_store_operands,
0010540:                                     BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_store_operands),
0010541:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = float_features,
0010542:                                                                      .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features)}))
0010543:                             {
0010544:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0010545:                                 return result;
0010546:                             }
0010547:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0010548:                             continue;
0010549:                         }
0010550:                         u32 system_v_integer_parts = 0;
0010551:                         u32 system_v_float_parts = 0;
0010552:                         bool system_v_register_aggregate =
0010553:                             result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && argument_aggregate_abi.part_count && !argument_aggregate_abi.memory && argument_in_registers;
0010554:                         // "Aggregates over two eightbytes are MEMORY" is a rule
0010555:                         // about aggregates; a 32- or 64-byte vector arrives in
0010556:                         // a vector register on a target that has one that wide.
0010557:                         // The IR ABI and the target between them have already
0010558:                         // said which of the two this is, so the size heuristic
0010559:                         // only speaks when they did not. A memory class from
0010560:                         // the classifier (a single-lane double vector, or an
0010561:                         // aggregate whose walk hit a MEMORY field) is
0010562:                         // authoritative at any size.
0010563:                         bool system_v_memory = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && !system_v_register_aggregate &&
0010564:                                                (argument_aggregate_abi.memory ||
0010565:                                                 (aggregate && argument_type && argument_type->layout.size > 16));
0010566:                         if (system_v_register_aggregate)
0010567:                         {
0010568:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_aggregate_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0010569:                             {
0010570:                                 if (codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(argument_aggregate_abi.parts[part].abi_class))
0010571:                                 {
0010572:                                     system_v_float_parts += 1;
0010573:                                 }
0010574:                                 else
0010575:                                 {
0010576:                                     system_v_integer_parts += 1;
0010577:                                 }
0010578:                             }
0010579:                         }
0010580:                         u32 register_parts = windows_indirect ? 1 : part_count;
0010581:                         if (windows_indirect)
0010582:                         {
0010583:                             u32 self_piece_size = 0;
0010584:                             u32 self_pieces = codegen_canonical_x64_windows_vector_argument_pieces(&target, argument_type, &self_piece_size);
0010585:                             if (!self_pieces)
0010586:                             {
0010587:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0010588:                                 return result;
0010589:                             }
0010590:                             if (self_piece_size)
0010591:                             {
0010592:                                 // Gather the pieces in slot order: the leading
0010593:                                 // references still sit in their argument
0010594:                                 // registers (read before the stack-piece
0010595:                                 // copies clobber RDX, which is one of them),
0010596:                                 // the tail was stored past the shadow space.
0010597:                                 // Each piece's bytes land at its offset in the
0010598:                                 // parameter's frame slot.
0010599:                                 u32 available = register_index < register_count ? register_count - register_index : 0;
0010600:                                 u32 register_pieces = BUSTER_MIN(self_pieces, available);
0010601:                                 u64 argument_stack_offset = codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_offset(prior_stack_bytes, 8);
0010602:                                 for (u32 piece = 0; piece < self_pieces; piece += 1)
0010603:                                 {
0010604:                                     u64 piece_value_offset = (u64)piece * self_piece_size;
0010605:                                     if ((s64)result_displacement + (s64)piece_value_offset + self_piece_size > INT32_MAX)
0010606:                                     {
0010607:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0010608:                                         return result;
0010609:                                     }
0010610:                                     X64Register piece_pointer = X64_REGISTER_RAX;
0010611:                                     X64Register piece_data = X64_REGISTER_RDX;
0010612:                                     if (piece < register_pieces)
0010613:                                     {
0010614:                                         piece_pointer = (X64Register)registers[register_index + piece];
0010615:                                         piece_data = X64_REGISTER_RAX;
0010616:                                     }
0010617:                                     else
0010618:                                     {
0010619:                                         s64 slot_displacement =
0010620:                                             (s64)48 + (s64)argument_stack_offset + (s64)(piece - register_pieces) * 8;
0010621:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand piece_pointer_load_operands[2] = {
0010622:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0010623:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0010624:                                                 X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0010625:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(&buffer, slot_displacement,
0010626:                                                                                                 canonical_x64_frame_base_offset)),
0010627:                                         };
0010628:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), piece_pointer_load_operands,
0010629:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(piece_pointer_load_operands)))
0010630:                                         {
0010631:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0010632:                                             return result;
0010633:                                         }
0010634:                                     }
0010635:                                     for (u32 chunk = 0; chunk < self_piece_size / 8; chunk += 1)
0010636:                                     {
0010637:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand piece_load_operands[2] = {
0010638:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(piece_data, 64),
0010639:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(piece_pointer, 64, (s64)chunk * 8),
0010640:                                         };
0010641:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand piece_store_operands[2] = {
0010642:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0010643:                                                 X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, result_displacement + (s32)piece_value_offset + (s32)(chunk * 8)),
0010644:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(piece_data, 64),
0010645:                                         };
0010646:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), piece_load_operands,
0010647:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(piece_load_operands)) ||
0010648:                                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), piece_store_operands,
0010649:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(piece_store_operands)))
0010650:                                         {
0010651:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0010652:                                             return result;
0010653:                                         }
0010654:                                     }
0010655:                                 }
0010656:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0010657:                                 continue;
0010658:                             }
0010659:                         }
0010660:                         if (!aggregate)
0010661:                         {
0010662:                             IrType* parameter_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0010663:                             if (parameter_type && (parameter_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || parameter_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION))
0010664:                             {
0010665:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0010666:                                 return result;
0010667:                             }
0010668:                         }
0010669:                         if (system_v_memory || (system_v_register_aggregate ? register_index + system_v_integer_parts > register_count ||
0010670:                                                                                   float_register_index + system_v_float_parts > 8
0010671:                                                                             : register_index + register_parts > register_count))
0010672:                         {
0010673:                             u32 first_stack_offset = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS ? 48 : 16;
0010674:                             // The caller placed this one at its own alignment,
0010675:                             // so skip the padding it left behind.
0010676:                             u64 argument_stack_offset = codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_offset(
0010677:                                 prior_stack_bytes,
0010678:                                 result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ? codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_alignment(argument_stack_type) : 8);
0010679:                             if (windows_indirect)
0010680:                             {
0010681:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand indirect_stack_load_operands[2] = {
0010682:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),

0010687:                                 };

0010689:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(indirect_stack_load_operands)))
0010690:                                 {
0010691:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0010692:                                     return result;
0010693:                                 }
0010694:                                 for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0010695:                                 {
0010696:                                     u32 part_offset = part_index * 8;
0010697:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand indirect_part_load_operands[2] = {
0010698:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0010699:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, part_offset),
0010700:                                     };
0010701:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand indirect_part_store_operands[2] = {
0010702:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0010703:                                                                               result_displacement + (s32)(part_index * 8)),
0010704:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0010705:                                     };
0010706:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), indirect_part_load_operands,
0010707:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(indirect_part_load_operands)) ||
0010708:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), indirect_part_store_operands,
0010709:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(indirect_part_store_operands)))
0010710:                                     {
0010711:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0010712:                                         return result;
0010713:                                     }
0010714:                                 }
0010715:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0010716:                                 continue;
0010717:                             }
0010718:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < stack_part_count; part_index += 1)
0010719:                             {
0010720:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_load_operands[2] = {
0010721:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0010722:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0010723:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0010724:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(
0010725:                                             &buffer, first_stack_offset + (s64)argument_stack_offset + (s64)part_index * 8,
0010726:                                             canonical_x64_frame_base_offset)),
0010727:                                 };
0010728:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_store_operands[2] = {
0010729:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0010730:                                                                           result_displacement + (s32)(part_index * 8)),
0010731:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0010732:                                 };
0010733:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), stack_load_operands,
0010734:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_load_operands)) ||
0010735:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), stack_store_operands,
0010736:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_store_operands)))
0010737:                                 {
0010738:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0010739:                                     return result;
0010740:                                 }
0010741:                             }
0010742:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0010743:                             continue;
0010744:                         }
0010745:                         if (windows_indirect)
0010746:                         {
0010747:                             u8 source_reg = registers[register_index];
0010748:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0010749:                             {
0010750:                                 u32 part_offset = part_index * 8;
0010751:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand indirect_register_load_operands[2] = {
0010752:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0010753:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed((X64Register)source_reg, 64, part_offset),
0010754:                                 };
0010755:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand indirect_register_store_operands[2] = {
0010756:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0010757:                                                                           result_displacement + (s32)(part_index * 8)),
0010758:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0010759:                                 };
0010760:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), indirect_register_load_operands,
0010761:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(indirect_register_load_operands)) ||
