0011959:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &skip_branch, 1))
0011960:                                 {
0011961:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0011962:                                     return result;
0011963:                                 }
0011964:                                 u32 direct_conversion_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0011965:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands, 2, float_feature_input))
0011966:                                 {
0011967:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0011968:                                     return result;
0011969:                                 }
0011970:                                 u32 store_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0011971:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
0011972:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)target_bit_width, result_displacement),
0011973:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)target_bit_width),
0011974:                                 };
0011975:                                 String8 store_mnemonic = target_bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD");
0011976:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, store_mnemonic, store_operands, 2, float_feature_input))
0011977:                                 {
0011978:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0011979:                                     return result;
0011980:                                 }
0011981:                                 if (buffer.error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE && nonnegative_branch_offset + 2 <= buffer.count && skip_branch_offset + 2 <= buffer.count)
0011982:                                 {
0011983:                                     s8 nonnegative_delta = (s8)((s32)direct_conversion_offset - (s32)(nonnegative_branch_offset + 2));
0011984:                                     s8 skip_delta = (s8)((s32)store_offset - (s32)(skip_branch_offset + 2));
0011985:                                     memcpy(buffer.bytes + nonnegative_branch_offset + 1, &nonnegative_delta, sizeof(nonnegative_delta));
0011986:                                     memcpy(buffer.bytes + skip_branch_offset + 1, &skip_delta, sizeof(skip_delta));
0011987:                                 }
0011988:                                 else if (buffer.error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
0011989:                                 {
0011990:                                     buffer.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0011991:                                 }
0011992:                                 if (buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
0011993:                                 {
0011994:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0011995:                                     return result;
0011996:                                 }
0011997:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0011998:                                 continue;
0011999:                             }
0012000:                             if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT && source_bit_width != 64)
0012001:                             {
0012002:                                 String8 extend_mnemonic = source_bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSXD") : S8("MOVSX");
0012003:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_operands[2] = {
0012004:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012005:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width),
0012006:                                 };
0012007:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, extend_mnemonic, extend_operands,
0012008:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(extend_operands)))
0012009:                                 {
0012010:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0012011:                                     return result;
0012012:                                 }
0012013:                             }
0012014:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT && source_bit_width < 32)
0012015:                             {
0012016:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_extend_operands[2] = {
0012017:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0012018:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)source_bit_width),
0012019:                                 };
0012020:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), zero_extend_operands,
0012021:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(zero_extend_operands)))
0012022:                                 {
0012023:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0012024:                                     return result;
0012025:                                 }
0012026:                             }
0012027:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT && source_bit_width == 32)
0012028:                             {
0012029:                                 // C integer arguments occupy an eight-byte canonical
0012030:                                 // slot, while an unsigned 32-bit parameter is
0012031:                                 // defined by its low word.  Clear the incoming
0012032:                                 // high bits before the 64-bit conversion so a
0012033:                                 // caller that supplies a sign-extended register
0012034:                                 // value still observes unsigned semantics.
0012035:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand zero_extend_operands[2] = {
0012036:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0012037:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0012038:                                 };
0012039:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), zero_extend_operands,
0012040:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(zero_extend_operands)))
0012041:                                 {
0012042:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0012043:                                     return result;
0012044:                                 }
0012045:                             }
0012046:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand convert_operands[2] = {
0012047:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)target_bit_width),
0012048:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012049:                             };
0012050:                             String8 convert_mnemonic = target_bit_width == 32 ? S8("CVTSI2SS") : S8("CVTSI2SD");
0012051:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
0012052:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)target_bit_width, result_displacement),
0012053:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)target_bit_width),
0012054:                             };
0012055:                             String8 store_mnemonic = target_bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD");
0012056:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands,
0012057:                                                                               BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(convert_operands), float_feature_input) ||
0012058:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, store_mnemonic, store_operands,
0012059:                                                                               BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands), float_feature_input))
0012060:                             {
0012061:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012062:                                 return result;
0012063:                             }
0012064:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012065:                             continue;
0012066:                         }
0012067:                         else if (source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0012068:                                  (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ||
0012069:                                   (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width != 64)))
0012070:                         {
0012071:                             u32 source_bit_width = source_type->bit_width;
0012072:                             u32 target_bit_width = target_type->bit_width;
0012073:                             // Keep the conversion in the 64-bit signed range
0012074:                             // even for narrower C destinations.  This is
0012075:                             // required for unsigned 32-bit values above
0012076:                             // INT32_MAX (for example 4000000000.0), after
0012077:                             // which the canonical frame store narrows as
0012078:                             // usual.
0012079:                             u32 conversion_bit_width = 64;
0012080:                             if ((source_bit_width != 32 && source_bit_width != 64) ||
0012081:                                 (target_bit_width != 8 && target_bit_width != 16 && target_bit_width != 32 && target_bit_width != 64) ||
0012082:                                 (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER && target_bit_width == 64))
0012083:                             {
0012084:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012085:                                 return result;
0012086:                             }
0012087:                             String8 float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0012088:                             BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput float_feature_input = {
0012089:                                 .names = float_features,
0012090:                                 .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features),
0012091:                             };
0012092:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
0012093:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)source_bit_width),
0012094:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)source_bit_width,
0012095:                                                                        c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
0012096:                             };
0012097:                             String8 load_mnemonic = source_bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD");
0012098:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand convert_operands[2] = {
0012099:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, (u16)conversion_bit_width),
0012100:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)source_bit_width),
0012101:                             };
0012102:                             String8 convert_mnemonic = source_bit_width == 32 ? S8("CVTTSS2SI") : S8("CVTTSD2SI");
0012103:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, load_mnemonic, load_operands,
0012104:                                                                               BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands), float_feature_input) ||
0012105:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands,
0012106:                                                                               BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(convert_operands), float_feature_input))
0012107:                             {
0012108:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012109:                                 return result;
0012110:                             }
0012111:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012112:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012113:                             continue;
0012114:                         }
0012115:                         else if (source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0012116:                                  conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width == 64)
0012117:                         {
0012118:                             if (source_type->bit_width != 32 && source_type->bit_width != 64)
0012119:                             {
0012120:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012121:                                 return result;
0012122:                             }
0012123:                             String8 float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0012124:                             BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput float_feature_input = {
0012125:                                 .names = float_features,
0012126:                                 .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features),
0012127:                             };
0012128:                             u16 float_width = (u16)source_type->bit_width;
0012129:                             String8 load_mnemonic = source_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD");
0012130:                             String8 compare_mnemonic = source_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("UCOMISS") : S8("UCOMISD");
0012131:                             String8 subtract_mnemonic = source_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("SUBSS") : S8("SUBSD");
0012132:                             String8 convert_mnemonic = source_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("CVTTSS2SI") : S8("CVTTSD2SI");
0012133:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
0012134:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, float_width),
0012135:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, float_width,
0012136:                                                                        c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
0012137:                             };
0012138:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand threshold_load[2] = {
0012139:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 32 : 64),
0012140:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(
0012141:                                     source_type->bit_width == 32 ? CODEGEN_F32_SIGNED64_LIMIT_BITS : CODEGEN_F64_SIGNED64_LIMIT_BITS,
0012142:                                     source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 32 : 64),
0012143:                             };
0012144:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand threshold_vector[2] = {
0012145:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(1, float_width),
0012146:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 32 : 64),
0012147:                             };
0012148:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand compare_operands[2] = {
0012149:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, float_width),
0012150:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(1, float_width),
0012151:                             };
0012152:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand subtract_operands[2] = {
0012153:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, float_width),
0012154:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(1, float_width),
0012155:                             };
0012156:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand convert_operands[2] = {
0012157:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012158:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, float_width),
0012159:                             };
0012160:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_bit_load[2] = {
0012161:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0012162:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(UINT64_C(0x8000000000000000), 64),
0012163:                             };
0012164:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_bit_or[2] = {
0012165:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012166:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0012167:                             };
0012168:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, load_mnemonic, load_operands, 2, float_feature_input) ||
0012169:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), threshold_load, 2) ||
0012170:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, source_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVD") : S8("MOVQ"),
0012171:                                                                                 threshold_vector, 2, float_feature_input) ||
0012172:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, compare_mnemonic, compare_operands, 2, float_feature_input))
0012173:                             {
0012174:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012175:                                 return result;
0012176:                             }
0012177:                             u32 below_threshold_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0012178:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand below_threshold_branch = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 8);
0012179:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JB"), &below_threshold_branch, 1) ||
0012180:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, subtract_mnemonic, subtract_operands, 2, float_feature_input) ||
0012181:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands, 2, float_feature_input) ||
0012182:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), high_bit_load, 2) ||
0012183:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("OR"), high_bit_or, 2))
0012184:                             {
0012185:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012186:                                 return result;
0012187:                             }
0012188:                             u32 skip_direct_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0012189:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand skip_direct_branch = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 8);
0012190:                             u32 direct_conversion_offset = skip_direct_branch_offset + 2;
0012191:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &skip_direct_branch, 1) ||
0012192:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(&buffer, convert_mnemonic, convert_operands, 2, float_feature_input))
0012193:                             {
0012194:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012195:                                 return result;
0012196:                             }
0012197:                             s64 below_threshold_delta = (s64)direct_conversion_offset - ((s64)below_threshold_branch_offset + 2);
0012198:                             s64 skip_direct_delta = (s64)buffer.count - ((s64)skip_direct_branch_offset + 2);
0012199:                             if (below_threshold_delta < INT8_MIN || below_threshold_delta > INT8_MAX || skip_direct_delta < INT8_MIN ||
0012200:                                 skip_direct_delta > INT8_MAX || below_threshold_branch_offset + 1 >= buffer.count || skip_direct_branch_offset + 1 >= buffer.count)
0012201:                             {
0012202:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0012203:                                 return result;
0012204:                             }
0012205:                             buffer.bytes[below_threshold_branch_offset + 1] = (u8)(s8)below_threshold_delta;
0012206:                             buffer.bytes[skip_direct_branch_offset + 1] = (u8)(s8)skip_direct_delta;
0012207:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012208:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012209:                             continue;
0012210:                         }
0012211:                         else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_IDENTITY && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
0012212:                                  source_type->bit_width == target_type->bit_width &&
0012213:                                  (source_type->bit_width == 32 || source_type->bit_width == 64))
0012214:                         {
0012215:                             // Two float types with the same representation but
0012216:                             // different identity -- `double` assigned to a
0012217:                             // `volatile double`, which the frontend spells as
0012218:                             // an identity cast because the qualified type is
0012219:                             // its own IrType.  The eightbyte the load above
0012220:                             // already placed in RAX is the whole answer.
0012221:                         }
0012222:                         else
0012223:                         {
0012224:                             result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012225:                             return result;
0012226:                         }
0012227:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012228:                     }
0012229:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STORE || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE)
0012230:                     {
0012231:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
0012232:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0012233:                         u32 aggregate_parts = 0;
0012234:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type,
0012235:                                                                                    &aggregate_parts);
0012236:                         IrType* stored_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
0012237:                         if (!aggregate && stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved && stored_type->layout.size > 8 &&
0012238:                             stored_type->layout.size <= (u64)UINT32_MAX * 8)
0012239:                         {
0012240:                             aggregate = true;
0012241:                             aggregate_parts = (u32)((stored_type->layout.size + 7) / 8);
0012242:                         }
0012243:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0012244:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
0012245:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
0012246:                         bool stored_f80_opaque = codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_bytes_cached(
0012247:                                                      f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type) &&
0012248:                                                  instruction->opcode != IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V;
0012249:                         if (!stored_f80_opaque &&
0012250:                             codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type))
0012251:                         {
0012252:                             if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE || !stored_type || stored_type->layout.size != 16 ||
0012253:                                 result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
0012254:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program,
0012255:                                                                                      function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type))
0012256:                             {
0012257:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012258:                                 return result;
0012259:                             }
0012260:                             if (indirect)
0012261:                             {
0012262:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0012263:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(
0012264:                                          &buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value]),
0012265:                                          X64_REGISTER_RAX, 0, &x87_stack_depth))
0012266:                                 {
0012267:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012268:                                     return result;
0012269:                                 }
0012270:                             }
0012271:                             else if (!codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(
0012272:                                          &buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value]),
0012273:                                          X64_REGISTER_RBP, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]), &x87_stack_depth))
0012274:                             {
0012275:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012276:                                 return result;
0012277:                             }
0012278:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012279:                             continue;
0012280:                         }
0012281:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && aggregate)
0012282:                         {
0012283:                             // See the atomic load above: the width is the place's
0012284:                             // promoted size, the value's own size can be smaller.
0012285:                             IrType* atomic_place_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, place->canonical_type);
0012286:                             u64 atomic_width = atomic_place_type && atomic_place_type->layout.resolved ? atomic_place_type->layout.size : 0;
0012287:                             u64 atomic_stored_size = stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved ? stored_type->layout.size : 0;
0012288:                             bool aggregate_kind = stored_type && (stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION);
0012289:                             if (aggregate_kind && (atomic_width == 1 || atomic_width == 2 || atomic_width == 4 || atomic_width == 8))
0012290:                             {
0012291:                                 // The address goes first: the indirect form of
0012292:                                 // c_x64_atomic_address reloads the pointer through rax,
0012293:                                 // which is where the value is about to live.
0012294:                                 c_x64_atomic_address(&emitter, instruction->operands[0], indirect);
0012295:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
0012296:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_keep_low_bytes(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_stored_size);
0012297:                                 u16 atomic_store_width = (u16)(atomic_width * 8);
0012298:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand aggregate_store_operands[2] = {
0012299:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, atomic_store_width, 0),
0012300:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_store_width),
0012301:                                 };
0012302:                                 // Sequential consistency exchanges, exactly as the
0012303:                                 // scalar path below spells it; a weaker order is the
0012304:                                 // plain store an aligned x86 write already is.
0012305:                                 if (buffer.error ||
0012306:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer,
0012307:                                                                          instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL ? S8("XCHG") : S8("MOV"),
0012308:                                                                          aggregate_store_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(aggregate_store_operands)))
0012309:                                 {
0012310:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012311:                                     return result;
0012312:                                 }
0012313:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012314:                                 continue;
0012315:                             }

0012385:                             }

0012387:                             {

0012389:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, stored_type->layout.size - part_offset);

0012697:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
0012698:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("AND");
0012699:                                 break;
0012700:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
0012701:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("OR");
0012702:                                 break;
0012703:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
0012704:                                 operation_mnemonic = S8("XOR");
0012705:                                 break;
0012706:                             case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
0012707:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
0012708:                                 break;
0012709:                             }
0012710:                         }
0012711:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand operation_operands[2] = {
0012712:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width),
0012713:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_width),
0012714:                         };
0012715:                         if (!operation_mnemonic.length ||
0012716:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, operation_mnemonic, operation_operands,
0012717:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(operation_operands)) ||
0012718:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_atomic_register_memory(&buffer, S8("CMPXCHG"), X64_REGISTER_RDX,
0012719:                                                                                       X64_REGISTER_R8, atomic_width))
0012720:                         {
0012721:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0012722:                             return result;
0012723:                         }
0012724:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand retry_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0012725:                         u32 retry_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0012726:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &retry_operand, 1))
0012727:                         {
0012728:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0012729:                             return result;
0012730:                         }
0012731:                         s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry_offset - (s64)buffer.count;
0012732:                         if (retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX || !buffer.bytes)
0012733:                         {
0012734:                             result.error = retry_displacement < INT32_MIN || retry_displacement > INT32_MAX ? CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY
0012735:                                                                                                              : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012736:                             return result;
0012737:                         }
0012738:                         s32 retry_delta = (s32)retry_displacement;
0012739:                         memcpy(buffer.bytes + retry_branch_offset + 2, &retry_delta, sizeof(retry_delta));
0012740:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012741:                     }
0012742:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE)
0012743:                     {
0012744:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
0012745:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0012746:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0012747:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0012748:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
0012749:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
0012750:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->layout.size == 16)
0012751:                         {
0012752:                             if (!target_cpu_feature_has(target, TARGET_CPU_FEATURE_X86_CX16))
0012753:                             {
0012754:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012755:                                 return result;
0012756:                             }
0012757:                             c_x64_atomic_address(&emitter, instruction->operands[0], indirect);
0012758:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
0012759:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[1]);
0012760:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x9d, instruction->operands[2]);
0012761:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
0012762:                             String8 cx16_features_names[] = {S8("cx16")};
0012763:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cx16_memory = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_R10, 128, 0);
0012764:                             if (buffer.error ||
0012765:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_attributes(
0012766:                                     &buffer, S8("CMPXCHG16B"), &cx16_memory, 1,
0012767:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = cx16_features_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(cx16_features_names)},
0012768:                                     (BusterX86MetadataPhysicalAttributes){.lock = true}))
0012769:                             {
0012770:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012771:                                 return result;
0012772:                             }
0012773:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012774:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012775:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012776:                             continue;
0012777:                         }
0012778:                         if (!value_type || (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER) || !value_type->layout.resolved ||
0012779:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8))
0012780:                         {
0012781:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012782:                             return result;
0012783:                         }
0012784:                         if (indirect)
0012785:                         {
0012786:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
0012787:                         }
0012788:                         else
0012789:                         {
0012790:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand address_operands[2] = {
0012791:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0012792:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0012793:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value])),
0012794:                             };
0012795:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), address_operands,
0012796:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(address_operands));
0012797:                         }
0012798:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[1]);
0012799:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[2]);
0012800:                         if (buffer.error)
0012801:                         {
0012802:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0012803:                             return result;
0012804:                         }
0012805:                         u16 atomic_width = (u16)(value_type->layout.size * 8);
0012806:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_atomic_register_memory(&buffer, S8("CMPXCHG"), X64_REGISTER_RDX,
0012807:                                                                                      X64_REGISTER_RCX, atomic_width))
0012808:                         {
0012809:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0012810:                             return result;
0012811:                         }
0012812:                         if (value_type->layout.size == 1 || value_type->layout.size == 2)
0012813:                         {
0012814:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand widen_operands[2] = {
0012815:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0012816:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, atomic_width),
0012817:                             };
0012818:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), widen_operands,
0012819:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(widen_operands)))
0012820:                             {
0012821:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012822:                                 return result;
0012823:                             }
0012824:                         }
0012825:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012826:                     }
0012827:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_FENCE)
0012828:                     {
0012829:                         if (!instruction->atomic_signal_fence && instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL)
0012830:                         {
0012831:                             String8 fence_features[] = {S8("sse2")};
0012832:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0012833:                                     &buffer, S8("MFENCE"), 0, 0,
0012834:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = fence_features, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(fence_features)}))
0012835:                             {
0012836:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012837:                                 return result;
0012838:                             }
0012839:                         }
0012840:                     }
0012841:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CLEAR_INSTRUCTION_CACHE)
0012842:                     {
0012843:                     }
0012844:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT)
0012845:                     {
0012846:                         IrType* constant_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0012847:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && constant_type->bit_width == 80)
0012848:                         {
0012849:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(constant_type) || result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
0012850:                                 instruction->immediate_count != 2 ||
0012851:                                 (instruction->immediates[1] & ~UINT64_C(0xffff)) ||
0012852:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_store_f80_constant(&buffer, result_displacement, instruction->immediates[0],
0012853:                                                                            (u16)instruction->immediates[1]))
0012854:                             {
0012855:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012856:                                 return result;
0012857:                             }
0012858:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0012859:                             continue;
0012860:                         }
0012861:                         u64 immediate = instruction->immediates[0];
0012862:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && instruction->immediate_is_negative)
0012863:                         {
0012864:                             immediate = 0 - immediate;
0012865:                         }
0012866:                         u16 constant_width = codegen_canonical_register_is_64_bit(program, instruction->canonical_type) ? 64 : 32;
0012867:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand constant_value =
0012868:                             constant_width == 32 && immediate > UINT32_MAX
0012869:                                 ? codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)(s32)(u32)immediate, constant_width)
0012870:                                 : codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(immediate, constant_width);
0012871:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand constant_operands[2] = {
0012872:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, constant_width),
0012873:                             constant_value,
0012874:                         };
0012875:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), constant_operands,
0012876:                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(constant_operands)))
0012877:                         {
0012878:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0012879:                             return result;
0012880:                         }
0012881:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012882:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0012883:                             constant_type->bit_width == 128)
0012884:                         {
0012885:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_constant_operands[2] = {
0012886:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0012887:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(
0012888:                                     instruction->immediate_count > 1 ? instruction->immediates[1]
0012889:                                                                       : instruction->immediate_is_negative ? UINT64_MAX : 0,
0012890:                                     64),
0012891:                             };
0012892:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), high_constant_operands,
0012893:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_constant_operands)))
0012894:                             {
0012895:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0012896:                                 return result;
0012897:                             }
0012898:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012899:                         }
0012900:                     }
0012901:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_START)
0012902:                     {
0012903:                         if (!canonical_variadic)
0012904:                         {
0012905:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0012906:                             return result;
0012907:                         }
0012908:                         u32 va_list_component_count = codegen_canonical_va_list_component_count(program, instruction->canonical_type);
0012909:                         if (!va_list_component_count)
0012910:                         {
0012911:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0012912:                             return result;
0012913:                         }
0012914:                         u32 gp_count = 0;
0012915:                         u32 fp_count = 0;
0012916:                         u32 stack_parts = 0;
0012917:                         for (u32 parameter_index = 0; parameter_index < canonical_function_type->parameter_count; parameter_index += 1)
0012918:                         {
0012919:                             IrTypeId parameter_type_id = canonical_function_type->parameter_types[parameter_index];
0012920:                             IrType* parameter_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, parameter_type_id);
0012921:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, parameter_type_id))
0012922:                             {
0012923:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0012924:                                 return result;
0012925:                             }
0012926:                             u32 parts = 1;
0012927:                             bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, parameter_type_id, &parts);
0012928:                             if (parameter_type && parameter_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0012929:                             {
0012930:                                 if (fp_count < 8)
0012931:                                 {
0012932:                                     fp_count += 1;
0012933:                                 }
0012934:                                 else
0012935:                                 {
0012936:                                     stack_parts += 1;
0012937:                                 }
0012938:                             }
0012939:                             else if (aggregate ? gp_count + parts <= 6 : gp_count < 6)
0012940:                             {
0012941:                                 gp_count += parts;
0012942:                             }
0012943:                             else
0012944:                             {
0012945:                                 stack_parts += parts;
0012946:                             }
0012947:                         }
0012948:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V)
0012949:                         {
0012950:                             u64 offsets = (u64)(gp_count * 8) | ((u64)(48 + fp_count * 16) << 32);
0012951:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_offsets_operands[2] = {
0012952:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012953:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(offsets, 64),
0012954:                             };
0012955:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), va_offsets_operands,
0012956:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_offsets_operands));
0012957:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0012958:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_overflow_address_operands[2] = {
0012959:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012960:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, 16 + (s64)stack_parts * 8),
0012961:                             };
0012962:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_overflow_store_operands[2] = {
0012963:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, (s64)result_displacement + 8),
0012964:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012965:                             };
0012966:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), va_overflow_address_operands,
0012967:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_overflow_address_operands));
0012968:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), va_overflow_store_operands,
0012969:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_overflow_store_operands));
0012970:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_register_save_address_operands[2] = {
0012971:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0012972:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0012973:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0012974:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(&buffer, canonical_va_save_displacement,
0012975:                                                                                      canonical_x64_frame_base_offset)),
0012976:                             };
0012977:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), va_register_save_address_operands,
0012978:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_register_save_address_operands));
0012979:                         }
0012980:                         else
0012981:                         {
0012982:                             // The variadic tail starts after the named
0012983:                             // parameters' slots. Most named parameters take
0012984:                             // one slot; a pieced vector takes one per piece.
0012985:                             u64 named_slots = windows_indirect_return ? 1 : 0;
0012986:                             for (u32 named_index = 0; named_index < canonical_function_type->parameter_count; named_index += 1)
0012987:                             {
0012988:                                 u64 named_piece_count = 1;
0012989:                                 CodegenCanonicalAbiValue named_abi = codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(
0012990:                                     program, canonical_function_type->parameter_types[named_index], result.abi, false, false);
0012991:                                 if (named_abi.indirect)
0012992:                                 {
0012993:                                     u32 named_piece_size = 0;
0012994:                                     u32 named_pieces = codegen_canonical_x64_windows_vector_argument_pieces(
0012995:                                         &target, ir_type_from_id(&program->types, canonical_function_type->parameter_types[named_index]),
0012996:                                         &named_piece_size);
0012997:                                     if (named_pieces)
0012998:                                     {
0012999:                                         named_piece_count = named_pieces;
0013000:                                     }
0013001:                                 }
0013002:                                 named_slots += named_piece_count;
0013003:                             }
0013004:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_windows_address_operands[2] = {
0013005:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0013006:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0013007:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0013008:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(&buffer, 16 + (s64)named_slots * 8,
0013009:                                                                                      canonical_x64_frame_base_offset)),
0013010:                             };
0013011:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), va_windows_address_operands,
0013012:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_windows_address_operands));
0013013:                         }
0013014:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_save_area_operands[2] = {
0013015:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0013016:                                 X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, (s64)result_displacement + (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ? 16 : 0)),
0013017:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0013018:                         };
0013019:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_zero_operands[2] = {
0013020:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0013021:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0013022:                         };
0013023:                         (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), va_save_area_operands,
0013024:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_save_area_operands));
0013025:                         (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), va_zero_operands,
0013026:                                                                   BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_zero_operands));
0013027:                         u32 zero_start = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ? 24 : 8;
0013028:                         for (u32 offset = zero_start; offset < va_list_component_count * 8; offset += 8)
0013029:                         {
0013030:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_zero_store_operands[2] = {
0013031:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, (s64)result_displacement + offset),
0013032:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0013033:                             };
0013034:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), va_zero_store_operands,
0013035:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_zero_store_operands));
0013036:                         }
0013037:                     }
0013038:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_COPY)
0013039:                     {
0013040:                         u32 va_list_component_count = codegen_canonical_va_list_component_count(program, instruction->canonical_type);
0013041:                         if (!va_list_component_count)
0013042:                         {
0013043:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0013044:                             return result;
0013045:                         }
0013046:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0013047:                         for (u32 component = 0; component < va_list_component_count; component += 1)
0013048:                         {
0013049:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_copy_load_operands[2] = {
0013050:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013051:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, (s64)component * 8),
0013052:                             };
0013053:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand va_copy_store_operands[2] = {
0013054:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, (s64)result_displacement + component * 8),
0013055:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013056:                             };
0013057:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), va_copy_load_operands,
0013058:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_copy_load_operands));
0013059:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), va_copy_store_operands,
0013060:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(va_copy_store_operands));
0013061:                         }
0013062:                     }
0013063:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_END)
0013064:                     {
0013065:                         // va_end is a semantic lifetime marker.  The native
0013066:                         // representations used here do not require a
0013067:                         // destructive operation, and writing a fixed fourth
0013068:                         // word would exceed pointer-sized Windows va_list
0013069:                         // objects.
0013070:                     }
0013071:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_ARG)
0013072:                     {
0013073:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0013074:                         // An aggregate that merely carries an f80 payload
0013075:                         // crosses the boundary as ordinary eightbytes -- musl's
0013076:                         // `union ldshape` rides two general-purpose registers --
0013077:                         // so it reads back through the generic path below.  A
0013078:                         // value whose own classification is the X87/X87_UP pair
0013079:                         // does not: System V sends that pair to memory, so the
0013080:                         // caller left it in a sixteen-aligned overflow slot and
0013081:                         // never in the register save area, and its read is the
0013082:                         // overflow arm alone.
0013083:                         bool va_arg_f80_opaque = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V &&
0013084:                                                  codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_opaque_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type);
0013085:                         if (!va_arg_f80_opaque && codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type))
0013086:                         {
0013087:                             if (!value_type || value_type->layout.size != 16 || result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
0013088:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type))
0013089:                             {
0013090:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0013091:                                 return result;
0013092:                             }
0013093:                             // Realign the overflow cursor to the sixteen the
0013094:                             // caller placed the value at, take the slot, and
0013095:                             // advance past it.  The payload is then copied
0013096:                             // through the x87 stack the way every other f80
0013097:                             // copy here is, which is what leaves the six
0013098:                             // padding bytes of the destination zeroed rather
0013099:                             // than carrying whatever the caller's slot held.
0013100:                             // The copy's own scratch is RAX, so the va_list
0013101:                             // object is finished with before it runs.
0013102:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0013103:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand f80_overflow_load_operands[2] = {
0013104:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013105:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013106:                             };
0013107:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand f80_align_add_operands[2] = {
0013108:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013109:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(15, 8),
0013110:                             };
0013111:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand f80_align_and_operands[2] = {
0013112:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013113:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(-16, 8),
0013114:                             };
0013115:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand f80_cursor_store_operands[2] = {
0013116:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013117:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013118:                             };
0013119:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand f80_cursor_advance_operands[2] = {
0013120:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013121:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)value_type->layout.size, 32),
0013122:                             };
0013123:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), f80_overflow_load_operands,
0013124:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(f80_overflow_load_operands)) ||
0013125:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), f80_align_add_operands,
0013126:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(f80_align_add_operands)) ||
0013127:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("AND"), f80_align_and_operands,
0013128:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(f80_align_and_operands)) ||
0013129:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), f80_cursor_store_operands,
0013130:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(f80_cursor_store_operands)) ||
0013131:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), f80_cursor_advance_operands,
0013132:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(f80_cursor_advance_operands)) ||
0013133:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_copy(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RDX, 0, X64_REGISTER_RBP, result_displacement,
0013134:                                                                        &x87_stack_depth))
0013135:                             {
0013136:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0013137:                                 return result;
0013138:                             }
0013139:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0013140:                             continue;
0013141:                         }
0013142:                         u32 integer_parts = 0;
0013143:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &integer_parts);
0013144:                         bool floating = value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT;
0013145:                         CodegenCanonicalAbiValue aggregate_abi = codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, false, true);
0013146:                         if (!value_type || !value_type->layout.size || value_type->layout.size > 16 ||
0013147:                             (!aggregate && !floating && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN &&
0013148:                              value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER))
0013149:                         {
0013150:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0013151:                             return result;
0013152:                         }
0013153:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0013154:                         bool split_system_v_aggregate =
0013155:                             result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && aggregate_abi.part_count && !aggregate_abi.memory && !aggregate_abi.indirect;
0013156:                         u32 split_integer_count = 0;
0013157:                         u32 split_float_count = 0;
0013158:                         if (split_system_v_aggregate)
0013159:                         {
0013160:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < aggregate_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0013161:                             {
0013162:                                 if (codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(aggregate_abi.parts[part].abi_class))
0013163:                                 {
0013164:                                     split_float_count += 1;
0013165:                                 }
0013166:                                 else
0013167:                                 {
0013168:                                     split_integer_count += 1;
0013169:                                 }
0013170:                             }
0013171:                         }
0013172:                         if (split_system_v_aggregate && split_float_count)
0013173:                         {
0013174:                             u32 overflow_branch_offsets[2] = {0};
0013175:                             u32 overflow_branch_sizes[2] = {0};
0013176:                             u32 overflow_branch_count = 0;
0013177:                             if (split_integer_count)
0013178:                             {
0013179:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand gp_offset_load_operands[2] = {
0013180:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 32),
0013181:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32, 0),
0013182:                                 };
0013183:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand gp_offset_compare_operands[2] = {
0013184:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 32),
0013185:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(48 - split_integer_count * 8, 32),
0013186:                                 };
0013187:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), gp_offset_load_operands,
0013188:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(gp_offset_load_operands)) ||
0013189:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CMP"), gp_offset_compare_operands,
0013190:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(gp_offset_compare_operands)))
0013191:                                 {
0013192:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013193:                                     return result;
0013194:                                 }
0013195:                                 u32 branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013196:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0013197:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNBE"), &overflow_branch_operand, 1))
0013198:                                 {
0013199:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013200:                                     return result;
0013201:                                 }
0013202:                                 u32 branch_size = (u32)buffer.count - branch_offset;
0013203:                                 if (branch_size < 4 || overflow_branch_count >= BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_branch_offsets))
0013204:                                 {
0013205:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0013206:                                     return result;
0013207:                                 }
0013208:                                 overflow_branch_offsets[overflow_branch_count] = branch_offset;
0013209:                                 overflow_branch_sizes[overflow_branch_count] = branch_size;
0013210:                                 overflow_branch_count += 1;
0013211:                             }
0013212:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand fp_offset_load_operands[2] = {
0013213:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, 32),
0013214:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32, 4),
0013215:                             };
0013216:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand fp_offset_compare_operands[2] = {
0013217:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, 32),
0013218:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(176 - split_float_count * 16, 32),
0013219:                             };
0013220:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), fp_offset_load_operands,
0013221:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(fp_offset_load_operands)) ||
0013222:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CMP"), fp_offset_compare_operands,
0013223:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(fp_offset_compare_operands)))
0013224:                             {
0013225:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013226:                                 return result;
0013227:                             }
0013228:                             u32 fp_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013229:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand fp_overflow_branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0013230:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNBE"), &fp_overflow_branch_operand, 1))
0013231:                             {
0013232:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013233:                                 return result;
0013234:                             }
0013235:                             u32 fp_branch_size = (u32)buffer.count - fp_branch_offset;
0013236:                             if (fp_branch_size < 4 || overflow_branch_count >= BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_branch_offsets))
0013237:                             {
0013238:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0013239:                                 return result;
0013240:                             }
0013241:                             overflow_branch_offsets[overflow_branch_count] = fp_branch_offset;
0013242:                             overflow_branch_sizes[overflow_branch_count] = fp_branch_size;
0013243:                             overflow_branch_count += 1;
0013244: 
0013245:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand register_save_area_load_operands[2] = {
0013246:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),

0013248:                             };
0013249:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), register_save_area_load_operands,
0013250:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(register_save_area_load_operands)))
0013251:                             {
0013252:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013253:                                 return result;
0013254:                             }
0013255:                             u32 integer_part = 0;
0013256:                             u32 float_part = 0;
0013257:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < aggregate_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0013258:                             {

0013261:                                 X64Register index_register = part_float ? X64_REGISTER_R8 : X64_REGISTER_RCX;
