0013267:                                     part_memory.memory.index = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(index_register, 64).reg;
0013268:                                     part_memory.memory.scale = 1;
0013269:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand part_load_operands[2] = {
0013270:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0013271:                                         part_memory,
0013272:                                     };
0013273:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand part_store_operands[2] = {
0013274:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0013275:                                             X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0013276:                                             (s64)result_displacement + (s64)aggregate_abi.parts[part].value_offset + copied),
0013277:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0013278:                                     };
0013279:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), part_load_operands,
0013280:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(part_load_operands)) ||
0013281:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), part_store_operands,
0013282:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(part_store_operands)))
0013283:                                     {
0013284:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0013285:                                         return result;
0013286:                                     }
0013287:                                 }
0013288:                             }
0013289:                             if (split_integer_count)
0013290:                             {
0013291:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand gp_offset_advance_operands[2] = {
0013292:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32, 0),
0013293:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(split_integer_count * 8, 8),
0013294:                                 };
0013295:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), gp_offset_advance_operands,
0013296:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(gp_offset_advance_operands)))
0013297:                                 {
0013298:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013299:                                     return result;
0013300:                                 }
0013301:                             }
0013302:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand fp_offset_advance_operands[2] = {

0013304:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(split_float_count * 16, 8),
0013305:                             };
0013306:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), fp_offset_advance_operands,
0013307:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(fp_offset_advance_operands)))
0013308:                             {
0013309:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013310:                                 return result;
0013311:                             }
0013312:                             u32 end_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013313:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand end_branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0013314:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &end_branch_operand, 1))
0013315:                             {
0013316:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013317:                                 return result;
0013318:                             }

0013320:                             if (end_branch_size < 4)
0013321:                             {

0013323:                                 return result;
0013324:                             }
0013325:                             u32 overflow_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013326:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_area_load_operands[2] = {
0013327:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013328:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013329:                             };
0013330:                             u32 stack_size = (u32)((value_type->layout.size + 7) & ~(u64)7);
0013331:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_area_advance_operands[2] = {
0013332:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013333:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)stack_size, 32),
0013334:                             };
0013335:                             bool overflow_ready = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), overflow_area_load_operands,
0013336:                                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_area_load_operands));
0013337:                             if (overflow_ready && value_type->layout.alignment > 8)
0013338:                             {
0013339:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand align_add[] = {
0013340:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013341:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(15, 8),
0013342:                                 };

0013344:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013345:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(-16, 8),
0013346:                                 };

0013348:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013349:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013350:                                 };
0013351:                                 overflow_ready = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), align_add, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(align_add)) &&
0013352:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("AND"), align_mask, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(align_mask)) &&
0013353:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), aligned_cursor, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(aligned_cursor));
0013354:                             }
0013355:                             if (!overflow_ready || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), overflow_area_advance_operands,
0013356:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_area_advance_operands)))
0013357:                             {
0013358:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013359:                                 return result;
0013360:                             }
0013361:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < stack_size / 8u; part += 1)
0013362:                             {
0013363:                                 u32 part_offset = part * 8;
0013364:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_part_load_operands[2] = {

0013380:                                     return result;
0013381:                                 }

0013384:                             for (u32 branch = 0; branch < overflow_branch_count; branch += 1)
0013385:                             {

0013388:                                 u32 field_offset = branch_offset + branch_size - 4;
0013389:                                 s64 delta = (s64)overflow_offset - ((s64)branch_offset + branch_size);
0013390:                                 if (delta < INT32_MIN || delta > INT32_MAX || field_offset + sizeof(s32) > buffer.count)
0013391:                                 {

0013393:                                     return result;
0013394:                                 }

0013396:                                 memcpy(buffer.bytes + field_offset, &displacement, sizeof(displacement));
0013397:                             }
0013398:                             u32 end_field_offset = end_branch_offset + end_branch_size - 4;
0013399:                             s64 end_delta = (s64)end_offset - ((s64)end_branch_offset + end_branch_size);
0013400:                             if (end_delta < INT32_MIN || end_delta > INT32_MAX || end_field_offset + sizeof(s32) > buffer.count)
0013401:                             {
0013402:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013403:                                 return result;
0013404:                             }

0013406:                             memcpy(buffer.bytes + end_field_offset, &end_displacement, sizeof(end_displacement));
0013407:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0013408:                             continue;
0013409:                         }
0013410:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS)
0013411:                         {

0013451:                         else
0013452:                         {
0013453:                             u32 descriptor_offset = floating ? 4 : 0;
0013454:                             u32 part_count = aggregate ? integer_parts : 1;
0013455:                             u32 increment = floating ? 16 : part_count * 8;
0013456:                             u32 limit = floating ? 176 - increment : 48 - increment;
0013457:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand descriptor_load_operands[2] = {
0013458:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 32),
0013459:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32, descriptor_offset),
0013460:                             };

0013462:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 32),
0013463:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(limit, 32),
0013464:                             };
0013465:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), descriptor_load_operands,
0013466:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(descriptor_load_operands));
0013467:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CMP"), descriptor_compare_operands,
0013468:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(descriptor_compare_operands));
0013469:                             u32 overflow_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013470:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_branch = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0013471:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNBE"), &overflow_branch, 1);
0013472:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand register_save_load_operands[2] = {
0013473:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013474:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 16),
0013475:                             };
0013476:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand register_save_add_operands[2] = {
0013477:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013478:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0013479:                             };
0013480:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand descriptor_advance_operands[2] = {
0013481:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32, descriptor_offset),
0013482:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(increment, 8),
0013483:                             };
0013484:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), register_save_load_operands,
0013485:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(register_save_load_operands));
0013486:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), register_save_add_operands,
0013487:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(register_save_add_operands));
0013488:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), descriptor_advance_operands,
0013489:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(descriptor_advance_operands));
0013490:                             u32 end_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013491:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand end_branch = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0013492:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &end_branch, 1);
0013493:                             u32 overflow_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013494:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_load_operands[2] = {
0013495:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013496:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013497:                             };
0013498:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), overflow_load_operands,
0013499:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_load_operands));
0013500:                             // The caller placed an over-aligned argument at its
0013501:                             // own alignment, so the overflow cursor has to skip
0013502:                             // the same padding before reading one back. Only
0013503:                             // sixteen is reachable: a wider type is refused
0013504:                             // above for being larger than two eightbytes.
0013505:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_stack_argument_alignment(value_type) > 8)
0013506:                             {
0013507:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_align_add_operands[2] = {
0013508:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013509:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(15, 8),
0013510:                                 };
0013511:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_align_and_operands[2] = {
0013512:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013513:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(-16, 8),
0013514:                                 };
0013515:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_cursor_store_operands[2] = {
0013516:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013517:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0013518:                                 };
0013519:                                 (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), overflow_align_add_operands,
0013520:                                                                           BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_align_add_operands));
0013521:                                 (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("AND"), overflow_align_and_operands,
0013522:                                                                           BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_align_and_operands));
0013523:                                 (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), overflow_cursor_store_operands,
0013524:                                                                           BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_cursor_store_operands));
0013525:                             }
0013526:                             u32 stack_size = (u32)((value_type->layout.size + 7) & ~(u64)7);
0013527:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand overflow_advance_operands[2] = {
0013528:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64, 8),
0013529:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(stack_size, 32),
0013530:                             };
0013531:                             (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), overflow_advance_operands,
0013532:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(overflow_advance_operands));
0013533:                             u32 common_copy_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0013534:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < (aggregate ? integer_parts : 1); part += 1)
0013535:                             {
0013536:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand register_part_load_operands[2] = {
0013537:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, 64),
0013538:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64, (s64)part * 8),
0013539:                                 };
0013540:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand register_part_store_operands[2] = {
0013541:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, (s64)result_displacement + (s64)part * 8),
0013542:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R8, 64),
0013543:                                 };
0013544:                                 (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), register_part_load_operands,
0013545:                                                                           BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(register_part_load_operands));
0013546:                                 (void)codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), register_part_store_operands,
0013547:                                                                           BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(register_part_store_operands));
0013548:                             }
0013549:                             if (buffer.error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE && overflow_branch_offset + 6 <= buffer.count && end_branch_offset + 5 <= buffer.count)
0013550:                             {
0013551:                                 s32 overflow_delta = (s32)(overflow_offset - (overflow_branch_offset + 6));
0013552:                                 s32 end_delta = (s32)(common_copy_offset - (end_branch_offset + 5));
0013553:                                 memcpy(buffer.bytes + overflow_branch_offset + 2, &overflow_delta, sizeof(overflow_delta));
0013554:                                 memcpy(buffer.bytes + end_branch_offset + 1, &end_delta, sizeof(end_delta));
0013555:                             }
0013556:                             else if (buffer.error == CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
0013557:                             {
0013558:                                 buffer.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013559:                             }
0013560:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0013561:                             continue;
0013562:                         }
0013563:                         for (u32 part = 0; part < (aggregate ? integer_parts : 1); part += 1)
0013564:                         {
0013565:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
0013566:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0013567:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64, (s64)part * 8),
0013568:                             };
0013569:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
0013570:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0013571:                                                                       (s64)result_displacement + (s64)part * 8),
0013572:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0013573:                             };
0013574:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), load_operands,
0013575:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands)) ||
0013576:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), store_operands,
0013577:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands)))
0013578:                             {
0013579:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013580:                                 return result;
0013581:                             }
0013582:                         }
0013583:                     }
0013584:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CALL)
0013585:                     {
0013586:                         CodegenCanonicalCallLayout call_layout = {0};
0013587:                         u32 call_instruction_index = (u32)(instruction - function->instructions);
0013588:                         if (call_layout_cache && call_layout_cache[call_instruction_index])
0013589:                         {
0013590:                             call_layout = *call_layout_cache[call_instruction_index];
0013591:                         }
0013592:                         else
0013593:                         {
0013594:                             CodegenError call_error = codegen_canonical_x64_call_layout_cached(arena, program, f80_cache, function, instruction,
0013595:                                                                                                   result.abi, target, &call_layout);
0013596:                             if (call_error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
0013597:                             {
0013598:                                 result.error = call_error;
0013599:                                 return result;
0013600:                             }
0013601:                         }
0013602:                         static u8 const system_v[] = {
0013603:                             7, 6, 2, 1, 8, 9,
0013604:                         };
0013605:                         static u8 const windows[] = {
0013606:                             1,
0013607:                             2,
0013608:                             8,
0013609:                             9,
0013610:                         };
0013611:                         u8 const* registers = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS ? windows : system_v;
0013612:                         u32 register_count = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS ? BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(windows) : BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(system_v);
0013613:                         u32 argument_count = call_layout.argument_count;
0013614:                         CodegenCanonicalCallArgument* arguments = call_layout.arguments;
0013615:                         CodegenCanonicalAbiValue call_return_abi = call_layout.return_abi;
0013616:                         bool call_windows_indirect_return = call_layout.windows_indirect_return;
0013617:                         bool call_x64_indirect_return = call_layout.indirect_return;
0013618:                         u32 simulated_float_registers = call_layout.simulated_float_registers;
0013619:                         // An argument wanting more than the sixteen bytes the
0013620:                         // stack pointer is already worth cannot be reached by
0013621:                         // pushing: where the pushes leave it depends on where
0013622:                         // the stack happened to be. Such a call moves the stack
0013623:                         // pointer down to the alignment the area needs instead,
0013624:                         // writes each argument at its own offset within it, and
0013625:                         // puts the stack pointer back from a frame slot after,
0013626:                         // which is the one restore an `and` cannot undo and a
0013627:                         // dynamically grown stack does not invalidate.
0013628:                         bool system_v_aligned_area = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && call_layout.stack_part_count &&
0013629:                                                      call_layout.stack_alignment > CODEGEN_X64_STACK_ALIGNMENT;
0013630:                         if (system_v_aligned_area && !x64_stack_save_offset)
0013631:                         {
0013632:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0013633:                             return result;
0013634:                         }
0013635:                         bool stack_padding = call_layout.stack_padding && !system_v_aligned_area;
0013636:                         if (stack_padding)
0013637:                         {
0013638:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_padding_operands[2] = {
0013639:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSP, 64),
0013640:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(8, 8),
0013641:                             };
0013642:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("SUB"), stack_padding_operands,
0013643:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_padding_operands)))
0013644:                             {
0013645:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013646:                                 return result;
0013647:                             }
0013648:                         }
0013649:                         if (system_v_aligned_area)
0013650:                         {
0013651:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_save_operands[2] = {
0013652:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, x64_stack_save_offset)),
0013653:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSP, 64),
0013654:                             };
0013655:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), stack_save_operands,
0013656:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_save_operands)))
0013657:                             {
0013658:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013659:                                 return result;
0013660:                             }
0013661:                             codegen_canonical_x64_adjust_stack(&buffer, call_layout.stack_part_count * 8, true);
0013662:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_align_operands[2] = {
0013663:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSP, 64),
0013664:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(-(s64)call_layout.stack_alignment, 32),
0013665:                             };
0013666:                             if (buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ||
0013667:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("AND"), stack_align_operands,
0013668:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_align_operands)))
0013669:                             {
0013670:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013671:                                 return result;
0013672:                             }
0013673:                             for (u32 argument_index = 0; argument_index < argument_count; argument_index += 1)
0013674:                             {
0013675:                                 CodegenCanonicalCallArgument* call_argument = arguments + argument_index;
0013676:                                 if (!call_argument->on_stack)
0013677:                                 {
0013678:                                     continue;
0013679:                                 }
0013680:                                 u32 argument_offset = value_offsets[instruction->operands[argument_index + 1].value];
0013681:                                 // No rounding here: a System V stack argument
0013682:                                 // is already at an offset respecting its own
0013683:                                 // alignment inside an area the call aligned to
0013684:                                 // the widest of them, so the slot address is
0013685:                                 // whatever the stack pointer already is.
0013686:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_copy_frame_to_rsp(&buffer, argument_offset, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset,
0013687:                                                                              call_argument->stack_offset, call_argument->stack_part_count * 8,
0013688:                                                                              CODEGEN_X64_STACK_ALIGNMENT))
0013689:                                 {
0013690:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013691:                                     return result;
0013692:                                 }
0013693:                             }
0013694:                         }
0013695:                         bool windows_dynamic_call = windows_dynamic_stack && result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS;
0013696:                         if (windows_dynamic_call)
0013697:                         {
0013698:                             codegen_canonical_x64_adjust_stack(&buffer, call_layout.windows_stack_size, true);
0013699:                             if (buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
0013700:                             {
0013701:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013702:                                 return result;
0013703:                             }
0013704:                         }
0013705:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS)
0013706:                         {
0013707:                             for (u32 argument_index = 0; argument_index < argument_count; argument_index += 1)
0013708:                             {
0013709:                                 CodegenCanonicalCallArgument* call_argument = arguments + argument_index;
0013710:                                 if (call_argument->windows_indirect &&
0013711:                                     (value_offsets[instruction->operands[argument_index + 1].value] > UINT32_MAX - call_argument->copy_size ||
0013712:                                      !codegen_canonical_x64_copy_frame_to_rsp(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[argument_index + 1].value],
0013713:                                                                                canonical_x64_frame_base_offset, call_argument->copy_offset,
0013714:                                                                                call_argument->copy_size, call_argument->copy_alignment)))
0013715:                                 {
0013716:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013717:                                     return result;
0013718:                                 }
0013719:                             }
0013720:                         }
0013721:                         // The pushes build the area downward, so an argument is
0013722:                         // preceded by whatever padding sits above it: emit that
0013723:                         // first and the one below it lands on its own offset.
0013724:                         u32 stack_cursor = system_v_aligned_area ? 0 : call_layout.stack_part_count * 8;
0013725:                         for (u32 argument_reverse_index = argument_count; argument_reverse_index > 0 && !system_v_aligned_area; argument_reverse_index -= 1)
0013726:                         {
0013727:                             u32 array_index = argument_reverse_index - 1;
0013728:                             if (!arguments[array_index].on_stack || result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V)
0013729:                             {
0013730:                                 continue;
0013731:                             }
0013732:                             IrValueId argument = instruction->operands[argument_reverse_index];
0013733:                             for (u32 padding = arguments[array_index].stack_offset + arguments[array_index].stack_part_count * 8; padding < stack_cursor;
0013734:                                  padding += 8)
0013735:                             {
0013736:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand push_padding_operand =
0013737:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
0013738:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("PUSH"), &push_padding_operand, 1))
0013739:                                 {
0013740:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013741:                                     return result;
0013742:                                 }
0013743:                             }
0013744:                             for (u32 part_index = arguments[array_index].stack_part_count; part_index > 0; part_index -= 1)
0013745:                             {
0013746:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand push_argument_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0013747:                                     X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0013748:                                     c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[argument.value]) + (s32)((part_index - 1) * 8));
0013749:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("PUSH"), &push_argument_operand, 1))
0013750:                                 {
0013751:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013752:                                     return result;
0013753:                                 }
0013754:                             }
0013755:                             stack_cursor = arguments[array_index].stack_offset;
0013756:                         }
0013757:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS)
0013758:                         {
0013759:                             u32 stack_index = 0;
0013760:                             for (u32 argument_index = 0; argument_index < argument_count; argument_index += 1)
0013761:                             {
0013762:                                 if (!arguments[argument_index].on_stack)
0013763:                                 {
0013764:                                     continue;
0013765:                                 }
0013766:                                 IrValueId argument = instruction->operands[argument_index + 1];
0013767:                                 if (arguments[argument_index].windows_piece_size)
0013768:                                 {
0013769:                                     // The pieces that did not fit in registers
0013770:                                     // continue as stack slots: each gets the
0013771:                                     // rounded-up address of its slice of the
0013772:                                     // caller-owned copy.
0013773:                                     for (u32 piece = arguments[argument_index].windows_register_piece_count;
0013774:                                          piece < arguments[argument_index].part_count; piece += 1)
0013775:                                     {
0013776:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_rsp_address(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RAX, arguments[argument_index].copy_offset,
0013777:                                                                                 arguments[argument_index].copy_alignment))
0013778:                                         {
0013779:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013780:                                             return result;
0013781:                                         }
0013782:                                         if (piece)
0013783:                                         {
0013784:                                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand piece_advance_operands[2] = {
0013785:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0013786:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)piece * arguments[argument_index].windows_piece_size,
0013787:                                                                                           32),
0013788:                                             };
0013789:                                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), piece_advance_operands,
0013790:                                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(piece_advance_operands)))
0013791:                                             {
0013792:                                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013793:                                                 return result;
0013794:                                             }
0013795:                                         }
0013796:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand piece_store_operands[2] = {
0013797:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RSP, 64, 32 + (s64)stack_index * 8),
0013798:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0013799:                                         };
0013800:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), piece_store_operands,
0013801:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(piece_store_operands)))
0013802:                                         {
0013803:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0013804:                                             return result;
0013805:                                         }
0013806:                                         stack_index += 1;
0013807:                                     }
0013808:                                     continue;
0013809:                                 }
0013810:                                 if (arguments[argument_index].windows_indirect)
0013811:                                 {
0013812:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_rsp_address(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RAX, arguments[argument_index].copy_offset,
0013813:                                                                             arguments[argument_index].copy_alignment))
0013814:                                     {
0013815:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013816:                                         return result;
0013817:                                     }
0013818:                                 }
0013819:                                 else
0013820:                                 {
0013821:                                     c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, argument);
0013822:                                 }
0013823:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand windows_stack_store_operands[2] = {
0013824:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RSP, 64, 32 + (s64)stack_index * 8),
0013825:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0013826:                                 };
0013827:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), windows_stack_store_operands,
0013828:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(windows_stack_store_operands)))
0013829:                                 {
0013830:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013831:                                     return result;
0013832:                                 }
0013833:                                 stack_index += arguments[argument_index].part_count;
0013834:                             }
0013835:                         }
0013836:                         if (call_x64_indirect_return)
0013837:                         {
0013838:                             X64Register indirect_return_register = call_windows_indirect_return ? X64_REGISTER_RCX : X64_REGISTER_RDI;
0013839:                             if (call_layout.windows_result_copy_size)
0013840:                             {
0013841:                                 // An over-aligned result bounces through its
0013842:                                 // reserved outgoing-area slot: the callee may
0013843:                                 // store it back with alignment-checking moves
0013844:                                 // the sixteen-aligned frame slot cannot take.
0013845:                                 // The cast is not redundant: an unfixed enum is
0013846:                                 // unsigned under the Itanium ABI and signed
0013847:                                 // under the MS one, so a host-Windows build
0013848:                                 // sees a sign conversion here that a Linux one
0013849:                                 // does not.
0013850:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_rsp_address(&buffer, (u32)indirect_return_register, call_layout.windows_result_copy_offset,
0013851:                                                                         call_layout.windows_result_copy_alignment))
0013852:                                 {
0013853:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0013854:                                     return result;
0013855:                                 }
0013856:                             }
0013857:                             else
0013858:                             {
0013859:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand indirect_return_operands[2] = {
0013860:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(indirect_return_register, 64),
0013861:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, result_displacement),
0013862:                                 };
0013863:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), indirect_return_operands,
0013864:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(indirect_return_operands)))
0013865:                                 {
0013866:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013867:                                     return result;
0013868:                                 }
0013869:                             }
0013870:                         }
0013871:                         IrType* call_callee_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0013872:                         if (call_callee_type && call_callee_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER)
0013873:                         {
0013874:                             call_callee_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, call_callee_type->element_type);
0013875:                         }
0013876:                         bool windows_variadic_call = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS && call_callee_type &&
0013877:                                                      call_callee_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FUNCTION && call_callee_type->is_variadic;
0013878:                         u32 register_index = call_x64_indirect_return ? 1 : 0;
0013879:                         for (u32 argument_index = 1; argument_index < instruction->operand_count; argument_index += 1)
0013880:                         {
0013881:                             IrValueId argument = instruction->operands[argument_index];
0013882:                             CodegenCanonicalCallArgument* call_argument = arguments + argument_index - 1;
0013883:                             IrType* argument_type = call_argument->type;
0013884:                             CodegenCanonicalAbiValue argument_abi = call_argument->abi;
0013885:                             // A pieced vector argument stages exactly the
0013886:                             // pieces the layout put in registers; its tail is
0013887:                             // already in the stack slots. Every other on-stack
0013888:                             // argument has nothing to stage here.
0013889:                             u32 register_parts = call_argument->windows_piece_size ? call_argument->windows_register_piece_count
0013890:                                                  : call_argument->windows_indirect ? 1
0013891:                                                                                     : call_argument->part_count;
0013892:                             if (call_argument->on_stack && !(call_argument->windows_piece_size && call_argument->windows_register_piece_count))
0013893:                             {
0013894:                                 continue;
0013895:                             }
0013896:                             if (!argument_type || (!call_argument->system_v_aggregate &&
0013897:                                                    (register_index > register_count || register_parts > register_count - register_index)))
0013898:                             {
0013899:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0013900:                                 return result;
0013901:                             }
0013902:                             if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0013903:                             {
0013904:                                 u8 float_register = call_argument->float_register;
0013905:                                 if (float_register >= 8 || (argument_type->bit_width != 32 && argument_type->bit_width != 64))
0013906:                                 {
0013907:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0013908:                                     return result;
0013909:                                 }
0013910:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand float_load_operands[2] = {
0013911:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(float_register, (u16)argument_type->bit_width),
0013912:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0013913:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)argument_type->bit_width,
0013914:                                         c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[argument.value])),
0013915:                                 };
0013916:                                 String8 float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0013917:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0013918:                                         &buffer, argument_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD"), float_load_operands,
0013919:                                         BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_load_operands),
0013920:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = float_features,
0013921:                                                                          .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features)}))
0013922:                                 {
0013923:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0013924:                                     return result;
0013925:                                 }
0013926:                                 continue;
0013927:                             }
0013928:                             if (call_argument->system_v_aggregate)
0013929:                             {
0013930:                                 u32 float_register = call_argument->float_register;
0013931:                                 for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < argument_abi.part_count; part_index += 1)
0013932:                                 {
0013933:                                     CodegenCanonicalAbiPart* part = argument_abi.parts + part_index;
0013934:                                     s32 displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[argument.value]) + (s32)part->value_offset;
0013935:                                     if (codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(part->abi_class))
0013936:                                     {
0013937:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_float_memory(&buffer, target, float_register, displacement, part->size, false))
0013938:                                         {
0013939:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0013940:                                             return result;
0013941:                                         }
0013942:                                         float_register += 1;
0013943:                                     }
0013944:                                     else
0013945:                                     {
0013946:                                         if (register_index >= register_count)
0013947:                                         {
0013948:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0013949:                                             return result;
0013950:                                         }
0013951:                                         u8 reg = registers[register_index++];
0013952:                                         // Scalars classify through this branch
0013953:                                         // too; the de-facto System V contract
0013954:                                         // clang's callers implement widens a
0013955:                                         // sub-32-bit integer argument to 32
0013956:                                         // bits, and callees exist that read
0013957:                                         // the widened register directly.
0013958:                                         // Aggregate parts stay raw eightbytes.
0013959:                                         bool narrow_scalar = argument_abi.part_count == 1 && argument_type &&
0013960:                                                              (argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
0013961:                                                               (argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0013962:                                                                (argument_type->bit_width == 8 || argument_type->bit_width == 16)));
0013963:                                         if (narrow_scalar)
0013964:                                         {
0013965:                                             u16 source_width = argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER ? (u16)argument_type->bit_width : 8;
0013966:                                             bool sign_extend = argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && argument_type->is_signed;
0013967:                                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_load_operands[2] = {
0013968:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr((X64Register)reg, 32),
0013969:                                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, source_width, displacement),
0013970:                                             };
0013971:                                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, sign_extend ? S8("MOVSX") : S8("MOVZX"),
0013972:                                                                                        extend_load_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(extend_load_operands)))
0013973:                                             {
0013974:                                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0013975:                                                 return result;
0013976:                                             }
0013977:                                             continue;
0013978:                                         }
0013979:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand integer_load_operands[2] = {
0013980:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr((X64Register)reg, 64),
0013981:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, displacement),
0013982:                                         };
0013983:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), integer_load_operands,
0013984:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(integer_load_operands)))
0013985:                                         {
0013986:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0013987:                                             return result;
0013988:                                         }
0013989:                                     }
0013990:                                 }
0013991:                                 continue;
0013992:                             }
0013993:                             u16 windows_float_width = 0;
0013994:                             if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS &&
0013995:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_windows_float_argument(program, argument_type, &windows_float_width))
0013996:                             {
0013997:                                 if (register_index >= register_count)
0013998:                                 {
0013999:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014000:                                     return result;
0014001:                                 }
0014002:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand float_load_operands[2] = {
0014003:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(register_index, windows_float_width),
0014004:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014005:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, windows_float_width,
0014006:                                         c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[argument.value])),
0014007:                                 };
0014008:                                 String8 float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0014009:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0014010:                                         &buffer, windows_float_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD"), float_load_operands,
0014011:                                         BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_load_operands),
0014012:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = float_features,
0014013:                                                                          .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features)}))
0014014:                                 {
0014015:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014016:                                     return result;
0014017:                                 }
0014018:                                 if (windows_variadic_call)
0014019:                                 {
0014020:                                     u8 reg = registers[register_index];
0014021:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand integer_load_operands[2] = {
0014022:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr((X64Register)reg, 64),
0014023:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,
0014024:                                                                                c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[argument.value])),
0014025:                                     };
0014026:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), integer_load_operands,
0014027:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(integer_load_operands)))
0014028:                                     {
0014029:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014030:                                         return result;
0014031:                                     }
0014032:                                 }
0014033:                                 register_index += 1;
0014034:                                 continue;
0014035:                             }
0014036:                             if (call_argument->windows_indirect)
0014037:                             {
0014038:                                 // One reference per register piece; a
0014039:                                 // single-reference argument is the loop's
0014040:                                 // one-piece case. Each register holds the
0014041:                                 // rounded-up copy address advanced to its

0014043:                                 for (u32 piece = 0; piece < register_parts; piece += 1)
0014044:                                 {
0014045:                                     u8 reg = registers[register_index++];
0014046:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_rsp_address(&buffer, reg, call_argument->copy_offset, call_argument->copy_alignment))
0014047:                                     {
0014048:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0014049:                                         return result;
0014050:                                     }
0014051:                                     if (piece)
0014052:                                     {
0014053:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand piece_advance_operands[2] = {
0014054:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr((X64Register)reg, 64),
0014055:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)piece * call_argument->windows_piece_size, 32),
0014056:                                         };
0014057:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("ADD"), piece_advance_operands,
0014058:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(piece_advance_operands)))
0014059:                                         {
0014060:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0014061:                                             return result;
0014062:                                         }
0014063:                                     }
0014064:                                 }
0014065:                                 continue;
0014066:                             }
0014067:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < call_argument->part_count; part_index += 1)
0014068:                             {
0014069:                                 u8 reg = registers[register_index++];
0014070:                                 // The de-facto contract clang's callers
0014071:                                 // implement widens sub-32-bit integer
0014072:                                 // arguments to 32 bits; callees exist that
0014073:                                 // read the widened register directly.
0014074:                                 IrType* scalar_type = call_argument->type;
0014075:                                 bool narrow_scalar = !call_argument->aggregate && call_argument->part_count == 1 && scalar_type &&
0014076:                                                      (scalar_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
0014077:                                                       (scalar_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0014078:                                                        (scalar_type->bit_width == 8 || scalar_type->bit_width == 16)));
0014079:                                 if (narrow_scalar)
0014080:                                 {
0014081:                                     u16 source_width = scalar_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER ? (u16)scalar_type->bit_width : 8;
0014082:                                     bool sign_extend = scalar_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && scalar_type->is_signed;
0014083:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_load_operands[2] = {
0014084:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr((X64Register)reg, 32),
0014085:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014086:                                             X64_REGISTER_RBP, source_width, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[argument.value])),
0014087:                                     };

0014089:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(extend_load_operands)))

0014091:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014092:                                         return result;
0014093:                                     }
0014094:                                     continue;
0014095:                                 }
0014096:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand integer_load_operands[2] = {
0014097:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr((X64Register)reg, 64),
0014098:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014099:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64,

0014101:                                 };

0014103:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(integer_load_operands)))

0014105:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014106:                                     return result;
0014107:                                 }
0014108:                             }
0014109:                         }
0014110:                         IrType* callee_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0014111:                         bool indirect_call = callee_type && callee_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
0014112:                         if (indirect_call)
0014113:                         {
0014114:                             callee_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, callee_type->element_type);
0014115:                         }
0014116:                         if (result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V && callee_type && callee_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FUNCTION && callee_type->is_variadic)
0014117:                         {
0014118:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand variadic_register_count_operands[2] = {
0014119:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0014120:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(simulated_float_registers, 32),
