0014123:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(variadic_register_count_operands)))
0014124:                             {
0014125:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014126:                                 return result;
0014127:                             }
0014128:                         }
0014129:                         if (indirect_call)
0014130:                         {
0014131:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0014132:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand call_register = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
0014133:                             if (buffer.error || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CALL"), &call_register, 1))
0014134:                             {
0014135:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014136:                                 return result;
0014137:                             }
0014138:                         }
0014139:                         else
0014140:                         {
0014141:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand call_target = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0014142:                             u32 call_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0014143:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CALL"), &call_target, 1))
0014144:                             {
0014145:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014146:                                 return result;
0014147:                             }
0014148:                             // The call itself is the same rel32 either way.
0014149:                             // Under -fPIC an interposable callee asks for the
0014150:                             // PLT family instead, which is the linker's
0014151:                             // permission to point that rel32 at a procedure
0014152:                             // linkage entry -- without it `ld` refuses a
0014153:                             // direct call to a preemptible function in a
0014154:                             // shared object rather than routing it.
0014155:                             bool procedure_linkage =
0014156:                                 position_independent && ir_symbol_is_interposable(ir_symbol_from_id(&program->symbols, instruction->symbol));
0014157:                             result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0014158:                                 .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0014159:                                 .offset = call_offset + 1,
0014160:                                 .kind = (u8)(procedure_linkage ? CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_X86_64_PLT32 : CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_X86_64_PC32),
0014161:                             };
0014162:                         }
0014163:                         // Bring a bounced hidden-pointer result home before the
0014164:                         // outgoing area it lives in is released. An unused
0014165:                         // result stays in the slot; the bounce is what kept
0014166:                         // the callee's store off the frame in that case.
0014167:                         if (call_layout.windows_result_copy_size && instruction->result.value != IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID &&
0014168:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_copy_rsp_to_frame(&buffer, call_layout.windows_result_copy_offset,
0014169:                                                                      call_layout.windows_result_copy_alignment,
0014170:                                                                      value_offsets[instruction->result.value], canonical_x64_frame_base_offset,
0014171:                                                                      call_layout.windows_result_copy_size))
0014172:                         {
0014173:                             result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0014174:                             return result;
0014175:                         }
0014176:                         if (windows_dynamic_call)
0014177:                         {
0014178:                             codegen_canonical_x64_adjust_stack(&buffer, call_layout.windows_stack_size, false);
0014179:                             if (buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE)
0014180:                             {
0014181:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014182:                                 return result;
0014183:                             }
0014184:                         }
0014185:                         if (system_v_aligned_area)
0014186:                         {
0014187:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand stack_restore_operands[2] = {
0014188:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSP, 64),
0014189:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, x64_stack_save_offset)),
0014190:                             };
0014191:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), stack_restore_operands,
0014192:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(stack_restore_operands)))
0014193:                             {
0014194:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014195:                                 return result;
0014196:                             }
0014197:                         }
0014198:                         else if (result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS && (call_layout.stack_part_count || stack_padding))
0014199:                         {
0014200:                             u32 cleanup = call_layout.stack_part_count * 8 + (stack_padding ? 8 : 0);
0014201:                             codegen_canonical_x64_adjust_stack(&buffer, cleanup, false);
0014202:                         }
0014203:                         IrType* call_return_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014204:                         bool call_f80_complex_result = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V &&
0014205:                                                        codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_complex_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type);
0014206:                         if (codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type) &&
0014207:                             !(result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V &&
0014208:                               codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_opaque_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type)))
0014209:                         {
0014210:                             if (result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V ||
0014211:                                 (!call_f80_complex_result &&
0014212:                                  (!codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type) ||
0014213:                                   !codegen_canonical_x64_abi_is_f80_result(call_return_type, &call_return_abi))))
0014214:                             {
0014215:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014216:                                 return result;
0014217:                             }
0014218:                             // SysV returns f80 in ST0, and a COMPLEX_X87 pair
0014219:                             // in ST0 (real) over ST1 (imaginary).  Spill the
0014220:                             // stack immediately into the canonical result
0014221:                             // slot, or pop it when the call result is unused.
0014222:                             u32 call_f80_result_halves = call_f80_complex_result ? 2 : 1;
0014223:                             if (x87_stack_depth != 0)
0014224:                             {
0014225:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014226:                                 return result;
0014227:                             }
0014228:                             if (instruction->result.value != IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
0014229:                             {
0014230:                                 x87_stack_depth += call_f80_result_halves;
0014231:                                 for (u32 half = 0; half < call_f80_result_halves; half += 1)
0014232:                                 {
0014233:                                     // The pops run in stack order, so the
0014234:                                     // half in ST0 lands at the offset the
0014235:                                     // classification named for it.
0014236:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_store_top(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0014237:                                                                                    result_displacement + (s32)(half * 16),
0014238:                                                                                    &x87_stack_depth))
0014239:                                     {
0014240:                                         result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014241:                                         return result;
0014242:                                     }
0014243:                                 }
0014244:                             }
0014245:                             else
0014246:                             {
0014247:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand x87_st0_operand = {
0014248:                                     .kind = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_OPERAND_REGISTER,
0014249:                                     .width = 80,
0014250:                                     .reg = {
0014251:                                         .index = 0,
0014252:                                         .width = 80,
0014253:                                         .physical_class = BUSTER_X86_METADATA_PHYSICAL_CLASS_SPECIAL,
0014254:                                     },
0014255:                                 };
0014256:                                 for (u32 half = 0; half < call_f80_result_halves; half += 1)
0014257:                                 {
0014258:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("FSTP"), &x87_st0_operand, 1))
0014259:                                     {
0014260:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014261:                                         return result;
0014262:                                     }
0014263:                                 }
0014264:                             }
0014265:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014266:                             continue;
0014267:                         }
0014268:                         if (instruction->result.value != IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
0014269:                         {
0014270:                             IrType* return_type = call_return_type;
0014271:                             if (return_type && return_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0014272:                             {
0014273:                                 if (return_type->bit_width != 32 && return_type->bit_width != 64)
0014274:                                 {
0014275:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014276:                                     return result;
0014277:                                 }
0014278:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand return_float_store_operands[2] = {
0014279:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)return_type->bit_width,
0014280:                                                                           result_displacement),
0014281:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)return_type->bit_width),
0014282:                                 };
0014283:                                 String8 return_float_features[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0014284:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0014285:                                         &buffer, return_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD"), return_float_store_operands,
0014286:                                         BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(return_float_store_operands),
0014287:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = return_float_features,
0014288:                                                                          .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(return_float_features)}))
0014289:                                 {
0014290:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014291:                                     return result;
0014292:                                 }
0014293:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014294:                                 continue;
0014295:                             }
0014296:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_result_in_abi_parts(&call_return_abi, result.abi))
0014297:                             {
0014298:                                 u32 integer_index = 0;
0014299:                                 u32 float_index = 0;
0014300:                                 for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < call_return_abi.part_count; part_index += 1)
0014301:                                 {
0014302:                                     CodegenCanonicalAbiPart* part = call_return_abi.parts + part_index;
0014303:                                     if (codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(part->abi_class))
0014304:                                     {
0014305:                                         u32 register_size = 0;
0014306:                                         u32 register_count_used = codegen_canonical_x64_vector_part_registers(&target, part->size, &register_size);
0014307:                                         if (!register_count_used || float_index + register_count_used > BUSTER_MAX((u32)2, register_count_used))
0014308:                                         {
0014309:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014310:                                             return result;
0014311:                                         }
0014312:                                         for (u32 chunk = 0; chunk < register_count_used; chunk += 1)
0014313:                                         {
0014314:                                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_float_memory(&buffer, target, float_index,
0014315:                                                                                     result_displacement + (s32)part->value_offset + (s32)(chunk * register_size),
0014316:                                                                                     register_size, true))
0014317:                                             {
0014318:                                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014319:                                                 return result;
0014320:                                             }
0014321:                                             float_index += 1;
0014322:                                         }
0014323:                                     }
0014324:                                     else
0014325:                                     {
0014326:                                         if (integer_index >= 2)
0014327:                                         {
0014328:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014329:                                             return result;
0014330:                                         }

0014332:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014333:                                                 X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, result_displacement + (s32)part->value_offset),
0014334:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(integer_index ? X64_REGISTER_RDX : X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),

0014337:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(integer_return_store_operands)))
0014338:                                         {
0014339:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0014340:                                             return result;
0014341:                                         }

0014343:                                     }
0014344:                                 }
0014345:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014346:                                 continue;
0014347:                             }
0014348:                             u32 return_parts = 0;
0014349:                             bool aggregate_return = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &return_parts);
0014350:                             CodegenCanonicalAbiValue aggregate_return_abi = codegen_canonical_x64_windows_vector_result(
0014351:                                 program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, &target,
0014352:                                 codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, true, false));
0014353:                             if (aggregate_return_abi.part_count && !aggregate_return_abi.indirect)
0014354:                             {
0014355:                                 aggregate_return = true;
0014356:                                 return_parts = aggregate_return_abi.part_count;
0014357:                             }
0014358:                             if (aggregate_return_abi.indirect)
0014359:                             {
0014360:                                 if (!call_x64_indirect_return)
0014361:                                 {
0014362:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0014363:                                     return result;
0014364:                                 }
0014365:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014366:                                 continue;
0014367:                             }
0014368:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0014369:                             if (aggregate_return && return_parts == 2)
0014370:                             {
0014371:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand aggregate_return_store_operands[2] = {
0014372:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, result_displacement + 8),
0014373:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0014374:                                 };
0014375:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), aggregate_return_store_operands,
0014376:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(aggregate_return_store_operands)))
0014377:                                 {
0014378:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014379:                                     return result;
0014380:                                 }
0014381:                             }
0014382:                         }
0014383:                     }
0014384:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ARRAY)
0014385:                     {
0014386:                         IrType* array = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014387:                         IrType* element = array ? ir_type_from_id(&program->types, array->element_type) : 0;
0014388:                         if (!array || !element || (array->kind != IR_TYPE_ARRAY && array->kind != IR_TYPE_VECTOR) ||
0014389:                             instruction->operand_count != array->element_count)
0014390:                         {
0014391:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014392:                             return result;
0014393:                         }
0014394:                         for (u32 element_index = 0; element_index < instruction->operand_count; element_index += 1)
0014395:                         {
0014396:                             u64 copied = 0;
0014397:                             while (copied < element->layout.size)
0014398:                             {
0014399:                                 u64 remaining = element->layout.size - copied;
0014400:                                 u32 chunk = remaining >= 8 ? 8 : remaining >= 4 ? 4 : remaining >= 2 ? 2 : 1;
0014401:                                 u16 memory_width = (u16)(chunk * 8);
0014402:                                 u16 register_width = chunk <= 2 ? 32 : memory_width;
0014403:                                 String8 load_mnemonic = chunk <= 2 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV");

0014405:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, register_width),
0014406:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014407:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, memory_width,
0014408:                                         c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[element_index].value]) + (s32)copied),
0014409:                                 };
0014410:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
0014411:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014412:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, memory_width,
0014413:                                         result_displacement + (s32)(element_index * element->layout.size + copied)),
0014414:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, memory_width),
0014415:                                 };
0014416:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, load_mnemonic, load_operands,
0014417:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands)) ||

0014419:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands)))
0014420:                                 {
0014421:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014422:                                     return result;
0014423:                                 }
0014424:                                 copied += chunk;
0014425:                             }
0014426:                         }
0014427:                     }
0014428:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_AGGREGATE)
0014429:                     {
0014430:                         IrType* aggregate = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014431:                         if (!aggregate || instruction->operand_count != instruction->immediate_count)
0014432:                         {
0014433:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014434:                             return result;
0014435:                         }
0014436:                         u64 aggregate_copied = 0;
0014437:                         while (aggregate_copied < aggregate->layout.size)
0014438:                         {
0014439:                             u64 remaining = aggregate->layout.size - aggregate_copied;
0014440:                             u32 chunk = remaining >= 8 ? 8 : remaining >= 4 ? 4 : remaining >= 2 ? 2 : 1;
0014441:                             u16 memory_width = (u16)(chunk * 8);

0014450:                             };

0014452:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(zero_operands)) ||

0014454:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(zero_store_operands)))
0014455:                             {
0014456:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014457:                                 return result;
0014458:                             }
0014459:                             aggregate_copied += chunk;
0014460:                         }
0014461:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0014462:                         {
0014463:                             u64 field_index = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0014464:                             if (field_index >= aggregate->field_count)
0014465:                             {
0014466:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014467:                                 return result;
0014468:                             }
0014469:                             IrField* field = aggregate->fields + field_index;
0014470:                             IrType* field_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, field->type);
0014471:                             if (!field_type || !field_type->layout.resolved)
0014472:                             {
0014473:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014474:                                 return result;
0014475:                             }
0014476:                             s32 field_displacement = result_displacement + (s32)field->offset;
0014477:                             if (field->is_bit_field)
0014478:                             {
0014479:                                 if (!field->bit_width)
0014480:                                 {
0014481:                                     continue;
0014482:                                 }
0014483:                                 // The storage the field is written through,
0014484:                                 // which packing may have narrowed below the
0014485:                                 // declared type or left as a span no single
0014486:                                 // access covers; `field->offset` names its
0014487:                                 // first byte and the pieces walk it. One piece
0014488:                                 // is the ordinary case, and the rows it emits
0014489:                                 // are the rows a single unit always emitted.
0014490:                                 u64 field_unit = ir_field_access_size(&program->types, field);
0014491:                                 IrFieldAccessPiece field_pieces[IR_FIELD_ACCESS_PIECE_CAPACITY];
0014492:                                 u32 field_piece_count = ir_field_access_pieces(field_unit, field_pieces);
0014493:                                 if (!field_piece_count || field->bit_width > 64)
0014494:                                 {
0014495:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014496:                                     return result;
0014497:                                 }
0014498:                                 for (u32 piece_index = 0; piece_index < field_piece_count; piece_index += 1)
0014499:                                 {
0014500:                                     u32 piece_low = (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].offset * 8;
0014501:                                     u32 piece_high = piece_low + (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].size * 8;
0014502:                                     u32 low = BUSTER_MAX(piece_low, field->bit_offset);
0014503:                                     u32 high = BUSTER_MIN(piece_high, field->bit_offset + field->bit_width);
0014504:                                     if (low >= high)
0014505:                                     {
0014506:                                         continue;
0014507:                                     }
0014508:                                     c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[operand_index]);
0014509:                                     if (low > field->bit_offset &&
0014510:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_shift_immediate(&buffer, S8("SHR"), low - field->bit_offset))
0014511:                                     {
0014512:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014513:                                         return result;
0014514:                                     }
0014515:                                     if (high - low < 64)
0014516:                                     {
0014517:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand mask_load_operands[2] = {
0014518:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0014519:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(((u64)1 << (high - low)) - 1, 64),
0014520:                                         };
0014521:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand mask_operands[2] = {
0014522:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0014523:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0014524:                                         };
0014525:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), mask_load_operands,
0014526:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(mask_load_operands)) ||
0014527:                                             !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("AND"), mask_operands,
0014528:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(mask_operands)))
0014529:                                         {
0014530:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0014531:                                             return result;
0014532:                                         }
0014533:                                     }
0014534:                                     if (low > piece_low && !codegen_canonical_x64_shift_immediate(&buffer, S8("SHL"), low - piece_low))
0014535:                                     {
0014536:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014537:                                         return result;
0014538:                                     }
0014539:                                     u16 piece_width = (u16)((u32)field_pieces[piece_index].size * 8);
0014540:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand bitfield_store_operands[2] = {
0014541:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, piece_width,
0014542:                                                                               field_displacement + (s32)field_pieces[piece_index].offset),
0014543:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, piece_width),
0014544:                                     };
0014545:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("OR"), bitfield_store_operands,
0014546:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(bitfield_store_operands)))
0014547:                                     {
0014548:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014549:                                         return result;
0014550:                                     }
0014551:                                 }
0014552:                                 continue;
0014553:                             }
0014554:                             u64 field_copied = 0;
0014555:                             while (field_copied < field_type->layout.size)
0014556:                             {
0014557:                                 u64 remaining = field_type->layout.size - field_copied;
0014558:                                 u32 chunk = remaining >= 8 ? 8 : remaining >= 4 ? 4 : remaining >= 2 ? 2 : 1;
0014559:                                 u16 memory_width = (u16)(chunk * 8);
0014560:                                 u16 register_width = chunk <= 2 ? 32 : memory_width;
0014561:                                 String8 load_mnemonic = chunk <= 2 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV");
0014562:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
0014563:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, register_width),
0014564:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0014565:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, memory_width,
0014566:                                         c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[operand_index].value]) + (s32)field_copied),
0014567:                                 };
0014568:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
0014569:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, memory_width,
0014570:                                                                           field_displacement + (s32)field_copied),
0014571:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, memory_width),
0014572:                                 };
0014573:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, load_mnemonic, load_operands,
0014574:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands)) ||
0014575:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), store_operands,
0014576:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands)))
0014577:                                 {
0014578:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014579:                                     return result;
0014580:                                 }
0014581:                                 field_copied += chunk;
0014582:                             }
0014583:                         }
0014584:                     }
0014585:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_UNARY)
0014586:                     {
0014587:                         IrType* canonical_unary_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014588:                         // Negation is the one unary row the x87 vocabulary
0014589:                         // answers: FCHS toggles the sign of the loaded value.
0014590:                         bool wide_float_negate = canonical_function_has_f80 && instruction->operand_count == 1 &&
0014591:                                                  instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_FLOAT_NEGATE &&
0014592:                                                  codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(canonical_unary_type) &&
0014593:                                                  codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(

0014596:                         {

0014602:                             }

0014604:                             continue;
0014605:                         }

0014607:                             (instruction->operand_count &&
0014608:                              codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type)))
0014609:                         {
0014610:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014611:                             return result;
0014612:                         }
0014613:                         if (canonical_unary_type && canonical_unary_type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)
0014614:                         {
0014615:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_vector_operation(
0014616:                                     &buffer, program, function, instruction, value_offsets, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset, target,

0014622:                                 return result;
0014623:                             }
0014624:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014625:                             continue;
0014626:                         }
0014627:                         IrType* canonical_unary_operand_type =
0014628:                             ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0014629:                         if (canonical_unary_operand_type && canonical_unary_operand_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0014630:                             canonical_unary_operand_type->bit_width == 128)
0014631:                         {
0014632:                             if (instruction->unary_operation != IR_UNARY_INTEGER_NEGATE &&
0014633:                                 instruction->unary_operation != IR_UNARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_NOT)
0014634:                             {
0014635:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014636:                                 return result;
0014637:                             }
0014638:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0014639:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
0014640:                             if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_NEGATE)
0014641:                             {

0014643:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
0014644:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand carry_operands[2] = {

0014646:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate(0, 8),

0014649:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64);

0014655:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014656:                                     return result;
0014657:                                 }
0014658:                             }
0014659:                             else
0014660:                             {

0014662:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
0014663:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand not_high_operand =
0014664:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64);

0014666:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("NOT"), &not_high_operand, 1))
0014667:                                 {
0014668:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014669:                                     return result;
0014670:                                 }
0014671:                             }
0014672:                             c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0014673:                             c_x64_store_high_rdx(&emitter, result_displacement);
0014674:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014675:                             continue;
0014676:                         }
0014677:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0014678:                         if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_BOOLEAN_NOT)
0014679:                         {
0014680:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand test_operands[2] = {
0014681:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0014682:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0014683:                             };

0014685:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8);
0014686:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand extend_operands[2] = {
0014687:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0014688:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0014689:                             };
0014690:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("TEST"), test_operands,
0014691:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(test_operands)) ||
0014692:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("SETZ"), &set_operand, 1) ||
0014693:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), extend_operands,
0014694:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(extend_operands)))
0014695:                             {
0014696:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014697:                                 return result;
0014698:                             }
0014699:                         }
0014700:                         else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_FLOAT_NEGATE)
0014701:                         {
0014702:                             IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014703:                             if (!type || type->kind != IR_TYPE_FLOAT || (type->bit_width != 32 && type->bit_width != 64))
0014704:                             {
0014705:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014706:                                 return result;
0014707:                             }
0014708:                             u64 sign = type->bit_width == 32 ? (u64)1 << 31 : (u64)1 << 63;
0014709:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand sign_load_operands[2] = {
0014710:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0014711:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(sign, 64),
0014712:                             };
0014713:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand xor_operands[2] = {
0014714:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0014715:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0014716:                             };
0014717:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), sign_load_operands,
0014718:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(sign_load_operands)) ||
0014719:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), xor_operands,
0014720:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(xor_operands)))
0014721:                             {
0014722:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014723:                                 return result;
0014724:                             }
0014725:                         }
0014726:                         else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_NEGATE || instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_NOT)
0014727:                         {
0014728:                             IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014729:                             if (!type || type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
0014730:                             {
0014731:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014732:                                 return result;
0014733:                             }
0014734:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand unary_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(
0014735:                                 X64_REGISTER_RAX, type->bit_width > 32 ? 64 : 32);
0014736:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(
0014737:                                     &buffer, instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_NEGATE ? S8("NEG") : S8("NOT"),
0014738:                                     &unary_operand, 1))
0014739:                             {
0014740:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014741:                                 return result;
0014742:                             }
0014743:                         }
0014744:                         else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_LEADING_ZEROS ||
0014745:                                  instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_TRAILING_ZEROS)
0014746:                         {
0014747:                             IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014748:                             if (!type || type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
0014749:                             {
0014750:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014751:                                 return result;
0014752:                             }
0014753:                             u16 operation_width = type->bit_width > 32 ? 64 : 32;
0014754:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand count_operands[2] = {
0014755:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, operation_width),
0014756:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, operation_width),
0014757:                             };
0014758:                             String8 count_mnemonic = instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_TRAILING_ZEROS
0014759:                                                          ? S8("BSF")
0014760:                                                          : S8("BSR");
0014761:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, count_mnemonic, count_operands,
0014762:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(count_operands)))
0014763:                             {
0014764:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0014765:                                 return result;
0014766:                             }
0014767:                             if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_LEADING_ZEROS)
0014768:                             {
0014769:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand invert_operands[2] = {
0014770:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, operation_width),
0014771:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_immediate((s64)type->bit_width - 1, 8),
0014772:                                 };
0014773:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), invert_operands,
0014774:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(invert_operands)))
0014775:                                 {
0014776:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014777:                                     return result;
0014778:                                 }
0014779:                             }
0014780:                         }
0014781:                         else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_POPULATION_COUNT)
0014782:                         {
0014783:                             IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0014784:                             if (!type || type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
0014785:                             {
0014786:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0014787:                                 return result;
0014788:                             }
0014789:                             x64_emit_population_count(&buffer, type->bit_width);
0014790:                         }
0014791:                         else
0014792:                         {
0014793:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014794:                             return result;
0014795:                         }
0014796:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0014797:                     }
0014798:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_BINARY)
0014799:                     {
0014800:                         IrTypeId operand_type = function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type;
0014801:                         IrType* operand_type_value = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, operand_type);
0014802:                         IrTypeId right_operand_type = function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type;
0014803:                         // Scalar f80 arithmetic and comparison run on the x87
0014804:                         // stack below.  Every other f80 shape reaching a
0014805:                         // binary row -- an aggregate wrapper, a mixed pair --
0014806:                         // still has no lowering.
0014807:                         bool wide_float_binary = canonical_function_has_f80 && codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(operand_type_value) &&
0014808:                                                  codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(ir_type_from_id(&program->types, right_operand_type));
0014809:                         if (!wide_float_binary &&
0014810:                             (codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, operand_type) ||
0014811:                              codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, right_operand_type) ||
0014812:                              codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, instruction->canonical_type)))
0014813:                         {
0014814:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014815:                             return result;
0014816:                         }
0014817:                         if (operand_type_value && operand_type_value->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)
0014818:                         {
0014819:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_vector_operation(
0014820:                                     &buffer, program, function, instruction, value_offsets, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset, target,
0014821:                                     &result.statistics.native_vector_operation_count, &result.statistics.split_vector_operation_count,
0014822:                                     &x64_upper_vector_dirty, &x64_last_wide_vector_result, &x64_last_wide_vector_size,
0014823:                                     &result.statistics.forwarded_wide_vector_load_count))
0014824:                             {
0014825:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014826:                                 return result;
0014827:                             }
0014828:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014829:                             continue;
0014830:                         }
0014831:                         if (operand_type_value && operand_type_value->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0014832:                         {
0014833:                             u32 width = operand_type_value->bit_width;
0014834:                             if (width != 32 && width != 64 && !wide_float_binary)
0014835:                             {
0014836:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014837:                                 return result;
0014838:                             }
0014839:                             IrBinaryOperation operation = instruction->binary_operation;
0014840:                             bool float_arithmetic = operation >= IR_BINARY_FLOAT_ADD && operation <= IR_BINARY_FLOAT_DIVIDE;
0014841:                             if (wide_float_binary)
0014842:                             {
0014843:                                 // The x87 sequence addresses its operands in
0014844:                                 // the frame, so nothing is loaded into a
0014845:                                 // register ahead of it.
0014846:                                 s32 left_displacement = c_x64_value_displacement(&emitter, instruction->operands[0]);
0014847:                                 s32 right_displacement = c_x64_value_displacement(&emitter, instruction->operands[1]);
0014848:                                 bool wide_emitted =
0014849:                                     float_arithmetic ? codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_binary(&buffer, operation, left_displacement,
0014850:                                                                                              right_displacement, result_displacement, &x87_stack_depth)
0014851:                                                      : codegen_canonical_x64_emit_f80_compare(&buffer, left_displacement, right_displacement,
0014852:                                                                                               &x87_stack_depth);
0014853:                                 if (!wide_emitted)
0014854:                                 {
0014855:                                     result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014856:                                     return result;
0014857:                                 }
0014858:                                 if (float_arithmetic)
0014859:                                 {
0014860:                                     // The x87 store already wrote the result
0014861:                                     // slot; a comparison continues into the
0014862:                                     // shared SETcc tail below.
0014863:                                     instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014864:                                     continue;
0014865:                                 }
0014866:                             }
0014867:                             else
0014868:                             {
0014869:                                 c_x64_load_float(&emitter, 0, instruction->operands[0], width);
0014870:                                 c_x64_load_float(&emitter, 1, instruction->operands[1], width);
0014871:                             }
0014872:                             String8 feature_names[] = {S8("sse"), S8("sse2")};
0014873:                             if (float_arithmetic)
0014874:                             {
0014875:                                 String8 mnemonic = operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_ADD        ? (width == 32 ? S8("ADDSS") : S8("ADDSD"))
0014876:                                                    : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_SUBTRACT   ? (width == 32 ? S8("SUBSS") : S8("SUBSD"))
0014877:                                                    : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_MULTIPLY   ? (width == 32 ? S8("MULSS") : S8("MULSD"))
0014878:                                                                                              : (width == 32 ? S8("DIVSS") : S8("DIVSD"));
0014879:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand arithmetic_operands[2] = {
0014880:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)width),
0014881:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(1, (u16)width),
0014882:                                 };
0014883:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0014884:                                         &buffer, mnemonic, arithmetic_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(arithmetic_operands),
0014885:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = feature_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(feature_names)}))
0014886:                                 {
0014887:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014888:                                     return result;
0014889:                                 }
0014890:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_operands[2] = {
0014891:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, (u16)width, result_displacement),
0014892:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)width),
0014893:                                 };
0014894:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0014895:                                         &buffer, width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVSD"), store_operands,
0014896:                                         BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_operands),
0014897:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = feature_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(feature_names)}))
0014898:                                 {
0014899:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014900:                                     return result;
0014901:                                 }
0014902:                             }
0014903:                             else
0014904:                             {
0014905:                                 // FUCOMIP already left the flags in the same
0014906:                                 // ZF/PF/CF encoding for the wide case.
0014907:                                 String8 compare_mnemonic = width == 32 ? S8("UCOMISS") : S8("UCOMISD");
0014908:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand compare_operands[2] = {
0014909:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, (u16)width),
0014910:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(1, (u16)width),
0014911:                                 };
0014912:                                 if (!wide_float_binary &&
0014913:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0014914:                                         &buffer, compare_mnemonic, compare_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(compare_operands),
0014915:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = feature_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(feature_names)}))
0014916:                                 {
0014917:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014918:                                     return result;
0014919:                                 }
0014920:                                 String8 condition_mnemonic = operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_EQUAL           ? S8("SETZ")
0014921:                                                              : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_NOT_EQUAL     ? S8("SETNZ")
0014922:                                                              : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_LESS          ? S8("SETB")
0014923:                                                              : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_LESS_EQUAL    ? S8("SETBE")
0014924:                                                              : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_GREATER       ? S8("SETNBE")
0014925:                                                              : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_GREATER_EQUAL ? S8("SETNB")
0014926:                                                                                                             : (String8){0};
0014927:                                 if (!condition_mnemonic.length)
0014928:                                 {
0014929:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0014930:                                     return result;
0014931:                                 }
0014932:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand set_operands[] = {
0014933:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0014934:                                 };
0014935:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, condition_mnemonic, set_operands,
0014936:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(set_operands)))
0014937:                                 {
0014938:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014939:                                     return result;
0014940:                                 }
0014941:                                 if (operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_EQUAL || operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_LESS || operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_LESS_EQUAL)
0014942:                                 {
0014943:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand ordered_operands[] = {
0014944:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 8),
0014945:                                     };
0014946:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("SETNP"), ordered_operands,
0014947:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(ordered_operands)))
0014948:                                     {
0014949:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014950:                                         return result;
0014951:                                     }
0014952:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand and_operands[2] = {
0014953:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0014954:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 8),
0014955:                                     };
0014956:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("AND"), and_operands,
0014957:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(and_operands)))
0014958:                                     {
0014959:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014960:                                         return result;
0014961:                                     }
0014962:                                 }
0014963:                                 else if (operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_NOT_EQUAL)
0014964:                                 {
0014965:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand unordered_operands[] = {
0014966:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 8),
0014967:                                     };
0014968:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("SETP"), unordered_operands,
0014969:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(unordered_operands)))
0014970:                                     {
0014971:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014972:                                         return result;
0014973:                                     }
0014974:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand or_operands[2] = {
0014975:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0014976:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 8),
0014977:                                     };
0014978:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("OR"), or_operands,
0014979:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(or_operands)))
0014980:                                     {
0014981:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0014982:                                         return result;
0014983:                                     }
0014984:                                 }
0014985:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand widen_operands[2] = {
0014986:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0014987:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0014988:                                 };
0014989:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), widen_operands,
0014990:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(widen_operands)))
0014991:                                 {
0014992:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0014993:                                     return result;
0014994:                                 }
0014995:                                 c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0014996:                             }
0014997:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0014998:                             continue;
0014999:                         }
0015000:                         bool integer128 = operand_type_value && operand_type_value->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && operand_type_value->bit_width == 128;
0015001:                         if (integer128)
0015002:                         {
0015003:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0015004:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0x95, instruction->operands[0]);
0015005:                             c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x8d, instruction->operands[1]);
0015006:                             c_x64_load_high(&emitter, 0xb5, instruction->operands[1]);
0015007:                             IrBinaryOperation operation = instruction->binary_operation;
0015008:                             if (operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_ADD || operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_SUBTRACT)
0015009:                             {
0015010:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand low_operands[2] = {
0015011:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0015012:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0015013:                                 };
0015014:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_operands[2] = {
0015015:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0015016:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSI, 64),
0015017:                                 };
0015018:                                 String8 mnemonic = operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_ADD ? S8("ADD") : S8("SUB");
0015019:                                 String8 carry_mnemonic = operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_ADD ? S8("ADC") : S8("SBB");
0015020:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, mnemonic, low_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(low_operands)) ||
0015021:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, carry_mnemonic, high_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_operands)))
0015022:                                 {
0015023:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0015024:                                     return result;
0015025:                                 }
0015026:                             }
0015027:                             else if (operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_AND || operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_OR ||
0015028:                                      operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_XOR)
0015029:                             {
0015030:                                 String8 mnemonic = operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_AND ? S8("AND")
0015031:                                                    : operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_OR ? S8("OR")
0015032:                                                                                               : S8("XOR");
0015033:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand low_operands[2] = {
0015034:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0015035:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0015036:                                 };
0015037:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_operands[2] = {
0015038:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0015039:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSI, 64),
0015040:                                 };
0015041:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, mnemonic, low_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(low_operands)) ||
0015042:                                     !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, mnemonic, high_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_operands)))
0015043:                                 {
0015044:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0015045:                                     return result;
0015046:                                 }
0015047:                             }
0015048:                             else if (operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_MULTIPLY)
0015049:                             {
0015050:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand move_r11_operands[2] = {
0015051:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R11, 64),
0015052:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0015053:                                 };
0015054:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand move_r9_operands[2] = {
0015055:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0015056:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0015057:                                 };
0015058:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand multiply_low_operands[] = {
0015059:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0015060:                                 };
0015061:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cross_low_high_operands[2] = {
0015062:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0015063:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0015064:                                 };
0015065:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand add_high_low_operands[2] = {
0015066:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0015067:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R9, 64),
0015068:                                 };
0015069:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand cross_high_low_operands[2] = {
0015070:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_R11, 64),
0015071:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RSI, 64),
0015072:                                 };
0015073:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), move_r11_operands,

0015812:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(scalar_operands)))

0015814:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0015815:                                 return result;
0015816:                             }
0015817:                             break;
0015818:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_OR:
0015819:                         case IR_BINARY_BOOLEAN_OR:
0015820:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("OR"), scalar_operands,
0015821:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(scalar_operands)))
0015822:                             {
0015823:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0015824:                                 return result;
0015825:                             }
0015826:                             break;
0015827:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_XOR:
0015828:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("XOR"), scalar_operands,
0015829:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(scalar_operands)))
0015830:                             {
0015831:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0015832:                                 return result;
0015833:                             }
0015834:                             break;
0015835:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_EQUAL:
0015836:                         case IR_BINARY_POINTER_EQUAL:
0015837:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_NOT_EQUAL:
0015838:                         case IR_BINARY_POINTER_NOT_EQUAL:
0015839:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS:
0015840:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0015841:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER:
0015842:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0015843:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS:
0015844:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0015845:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER:
0015846:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0015847:                         {
0015848:                             String8 condition_mnemonic = instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_EQUAL ||
0015849:                                                                   instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_POINTER_EQUAL ? S8("SETZ")
0015850:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_NOT_EQUAL ||
0015851:                                                 instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_POINTER_NOT_EQUAL ? S8("SETNZ")
0015852:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS ? S8("SETL")
0015853:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS_EQUAL ? S8("SETLE")
0015854:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER ? S8("SETNLE")
0015855:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL ? S8("SETNL")
0015856:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS ? S8("SETB")
0015857:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS_EQUAL ? S8("SETBE")
0015858:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER ? S8("SETNBE")
0015859:                                       : instruction->binary_operation == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL ? S8("SETNB")
0015860:                                                                                                            : (String8){0};
0015861:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand set_operands[] = {
0015862:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0015863:                             };
0015864:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand widen_operands[2] = {
0015865:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 32),
0015866:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 8),
0015867:                             };
0015868:                             if (!condition_mnemonic.length ||
0015869:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CMP"), scalar_operands,
0015870:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(scalar_operands)) ||
0015871:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, condition_mnemonic, set_operands,
0015872:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(set_operands)) ||
0015873:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOVZX"), widen_operands,
0015874:                                                                       BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(widen_operands)))
0015875:                             {
0015876:                                 result.error = buffer.error ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0015877:                                 return result;
0015878:                             }
0015879:                             break;
0015880:                         }
0015881:                         default:
0015882:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0015883:                             return result;
0015884:                         }
0015885:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0015886:                     }
0015887:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LABEL_ADDRESS)
0015888:                     {
0015889:                         if (instruction->target_count != 1)
0015890:                         {
0015891:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0015892:                             return result;
0015893:                         }
0015894:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand label_operands[2] = {
0015895:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
