0015896:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_rip_relative(64, 0),
0015897:                         };
0015898:                         u32 label_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0015899:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("LEA"), label_operands, BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(label_operands)))
0015900:                         {
0015901:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0015902:                             return result;
0015903:                         }
0015904:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0015905:                             .target = instruction->targets[0],
0015906:                             .offset = label_offset + 3,
0015907:                             .label_address = true,
0015908:                             }))
0015909:                         {
0015910:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0015911:                             return result;
0015912:                         }
0015913:                         c_x64_store_result(&emitter, result_displacement);
0015914:                     }
0015915:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_BRANCH)
0015916:                     {
0015917:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0015918:                         u32 branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0015919:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &branch_operand, 1))
0015920:                         {
0015921:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0015922:                             return result;
0015923:                         }
0015924:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0015925:                             .target = instruction->targets[0],
0015926:                             .offset = branch_offset + 1,
0015927:                             }))
0015928:                         {
0015929:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0015930:                             return result;
0015931:                         }
0015932:                     }
0015933:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_BRANCH_IF)
0015934:                     {
0015935:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0015936:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand test_operands[2] = {
0015937:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0015938:                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0015939:                         };
0015940:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0015941:                         if (buffer.error || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("TEST"), test_operands,
0015942:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(test_operands)))
0015943:                         {
0015944:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0015945:                             return result;
0015946:                         }
0015947:                         u32 conditional_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0015948:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JNZ"), &branch_operand, 1))
0015949:                         {
0015950:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0015951:                             return result;
0015952:                         }
0015953:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0015954:                             .target = instruction->targets[0],
0015955:                             .offset = conditional_branch_offset + 2,
0015956:                             }))
0015957:                         {
0015958:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0015959:                             return result;
0015960:                         }
0015961:                         u32 fallthrough_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0015962:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &branch_operand, 1))
0015963:                         {
0015964:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0015965:                             return result;
0015966:                         }
0015967:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0015968:                             .target = instruction->targets[1],
0015969:                             .offset = fallthrough_branch_offset + 1,
0015970:                             }))
0015971:                         {
0015972:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0015973:                             return result;
0015974:                         }
0015975:                     }
0015976:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDIRECT_BRANCH)
0015977:                     {
0015978:                         if (instruction->operand_count != 1)
0015979:                         {
0015980:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0015981:                             return result;
0015982:                         }
0015983:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0015984:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64);
0015985:                         if (buffer.error || !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &branch_operand, 1))
0015986:                         {
0015987:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0015988:                             return result;
0015989:                         }
0015990:                     }
0015991:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_SWITCH)
0015992:                     {
0015993:                         if (instruction->target_count != instruction->immediate_count + 1)
0015994:                         {
0015995:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0015996:                             return result;
0015997:                         }
0015998:                         u16 compare_width = 64;
0015999:                         if (instruction->operand_count && instruction->operands[0].value < function->value_count)
0016000:                         {
0016001:                             IrType* switch_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0016002:                             if (switch_type && (switch_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
0016003:                                                 (switch_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && switch_type->bit_width <= 32)))
0016004:                             {
0016005:                                 compare_width = 32;
0016006:                             }
0016007:                         }
0016008:                         c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, instruction->operands[0]);
0016009:                         for (u32 case_index = 0; case_index < instruction->immediate_count; case_index += 1)
0016010:                         {
0016011:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand move_case_operands[2] = {
0016012:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, compare_width),
0016013:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_unsigned_immediate(instruction->immediates[case_index], compare_width),
0016014:                             };
0016015:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand compare_case_operands[2] = {
0016016:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, compare_width),
0016017:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, compare_width),
0016018:                             };
0016019:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand case_branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0016020:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), move_case_operands,
0016021:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(move_case_operands)) ||
0016022:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("CMP"), compare_case_operands,
0016023:                                                                        BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(compare_case_operands)))
0016024:                             {
0016025:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0016026:                                 return result;
0016027:                             }
0016028:                             u32 case_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0016029:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JZ"), &case_branch_operand, 1))
0016030:                             {
0016031:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0016032:                                 return result;
0016033:                             }
0016034:                             if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0016035:                                 .target = instruction->targets[case_index],
0016036:                                 .offset = case_branch_offset + 2,
0016037:                                 .conditional = true,
0016038:                                 }))
0016039:                             {
0016040:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0016041:                                 return result;
0016042:                             }
0016043:                         }
0016044:                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand default_branch_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0016045:                         u32 default_branch_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0016046:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &default_branch_operand, 1))
0016047:                         {
0016048:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0016049:                             return result;
0016050:                         }
0016051:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0016052:                             .target = instruction->targets[instruction->target_count - 1],
0016053:                             .offset = default_branch_offset + 1,
0016054:                             }))
0016055:                         {
0016056:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0016057:                             return result;
0016058:                         }
0016059:                     }
0016060:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_SIMD)
0016061:                     {
0016062:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_simd_operation(&buffer, instruction, value_offsets, canonical_x64_frame_base_offset, target))
0016063:                         {
0016064:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016065:                             return result;
0016066:                         }
0016067:                         x64_upper_vector_dirty = true;
0016068:                         x64_last_wide_vector_result = IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
0016069:                         x64_last_wide_vector_size = 0;
0016070:                         result.statistics.simd_operation_count += 1;
0016071:                     }
0016072:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INLINE_ASSEMBLY)
0016073:                     {
0016074:                         IrInstructionExtra asm_extra = ir_instruction_extra(function, ir_instruction_self_id(function, instruction));
0016075:                         bool cpuid = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("cpuid"));
0016076:                         bool xgetbv = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("xgetbv"));
0016077:                         bool undefined = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("ud2"));
0016078:                         bool no_instruction = !asm_extra.literal.length;
0016079:                         bool nop = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("nop"));
0016080:                         bool pause = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("pause"));
0016081:                         bool interrupt = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("int3"));
0016082:                         u32 jump_target_index = 0;
0016083:                         bool jump_label = codegen_inline_assembly_jump_target(function, instruction, asm_extra.literal, S8("jmp %l"), &jump_target_index);
0016084:                         bool operandless = undefined || nop || pause || interrupt;
0016085:                         bool ordinary = !cpuid && !xgetbv && !operandless && !no_instruction && !jump_label;
0016086:                         if ((operandless && instruction->operand_count) || (jump_label && instruction->target_count < 2) ||
0016087:                             instruction->operand_count != instruction->immediate_count || asm_extra.operand_name_count != instruction->operand_count ||
0016088:                             (instruction->operand_count && (!instruction->operands || !instruction->immediates || !asm_extra.operand_names)) ||
0016089:                             (asm_extra.clobber_count && !asm_extra.clobbers))
0016090:                         {
0016091:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016092:                             return result;
0016093:                         }
0016094:                         for (u32 clobber_index = 0; clobber_index < asm_extra.clobber_count; clobber_index += 1)
0016095:                         {
0016096:                             String8 clobber = asm_extra.clobbers[clobber_index];
0016097:                             u32 vector_clobber = 0;
0016098:                             bool accepted = string_equal(clobber, S8("memory")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("cc")) ||
0016099:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("st")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("rax")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("eax")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("ax")) ||
0016100:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("al")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("rbx")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("ebx")) ||
0016101:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("bx")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("bl")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("rcx")) ||
0016102:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("ecx")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("cx")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("cl")) ||
0016103:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("rdx")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("edx")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("dx")) ||
0016104:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("dl")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("rsi")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("esi")) ||
0016105:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("si")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("sil")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("rdi")) ||
0016106:                                             string_equal(clobber, S8("edi")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("di")) || string_equal(clobber, S8("dil")) ||
0016107:                                             codegen_inline_assembly_clobber_vector_register(clobber, &vector_clobber);
0016108:                             if (!accepted && clobber.length >= 2 && clobber.pointer[0] == 'r')
0016109:                             {
0016110:                                 u64 number = 0;
0016111:                                 String8 suffix = {
0016112:                                     .pointer = clobber.pointer + 1,
0016113:                                     .length = clobber.length - 1,
0016114:                                 };
0016115:                                 accepted = codegen_decimal_number(suffix, &number) && number >= 8 && number <= 11;
0016116:                             }
0016117:                             if (!accepted)
0016118:                             {
0016119:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016120:                                 return result;
0016121:                             }
0016122:                         }
0016123:                         bool indirect_operands = false;
0016124:                         bool used_registers[12] = {0};
0016125:                         bool clobbered_registers[12] = {0};
0016126:                         // The SSE file is allocated out of its own pool: an 'x'
0016127:                         // operand takes a vector register and no general one,
0016128:                         // so the two allocations never collide and the parallel
0016129:                         // array is what says which file an operand landed in.
0016130:                         bool used_vector_registers[BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(codegen_x64_asm_vector_names)] = {0};
0016131:                         bool* indirect = arena_allocate(arena, bool, instruction->operand_count ? instruction->operand_count : 1);
0016132:                         X64Register* asm_registers = arena_allocate(arena, X64Register, instruction->operand_count ? instruction->operand_count : 1);
0016133:                         u32* asm_vector_registers = arena_allocate(arena, u32, instruction->operand_count ? instruction->operand_count : 1);
0016134:                         bool asm_vector_operands = false;
0016135:                         // The x87 stack is a stack, so what the emitter needs
0016136:                         // is which operand goes where rather than a register
0016137:                         // per operand. The frontend already refused every
0016138:                         // shape but the ones below; these say which of them
0016139:                         // this asm is.
0016140:                         u32 asm_x87_top = UINT32_MAX;
0016141:                         u32 asm_x87_below = UINT32_MAX;
0016142:                         bool asm_x87_stack_clobber = false;
0016143:                         for (u32 clobber_index = 0; clobber_index < asm_extra.clobber_count; clobber_index += 1)
0016144:                         {
0016145:                             asm_x87_stack_clobber |= string_equal(asm_extra.clobbers[clobber_index], S8("st"));
0016146:                         }
0016147:                         for (u32 clobber_index = 0; clobber_index < asm_extra.clobber_count; clobber_index += 1)
0016148:                         {
0016149:                             X64Register clobber_register = X64_REGISTER_RAX;
0016150:                             u32 vector_clobber = 0;
0016151:                             if (codegen_inline_assembly_clobber_register(asm_extra.clobbers[clobber_index], &clobber_register))
0016152:                             {
0016153:                                 clobbered_registers[clobber_register] = true;
0016154:                                 used_registers[clobber_register] = true;
0016155:                             }
0016156:                             else if (codegen_inline_assembly_clobber_vector_register(asm_extra.clobbers[clobber_index], &vector_clobber) &&
0016157:                                      vector_clobber < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(used_vector_registers))
0016158:                             {
0016159:                                 used_vector_registers[vector_clobber] = true;
0016160:                             }
0016161:                         }
0016162:                         // Reserve every fixed-register operand before assigning
0016163:                         // any generic r operand.  The allocation order is not
0016164:                         // part of GNU asm's constraint semantics: a generic
0016165:                         // operand appearing before an a/b/c/d operand must not
0016166:                         // steal that fixed register and make a valid operand
0016167:                         // list appear to alias two outputs.
0016168:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0016169:                         {
0016170:                             u64 constraint = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0016171:                             if (!codegen_inline_assembly_constraint_shape_valid(constraint, operand_index, instruction->operand_count, 0) ||
0016172:                                 (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) >= IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_COUNT)
0016173:                             {
0016174:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016175:                                 return result;
0016176:                             }
0016177:                             if ((constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0)
0016178:                             {
0016179:                                 u32 match_index = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(constraint);
0016180:                                 u64 output_constraint = instruction->immediates[match_index];
0016181:                                 if ((output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) == 0 ||
0016182:                                     (output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0 ||
0016183:                                     (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) !=
0016184:                                         (output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK))
0016185:                                 {
0016186:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016187:                                     return result;
0016188:                                 }
0016189:                                 for (u32 previous_index = 0; previous_index < operand_index; previous_index += 1)
0016190:                                 {
0016191:                                     u64 previous_constraint = instruction->immediates[previous_index];
0016192:                                     if ((previous_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0 &&
0016193:                                         IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(previous_constraint) == match_index)
0016194:                                     {
0016195:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016196:                                         return result;
0016197:                                     }
0016198:                                 }
0016199:                             }
0016200:                             X64Register fixed_register = X64_REGISTER_RAX;
0016201:                             if (codegen_inline_assembly_constraint_register(constraint, &fixed_register))
0016202:                             {
0016203:                                 if (clobbered_registers[fixed_register])
0016204:                                 {
0016205:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016206:                                     return result;
0016207:                                 }
0016208:                                 used_registers[fixed_register] = true;
0016209:                             }
0016210:                         }
0016211:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0016212:                         {
0016213:                             IrValueId operand = instruction->operands[operand_index];
0016214:                             if (operand.value >= function->value_count || function->values[operand.value].definition.value >= function->instruction_count)
0016215:                             {
0016216:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016217:                                 return result;
0016218:                             }
0016219:                             IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[operand.value].definition.value;
0016220:                             indirect[operand_index] = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0016221:                                                        definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE;
0016222:                             indirect_operands |= indirect[operand_index];
0016223:                         }
0016224:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0016225:                         {
0016226:                             u64 constraint = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0016227:                             u64 constraint_index = constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK;
0016228:                             if (constraint_index >= IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_COUNT)
0016229:                             {
0016230:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016231:                                 return result;
0016232:                             }
0016233:                             IrValueId operand = instruction->operands[operand_index];
0016234:                             if (operand.value >= function->value_count || function->values[operand.value].definition.value >= function->instruction_count)
0016235:                             {
0016236:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016237:                                 return result;
0016238:                             }
0016239:                             IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[operand.value].definition.value;
0016240:                             indirect[operand_index] = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0016241:                                                        definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE;
0016242:                             indirect_operands |= indirect[operand_index];
0016243:                             IrType* operand_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[operand.value].canonical_type);
0016244:                             bool vector_operand = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_VECTOR(constraint_index);
0016245:                             bool x87_operand = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_X87(constraint_index);
0016246:                             // A vector or x87 operand is checked whatever the
0016247:                             // template is, because the move that carries it in
0016248:                             // and out is emitted for the empty template too;
0016249:                             // the general file's check is the
0016250:                             // ordinary-template one it has always been.
0016251:                             if (vector_operand && !codegen_inline_assembly_vector_operand(operand_type))
0016252:                             {
0016253:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016254:                                 return result;
0016255:                             }
0016256:                             if (x87_operand)
0016257:                             {
0016258:                                 // The push and the pop-store are written
0016259:                                 // against a frame slot, whose sixteen bytes the
0016260:                                 // emitter owns down to the six it zeroes behind
0016261:                                 // an eighty-bit value. An operand reached
0016262:                                 // through a pointer is somebody else's storage,
0016263:                                 // so it stays out rather than being written
0016264:                                 // through a rule this path cannot state.
0016265:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80(operand_type) || indirect[operand_index] || x87_stack_depth)
0016266:                                 {
0016267:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016268:                                     return result;
0016269:                                 }
0016270:                                 u32* position = constraint_index == IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_T ? &asm_x87_top : &asm_x87_below;
0016271:                                 if (*position != UINT32_MAX)
0016272:                                 {
0016273:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016274:                                     return result;
0016275:                                 }
0016276:                                 *position = operand_index;
0016277:                             }
0016278:                             if (!vector_operand && !x87_operand && ordinary &&
0016279:                                 (codegen_inline_assembly_type_class(operand_type) == IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_INVALID ||
0016280:                                  !codegen_canonical_x64_asm_memory_width((u32)operand_type->layout.size)))
0016281:                             {
0016282:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016283:                                 return result;
0016284:                             }
0016285:                             asm_vector_operands |= vector_operand;
0016286:                             if ((constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0)
0016287:                             {
0016288:                                 if (codegen_inline_assembly_type_class(operand_type) == IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_INVALID)
0016289:                                 {
0016290:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016291:                                     return result;
0016292:                                 }
0016293:                                 u32 match_index = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(constraint);
0016294:                                 IrValueId output = instruction->operands[match_index];
0016295:                                 u64 output_constraint = instruction->immediates[match_index];
0016296:                                 IrType* output_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[output.value].canonical_type);
0016297:                                 if ((output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) == 0 ||
0016298:                                     (output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0 ||
0016299:                                     (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) !=
0016300:                                         (output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) ||
0016301:                                     !codegen_inline_assembly_types_compatible(output_type, operand_type))
0016302:                                 {
0016303:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016304:                                     return result;
0016305:                                 }
0016306:                                 for (u32 previous_index = 0; previous_index < operand_index; previous_index += 1)
0016307:                                 {
0016308:                                     u64 previous_constraint = instruction->immediates[previous_index];
0016309:                                     if ((previous_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0 &&
0016310:                                         IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(previous_constraint) == match_index)
0016311:                                     {
0016312:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016313:                                         return result;
0016314:                                     }
0016315:                                 }
0016316:                                 asm_registers[operand_index] = asm_registers[match_index];
0016317:                                 asm_vector_registers[operand_index] = asm_vector_registers[match_index];
0016318:                                 continue;
0016319:                             }
0016320:                             if (x87_operand)
0016321:                             {
0016322:                                 // A stack position is not allocated, so this
0016323:                                 // operand takes nothing out of either pool; the
0016324:                                 // parked general-file entry is the same one a
0016325:                                 // vector operand gets, for the same reason.
0016326:                                 asm_registers[operand_index] = X64_REGISTER_RSP;
0016327:                                 continue;
0016328:                             }
0016329:                             if (vector_operand)
0016330:                             {
0016331:                                 bool found_vector = false;
0016332:                                 for (u32 candidate = 0; candidate < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(used_vector_registers) && !found_vector; candidate += 1)
0016333:                                 {
0016334:                                     if (!used_vector_registers[candidate])
0016335:                                     {
0016336:                                         used_vector_registers[candidate] = true;
0016337:                                         asm_vector_registers[operand_index] = candidate;
0016338:                                         found_vector = true;
0016339:                                     }
0016340:                                 }
0016341:                                 if (!found_vector)
0016342:                                 {
0016343:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016344:                                     return result;
0016345:                                 }
0016346:                                 // RSP is never handed to an asm operand, so
0016347:                                 // parking the general-file entry there keeps
0016348:                                 // the aliasing scan above from ever matching a
0016349:                                 // register this operand does not occupy.
0016350:                                 asm_registers[operand_index] = X64_REGISTER_RSP;
0016351:                                 continue;
0016352:                             }
0016353:                             X64Register register_index = X64_REGISTER_RAX;
0016354:                             bool is_fixed_register = codegen_inline_assembly_constraint_register(constraint, &register_index);
0016355:                             if (is_fixed_register)
0016356:                             {
0016357:                                 if (clobbered_registers[register_index])
0016358:                                 {
0016359:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016360:                                     return result;
0016361:                                 }
0016362:                                 // R11 is the scratch the indirect operand path
0016363:                                 // loads addresses through, which is why the
0016364:                                 // generic allocator skips it there. A pinned
0016365:                                 // R11 operand cannot give that up, so the pair
0016366:                                 // is refused rather than silently clobbered.
0016367:                                 if (indirect_operands && register_index == X64_REGISTER_R11)
0016368:                                 {
0016369:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016370:                                     return result;
0016371:                                 }
0016372:                                 for (u32 previous_index = 0; previous_index < operand_index; previous_index += 1)
0016373:                                 {
0016374:                                     if (asm_registers[previous_index] == register_index)
0016375:                                     {
0016376:                                         u64 previous_constraint = instruction->immediates[previous_index];
0016377:                                         bool current_output = (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) != 0;
0016378:                                         bool previous_output = (previous_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) != 0;
0016379:                                         bool current_read_write = (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0;
0016380:                                         bool previous_read_write = (previous_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0;
0016381:                                         bool output_input_pair = current_output != previous_output &&
0016382:                                                                  ((current_output && !current_read_write) || (previous_output && !previous_read_write));
0016383:                                         if (!output_input_pair)
0016384:                                         {
0016385:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016386:                                             return result;
0016387:                                         }
0016388:                                     }
0016389:                                 }
0016390:                                 used_registers[register_index] = true;
0016391:                             }
0016392:                             else
0016393:                             {
0016394:                                 X64Register const* candidates = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS ? codegen_x64_asm_windows_registers
0016395:                                                                                                            : codegen_x64_asm_system_v_registers;
0016396:                                 u32 candidate_count = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_WINDOWS ? BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(codegen_x64_asm_windows_registers)
0016397:                                                                                                  : BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(codegen_x64_asm_system_v_registers);
0016398:                                 bool found = false;
0016399:                                 for (u32 candidate_index = 0; candidate_index < candidate_count; candidate_index += 1)
0016400:                                 {
0016401:                                     X64Register candidate = candidates[candidate_index];
0016402:                                     if (!used_registers[candidate] && !(indirect_operands && candidate == X64_REGISTER_R11))
0016403:                                     {
0016404:                                         register_index = candidate;
0016405:                                         used_registers[candidate] = true;
0016406:                                         found = true;
0016407:                                         break;
0016408:                                     }
0016409:                                 }
0016410:                                 if (!found)
0016411:                                 {
0016412:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016413:                                     return result;
0016414:                                 }
0016415:                             }
0016416:                             asm_registers[operand_index] = register_index;
0016417:                         }
0016418:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0016419:                         {
0016420:                             u64 constraint = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0016421:                             bool memory_operand = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_MEMORY(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK);
0016422:                             // An x87 operand is pushed by the step below rather
0016423:                             // than here, because the order the pushes go in is
0016424:                             // what puts each operand in its position and that
0016425:                             // order is not the operand order.
0016426:                             if (IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_X87(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK))
0016427:                             {
0016428:                                 continue;
0016429:                             }
0016430:                             if (!memory_operand && (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) &&
0016431:                                 !(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE))
0016432:                             {
0016433:                                 continue;
0016434:                             }
0016435:                             IrValueId input = instruction->operands[operand_index];
0016436:                             IrType* input_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[input.value].canonical_type);
0016437:                             if (!input_type || !input_type->layout.resolved || input_type->layout.size > UINT32_MAX ||
0016438:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_asm_memory_width((u32)input_type->layout.size))
0016439:                             {
0016440:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016441:                                 return result;
0016442:                             }
0016443:                             // A memory operand's register holds the address of
0016444:                             // its storage, so an indirect place hands over the
0016445:                             // pointer the slot already holds and a direct one
0016446:                             // hands over the slot's own address. Either way the
0016447:                             // template reference expands to a load through it,
0016448:                             // and nothing is read or written here.
0016449:                             if (memory_operand)
0016450:                             {
0016451:                                 u32 displacement = (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[input.value]);
0016452:                                 if (indirect[operand_index])
0016453:                                 {
0016454:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_RBP, displacement, 8);
0016455:                                 }
0016456:                                 else
0016457:                                 {
0016458:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_asm_address(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_RBP, displacement);
0016459:                                 }
0016460:                                 continue;
0016461:                             }
0016462:                             bool vector_input = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_VECTOR(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK);
0016463:                             if (indirect[operand_index])
0016464:                             {
0016465:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_R11, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016466:                                                               (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[input.value]), 8);
0016467:                                 if (vector_input)
0016468:                                 {
0016469:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_asm_vector_memory(&buffer, asm_vector_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_R11, 0,
0016470:                                                                             (u32)input_type->layout.size, false);
0016471:                                 }
0016472:                                 else
0016473:                                 {
0016474:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_R11, 0,
0016475:                                                                   (u32)input_type->layout.size);
0016476:                                 }
0016477:                             }
0016478:                             else if (vector_input)
0016479:                             {
0016480:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_asm_vector_memory(&buffer, asm_vector_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016481:                                                                         (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[input.value]),
0016482:                                                                         (u32)input_type->layout.size, false);
0016483:                             }
0016484:                             else
0016485:                             {
0016486:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016487:                                                               (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[input.value]),
0016488:                                                               (u32)input_type->layout.size);
0016489:                             }
0016490:                         }
0016491:                         // The x87 operands go onto the stack last and deepest
0016492:                         // first, so the `u` operand ends at ST(1) and the `t`
0016493:                         // operand on top -- which is what their names mean.
0016494:                         // musl's `fprem` reads both that way.
0016495:                         if (asm_x87_below != UINT32_MAX &&
0016496:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_x87_push(&buffer, false, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016497:                                                             c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[asm_x87_below].value]),
0016498:                                                             80, &x87_stack_depth))
0016499:                         {
0016500:                             result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016501:                             return result;
0016502:                         }
0016503:                         if (asm_x87_top != UINT32_MAX &&
0016504:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_x87_push(&buffer, false, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016505:                                                             c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[asm_x87_top].value]),
0016506:                                                             80, &x87_stack_depth))
0016507:                         {
0016508:                             result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016509:                             return result;
0016510:                         }
0016511:                         if (cpuid || undefined || nop || interrupt)
0016512:                         {
0016513:                             String8 mnemonic = cpuid ? S8("CPUID") : undefined ? S8("UD2") : nop ? S8("NOP") : S8("INT3");
0016514:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, mnemonic, 0, 0))
0016515:                             {
0016516:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0016517:                                 return result;
0016518:                             }
0016519:                         }
0016520:                         else if (xgetbv)
0016521:                         {
0016522:                             AssemblyEncodeResult encoded = assembly_encode(arena, asm_extra.literal,
0016523:                                                                             (AssemblyEncodeOptions){
0016524:                                                                                 .target = target,
0016525:                                                                                 .syntax = codegen_inline_assembly_syntax(options),
0016526:                                                                             });
0016527:                             if (encoded.diagnostic_count || encoded.relocation_count)
0016528:                             {
0016529:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016530:                                 return result;
0016531:                             }
0016532:                             for (u32 symbol_index = 0; symbol_index < encoded.symbol_count; symbol_index += 1)
0016533:                             {
0016534:                                 if (!encoded.symbols[symbol_index].defined)
0016535:                                 {
0016536:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016537:                                     return result;
0016538:                                 }
0016539:                             }
0016540:                             u8* encoded_bytes = 0;
0016541:                             if (!codegen_buffer_reserve(&buffer, encoded.bytes.length, &encoded_bytes))
0016542:                             {
0016543:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0016544:                                 return result;
0016545:                             }
0016546:                             if (encoded.bytes.length)
0016547:                             {
0016548:                                 memcpy(encoded_bytes, encoded.bytes.pointer, encoded.bytes.length);
0016549:                             }
0016550:                         }
0016551:                         else if (pause)
0016552:                         {
0016553:                             // PAUSE is an architectural baseline instruction
0016554:                             // even though the imported ISA row is tagged with
0016555:                             // the PAUSE feature.  Inline asm has historically
0016556:                             // accepted it unconditionally; provide that
0016557:                             // explicit feature authorization while still
0016558:                             // emitting through the canonical metadata bridge.
0016559:                             String8 feature_names[] = {S8("pause")};
0016560:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0016561:                                     &buffer, S8("PAUSE"), 0, 0,
0016562:                                     (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = feature_names, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(feature_names)}))
0016563:                             {
0016564:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0016565:                                 return result;
0016566:                             }
0016567:                         }
0016568:                         else if (ordinary)
0016569:                         {
0016570:                             String8 source = {0};
0016571:                             if (!codegen_inline_assembly_resolve_template(arena, program, function, instruction, asm_extra, asm_registers,
0016572:                                                                            asm_vector_registers, codegen_inline_assembly_syntax(options), &source,
0016573:                                                                            &result.failure_reason))
0016574:                             {
0016575:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016576:                                 return result;
0016577:                             }
0016578:                             if (!codegen_emit_inline_assembly(arena, program, target, options, source, asm_extra.literal, &buffer, &result,
0016579:                                                              relocation_capacity))
0016580:                             {
0016581:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016582:                                 return result;
0016583:                             }
0016584:                         }
0016585:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0016586:                         {
0016587:                             u64 constraint = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0016588:                             // An x87 output was stored and popped by the unwind
0016589:                             // above, which is where the stack discipline is;
0016590:                             // storing a register over it here would have no
0016591:                             // register to store.
0016592:                             if (IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_X87(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK))
0016593:                             {
0016594:                                 continue;
0016595:                             }
0016596:                             // A memory output was written by the assembly
0016597:                             // itself, through the address its register holds.
0016598:                             // Storing a register back over it here would undo
0016599:                             // exactly the write the operand exists for.
0016600:                             if (!(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) ||
0016601:                                 IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_MEMORY(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK))
0016602:                             {
0016603:                                 continue;
0016604:                             }
0016605:                             IrValueId place_id = instruction->operands[operand_index];
0016606:                             if (place_id.value >= function->value_count)
0016607:                             {
0016608:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016609:                                 return result;
0016610:                             }
0016611:                             IrValue* place = function->values + place_id.value;
0016612:                             if (place->definition.value >= function->instruction_count)
0016613:                             {
0016614:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016615:                                 return result;
0016616:                             }
0016617:                             IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0016618:                             IrType* output_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, place->canonical_type);
0016619:                             if (!output_type || !output_type->layout.resolved || output_type->layout.size > UINT32_MAX ||
0016620:                                 !codegen_canonical_x64_asm_memory_width((u32)output_type->layout.size))
0016621:                             {
0016622:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016623:                                 return result;
0016624:                             }
0016625:                             bool output_indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0016626:                                                    definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE;
0016627:                             bool vector_output = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_IS_VECTOR(constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK);
0016628:                             if (output_indirect)
0016629:                             {
0016630:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_asm_load(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_R11, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016631:                                                               (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[place_id.value]), 8);
0016632:                                 if (vector_output)
0016633:                                 {
0016634:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_asm_vector_memory(&buffer, asm_vector_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_R11, 0,
0016635:                                                                             (u32)output_type->layout.size, true);
0016636:                                 }
0016637:                                 else
0016638:                                 {
0016639:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_asm_store(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_R11, asm_registers[operand_index], 0,
0016640:                                                                    (u32)output_type->layout.size);
0016641:                                 }
0016642:                             }
0016643:                             else if (vector_output)
0016644:                             {
0016645:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_asm_vector_memory(&buffer, asm_vector_registers[operand_index], X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016646:                                                                         (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[place_id.value]),
0016647:                                                                         (u32)output_type->layout.size, true);
0016648:                             }
0016649:                             else
0016650:                             {
0016651:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_asm_store(&buffer, X64_REGISTER_RBP, asm_registers[operand_index],
0016652:                                                                (u32)c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[place_id.value]),
0016653:                                                                (u32)output_type->layout.size);
0016654:                             }
0016655:                         }
0016656:                         // Unwinding the stack the pushes above built, and it
0016657:                         // runs after the general-file stores rather than
0016658:                         // straight after the template: the six padding bytes a
0016659:                         // stored eighty-bit value is followed by are zeroed
0016660:                         // through RAX, and musl's `fmodl` reads its status word
0016661:                         // back out of AX through an `"=a"` output. Storing that
0016662:                         // output first is what keeps the two apart.
0016663:                         //
0016664:                         // A template that declared the `st` clobber popped what
0016665:                         // it was handed -- musl's `fistpll` -- so the depth is
0016666:                         // one shallower than the pushes left it and nothing is
0016667:                         // read back; an output in ST(0) is stored and popped;
0016668:                         // and whatever is still standing after that is the `u`
0016669:                         // operand, which the template read and nobody else
0016670:                         // wants.
0016671:                         if (asm_x87_stack_clobber)
0016672:                         {
0016673:                             if (!x87_stack_depth)
0016674:                             {
0016675:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0016676:                                 return result;
0016677:                             }
0016678:                             x87_stack_depth -= 1;
0016679:                         }
0016680:                         if (asm_x87_top != UINT32_MAX && (instruction->immediates[asm_x87_top] & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) &&
0016681:                             !codegen_canonical_x64_x87_pop_store(&buffer, false, X64_REGISTER_RBP,
0016682:                                                                  c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[instruction->operands[asm_x87_top].value]),
0016683:                                                                  80, &x87_stack_depth))
0016684:                         {
0016685:                             result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016686:                             return result;
0016687:                         }
0016688:                         while (x87_stack_depth)
0016689:                         {
0016690:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_x87_discard(&buffer, &x87_stack_depth))
0016691:                             {
0016692:                                 result.error = buffer.error != CODEGEN_ERROR_NONE ? buffer.error : CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016693:                                 return result;
0016694:                             }
0016695:                         }
0016696:                         // A template that ran with operands in the SSE file
0016697:                         // wrote vector registers the wide-vector cache still
0016698:                         // believes hold a value, so the cache is dropped here
0016699:                         // the same way an explicit SIMD operation drops it.
0016700:                         if (asm_vector_operands)
0016701:                         {
0016702:                             x64_last_wide_vector_result = IR_VALUE_ID_INVALID;
0016703:                             x64_last_wide_vector_size = 0;
0016704:                         }
0016705:                         // Inline assembly may use RBX for a fixed b operand or
0016706:                         // declare it clobbered.  Restore the ABI-owned value
0016707:                         // before either falling through or taking an asm-goto
0016708:                         // edge; otherwise a successor can observe the clobber
0016709:                         // or call with an invalid callee-saved register.
0016710:                         c_x64_restore_rbx(&emitter);
0016711:                         if (jump_label)
0016712:                         {
0016713:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand jump_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0016714:                             u32 jump_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0016715:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &jump_operand, 1))
0016716:                             {
0016717:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0016718:                                 return result;
0016719:                             }
0016720:                             if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0016721:                                 .target = instruction->targets[jump_target_index],
0016722:                                 .offset = jump_offset + 1,
0016723:                                 }))
0016724:                             {
0016725:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0016726:                                 return result;
0016727:                             }
0016728:                         }
0016729:                         else if (instruction->target_count)
0016730:                         {
0016731:                             BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand jump_operand = codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_relative(0, 32);
0016732:                             u32 jump_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0016733:                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("JMP"), &jump_operand, 1))
0016734:                             {
0016735:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0016736:                                 return result;
0016737:                             }
0016738:                             if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0016739:                                 .target = instruction->targets[0],
0016740:                                 .offset = jump_offset + 1,
0016741:                                 }))
0016742:                             {
0016743:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0016744:                                 return result;
0016745:                             }
0016746:                         }
0016747:                     }
0016748:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEBUG_TRAP)
0016749:                     {
0016750:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("INT3"), 0, 0))
0016751:                         {
0016752:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0016753:                             return result;
0016754:                         }
0016755:                     }
0016756:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_UNREACHABLE)
0016757:                     {
0016758:                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("UD2"), 0, 0))
0016759:                         {
0016760:                             result.error = buffer.error;
0016761:                             return result;
0016762:                         }
0016763:                     }
0016764:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_RETURN)
0016765:                     {
0016766:                         if (instruction->operand_count)
0016767:                         {
0016768:                             IrValueId return_value = instruction->operands[0];
0016769:                             IrType* return_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type);
0016770:                             u32 return_parts = 0;
0016771:                             bool aggregate_return =
0016772:                                 codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type, &return_parts);
0016773:                             CodegenCanonicalAbiValue aggregate_return_abi = codegen_canonical_x64_windows_vector_result(
0016774:                                 program, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type, result.abi, &target,
0016775:                                 codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type, result.abi, true, false));
0016776:                             bool return_f80_complex = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V &&
0016777:                                                       codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_complex_cached(
0016778:                                                           f80_cache, program, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type);
0016779:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_type_contains_f80_cached(f80_cache, program, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type) &&
0016780:                                 !(result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V &&
0016781:                                   codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_opaque_cached(f80_cache, program,
0016782:                                                                                   function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type)))
0016783:                             {
0016784:                                 if (result.abi != CODEGEN_ABI_X86_64_SYSTEM_V || x87_stack_depth != 0 ||
0016785:                                     (!return_f80_complex &&
0016786:                                      (!codegen_canonical_x64_type_is_f80_x87_shape_cached(f80_cache, program,
0016787:                                                                                           function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type) ||
0016788:                                       !codegen_canonical_x64_abi_is_f80_result(return_type, &aggregate_return_abi))))
0016789:                                 {
0016790:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0016791:                                     return result;
0016792:                                 }
0016793:                                 s32 return_f80_displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[return_value.value]);
0016794:                                 if (return_f80_complex)
0016795:                                 {
0016796:                                     // COMPLEX_X87 wants the real half on top,
0016797:                                     // so the imaginary one is pushed first and
0016798:                                     // ends up in ST1.
0016799:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_x87_memory(&buffer, false, X64_REGISTER_RBP, return_f80_displacement + 16);
0016800:                                     x87_stack_depth += 1;
0016801:                                 }
0016802:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_x87_memory(&buffer, false, X64_REGISTER_RBP, return_f80_displacement);
0016803:                                 x87_stack_depth += 1;
0016804:                                 c_x64_restore_rbx(&emitter);
0016805:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_emit_return(&buffer, frame_size, result.abi, windows_dynamic_stack);
0016806:                                 x87_stack_depth = 0; // the RET terminates this path; the next block starts with an empty stack.
0016807:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0016808:                                 continue;
0016809:                             }
0016810:                             if (aggregate_return_abi.part_count && !aggregate_return_abi.indirect)
0016811:                             {
0016812:                                 aggregate_return = true;
0016813:                                 return_parts = aggregate_return_abi.part_count;
0016814:                             }
0016815:                             if (codegen_canonical_x64_result_in_abi_parts(&aggregate_return_abi, result.abi))
0016816:                             {
0016817:                                 u32 integer_index = 0;
0016818:                                 u32 float_index = 0;
0016819:                                 for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < aggregate_return_abi.part_count; part_index += 1)
0016820:                                 {
0016821:                                     CodegenCanonicalAbiPart* part = aggregate_return_abi.parts + part_index;
0016822:                                     s32 displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[return_value.value]) + (s32)part->value_offset;
0016823:                                     if (codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(part->abi_class))
0016824:                                     {
0016825:                                         u32 register_size = 0;
0016826:                                         u32 register_count_used = codegen_canonical_x64_vector_part_registers(&target, part->size, &register_size);
0016827:                                         // Two vector registers is what a result
0016828:                                         // gets, unless the part is one this
0016829:                                         // target has to split, which takes as
0016830:                                         // many as it takes and starts at zero.
0016831:                                         if (!register_count_used || float_index + register_count_used > BUSTER_MAX((u32)2, register_count_used))
0016832:                                         {
0016833:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0016834:                                             return result;
0016835:                                         }
0016836:                                         for (u32 chunk = 0; chunk < register_count_used; chunk += 1)
0016837:                                         {
0016838:                                             if (!codegen_canonical_x64_float_memory(&buffer, target, float_index, displacement + (s32)(chunk * register_size),
0016839:                                                                                     register_size, false))
0016840:                                             {
0016841:                                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0016842:                                                 return result;
0016843:                                             }
0016844:                                             float_index += 1;
0016845:                                         }
0016846:                                     }
0016847:                                     else
0016848:                                     {
0016849:                                         if (integer_index >= 2)
0016850:                                         {
0016851:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0016852:                                             return result;
0016853:                                         }
0016854:                                         X64Register result_register = integer_index ? X64_REGISTER_RDX : X64_REGISTER_RAX;
0016855:                                         BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_operands[2] = {
0016856:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(result_register, 64),
0016857:                                             codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, displacement),
0016858:                                         };
0016859:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), load_operands,
0016860:                                                                                    BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_operands)))
0016861:                                         {
0016862:                                             result.error = buffer.error;
0016863:                                             return result;
0016864:                                         }
0016865:                                         integer_index += 1;
0016866:                                     }
0016867:                                 }
0016868:                                 c_x64_restore_rbx(&emitter);
0016869:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_emit_return(&buffer, frame_size, result.abi, windows_dynamic_stack);
0016870:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0016871:                                 continue;
0016872:                             }
0016873:                             if (aggregate_return_abi.indirect)
0016874:                             {
0016875:                                 if (!x64_indirect_return)
0016876:                                 {
0016877:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0016878:                                     return result;
0016879:                                 }
0016880:                                 s64 hidden_displacement = codegen_canonical_x64_rebase_frame_displacement(&buffer, hidden_result_displacement,
0016881:                                                                                                              canonical_x64_frame_base_offset);
0016882:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand hidden_load_operands[2] = {
0016883:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0016884:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, hidden_displacement),
0016885:                                 };
0016886:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), hidden_load_operands,
0016887:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(hidden_load_operands)))
0016888:                                 {
0016889:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0016890:                                     return result;
0016891:                                 }
0016892:                                 // Copy the exact type size: the caller
0016893:                                 // allocated exactly the type behind the
0016894:                                 // hidden pointer, and clang keeps its stack
0016895:                                 // canary right after, so the rounding the
0016896:                                 // eightbyte part count carries must not be
0016897:                                 // written.
0016898:                                 IrType* return_layout_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type);
0016899:                                 u64 return_copy_size =
0016900:                                     return_layout_type && return_layout_type->layout.resolved ? return_layout_type->layout.size : (u64)return_parts * 8;
0016901:                                 u64 return_copied = 0;
0016902:                                 while (return_copied < return_copy_size)
0016903:                                 {
0016904:                                     u64 return_remaining = return_copy_size - return_copied;
0016905:                                     u16 chunk_bits = return_remaining >= 8 ? 64 : return_remaining >= 4 ? 32 : return_remaining >= 2 ? 16 : 8;
0016906:                                     u16 load_register_bits = chunk_bits <= 16 ? 32 : chunk_bits;
0016907:                                     s64 displacement = c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[return_value.value]) + (s64)return_copied;
0016908:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand load_part_operands[2] = {
0016909:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, load_register_bits),
0016910:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, chunk_bits, displacement),
0016911:                                     };
