0016912:                                     BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand store_part_operands[2] = {
0016913:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory_relaxed(X64_REGISTER_RCX, chunk_bits, (s64)return_copied),
0016914:                                         codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, chunk_bits),
0016915:                                     };
0016916:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, chunk_bits <= 16 ? S8("MOVZX") : S8("MOV"), load_part_operands,
0016917:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(load_part_operands)) ||
0016918:                                         !codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), store_part_operands,
0016919:                                                                                BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(store_part_operands)))
0016920:                                     {
0016921:                                         result.error = buffer.error;
0016922:                                         return result;
0016923:                                     }
0016924:                                     return_copied += chunk_bits / 8;
0016925:                                 }
0016926:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand move_result_operands[2] = {
0016927:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RAX, 64),
0016928:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RCX, 64),
0016929:                                 };
0016930:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), move_result_operands,
0016931:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(move_result_operands)))
0016932:                                 {
0016933:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0016934:                                     return result;
0016935:                                 }
0016936:                                 c_x64_restore_rbx(&emitter);
0016937:                                 codegen_canonical_x64_emit_return(&buffer, frame_size, result.abi, windows_dynamic_stack);
0016938:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0016939:                                 continue;
0016940:                             }
0016941:                             if (return_type && return_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0016942:                             {
0016943:                                 if (return_type->bit_width != 32 && return_type->bit_width != 64)
0016944:                                 {
0016945:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0016946:                                     return result;
0016947:                                 }
0016948:                                 u16 float_width = (u16)return_type->bit_width;
0016949:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand float_load_operands[2] = {
0016950:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_vector(0, float_width),
0016951:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(X64_REGISTER_RBP, float_width,
0016952:                                                                             c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[return_value.value])),
0016953:                                 };
0016954:                                 String8 float_features[] = {return_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("sse") : S8("sse2")};
0016955:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit_features(
0016956:                                         &buffer, return_type->bit_width == 32 ? S8("MOVSS") : S8("MOVQ"), float_load_operands,
0016957:                                         BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_load_operands),
0016958:                                         (BusterX86MetadataFeatureInput){.names = float_features, .count = BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(float_features)}))
0016959:                                 {
0016960:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0016961:                                     return result;
0016962:                                 }
0016963:                             }
0016964:                             else
0016965:                             {
0016966:                                 c_x64_load(&emitter, 0x85, return_value);
0016967:                             }
0016968:                             if (aggregate_return && return_parts == 2)
0016969:                             {
0016970:                                 BusterX86MetadataPhysicalOperand high_return_operands[2] = {
0016971:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_gpr(X64_REGISTER_RDX, 64),
0016972:                                     codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_memory(
0016973:                                         X64_REGISTER_RBP, 64, c_x64_frame_displacement(&emitter, value_offsets[return_value.value]) + 8),
0016974:                                 };
0016975:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_x64_metadata_emit(&buffer, S8("MOV"), high_return_operands,
0016976:                                                                            BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(high_return_operands)))
0016977:                                 {
0016978:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0016979:                                     return result;
0016980:                                 }
0016981:                             }
0016982:                         }
0016983:                         c_x64_restore_rbx(&emitter);
0016984:                         codegen_canonical_x64_emit_return(&buffer, frame_size, result.abi, windows_dynamic_stack);
0016985:                     }
0016986:                     else
0016987:                     {
0016988:                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0016989:                         return result;
0016990:                     }
0016991:                 }
0016992:                 else
0016993:                 {
0016994:                     u32 result_offset = instruction->result.value == IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID ? 0 : value_offsets[instruction->result.value];
0016995:                     if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
0016996:                     {
0016997:                         IrValue* local = function->values + instruction->result.value;
0016998:                         u32 local_alignment = local->alignment;
0016999:                         codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, aligned_local_offsets[instruction->result.value]);
0017000:                         a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, local_alignment - 1);
0017001:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
0017002:                         a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, ~(u64)(local_alignment - 1));
0017003:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a0a0129);
0017004:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017005:                     }
0017006:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STACK_ALLOCATE)
0017007:                     {
0017008:                         u32 stack_alignment = (u32)instruction->immediates[0];
0017009:                         stack_alignment = BUSTER_MAX(stack_alignment, 16);
0017010:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0017011:                         if (stack_alignment > 16)
0017012:                         {
0017013:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xcb2963e9); // sub x9, sp, x9
0017014:                         }
0017015:                         else
0017016:                         {
0017017:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, stack_alignment - 1);
0017018:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
0017019:                         }
0017020:                         a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, ~(u64)(stack_alignment - 1));
0017021:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a0a0129);
0017022:                         if (stack_alignment > 16)
0017023:                         {
0017024:                             // The aligned target becomes the full probe distance.
0017025:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xcb2963e9); // sub x9, sp, x9
0017026:                         }
0017027:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf140053f);
0017028:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x540000a3);
0017029:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd14007ff);
0017030:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf94003ff);
0017031:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd1400529);
0017032:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x17fffffb);
0017033:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xcb2963ff);
0017034:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf94003ff);
0017035:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x910003ea);
0017036:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
0017037:                     }
0017038:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STACK_SAVE)
0017039:                     {
0017040:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x910003e9);
0017041:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017042:                     }
0017043:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STACK_RESTORE)
0017044:                     {
0017045:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0017046:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9100013f);
0017047:                     }
0017048:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ARGUMENT)
0017049:                     {
0017050:                         u32 argument_index = (u32)instruction->immediates[0];
0017051:                         IrType* function_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->canonical_type);
0017052:                         if (!function_type || function_type->kind != IR_TYPE_FUNCTION || argument_index >= function_type->parameter_count)
0017053:                         {
0017054:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0017055:                             return result;
0017056:                         }
0017057:                         u32 register_index = 0;
0017058:                         u32 float_register_index = 0;
0017059:                         u32 prior_stack_parts = 0;
0017060:                         for (u32 prior_index = 0; prior_index < argument_index; prior_index += 1)
0017061:                         {
0017062:                             u32 prior_parts = 1;
0017063:                             bool prior_aggregate =
0017064:                                 codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function_type->parameter_types[prior_index], &prior_parts);
0017065:                             IrType* prior_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function_type->parameter_types[prior_index]);
0017066:                             CodegenCanonicalAbiValue prior_abi =
0017067:                                 codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, function_type->parameter_types[prior_index], result.abi, false, false);
0017068:                             bool prior_hfa = prior_abi.part_count != 0;
0017069:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < prior_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0017070:                             {
0017071:                                 prior_hfa &= codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(prior_abi.parts[part].abi_class);
0017072:                             }
0017073:                             if (prior_hfa && !aarch64_windows_variadic)
0017074:                             {
0017075:                                 if (float_register_index + prior_abi.part_count <= 8)
0017076:                                 {
0017077:                                     float_register_index += prior_abi.part_count;
0017078:                                 }
0017079:                                 else
0017080:                                 {
0017081:                                     float_register_index = 8;
0017082:                                     prior_stack_parts = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(prior_stack_parts, prior_type->layout.alignment > 8);
0017083:                                     prior_stack_parts += (u32)((prior_type->layout.size + 7) / 8);
0017084:                                 }
0017085:                                 continue;
0017086:                             }
0017087:                             if (prior_type && prior_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && !aarch64_windows_variadic)
0017088:                             {
0017089:                                 if (float_register_index < 8)
0017090:                                 {
0017091:                                     float_register_index += 1;
0017092:                                 }
0017093:                                 else
0017094:                                 {
0017095:                                     prior_stack_parts += 1;
0017096:                                 }
0017097:                                 continue;
0017098:                             }
0017099:                             bool prior_indirect = prior_aggregate && prior_type && prior_type->layout.size > 16;
0017100:                             if (prior_indirect)
0017101:                             {
0017102:                                 prior_parts = 1;
0017103:                             }
0017104:                             bool prior_even_integer_pair =
0017105:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_even_integer_pair(program, function_type->parameter_types[prior_index], result.abi, false);
0017106:                             if (prior_even_integer_pair && register_index < 8)
0017107:                             {
0017108:                                 // AAPCS64 C.8: a 16-byte INTEGER value starts
0017109:                                 // at an even-numbered X register.
0017110:                                 register_index = (register_index + 1u) & ~1u;
0017111:                             }
0017112:                             if (register_index + prior_parts <= 8)
0017113:                             {
0017114:                                 register_index += prior_parts;
0017115:                             }
0017116:                             else
0017117:                             {
0017118:                                 // AAPCS64 C.12: a composite that does not fit
0017119:                                 // the remaining integer registers closes the
0017120:                                 // file as it goes to the stack.
0017121:                                 if (prior_aggregate && !prior_indirect)
0017122:                                 {
0017123:                                     register_index = 8;
0017124:                                 }
0017125:                                 prior_stack_parts = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(prior_stack_parts, prior_even_integer_pair);
0017126:                                 prior_stack_parts += prior_parts;
0017127:                             }
0017128:                         }
0017129:                         u32 part_count = 1;
0017130:                         IrType* argument_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0017131:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &part_count);
0017132:                         CodegenCanonicalAbiValue argument_abi = codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, false, false);
0017133:                         bool argument_hfa = argument_abi.part_count != 0;
0017134:                         for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0017135:                         {
0017136:                             argument_hfa &= codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(argument_abi.parts[part].abi_class);
0017137:                         }
0017138:                         bool indirect = aggregate && argument_type && argument_type->layout.size > 16;
0017139:                         u32 abi_part_count = indirect ? 1 : part_count;
0017140:                         bool even_integer_pair =
0017141:                             codegen_canonical_a64_even_integer_pair(program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, false);
0017142:                         if (even_integer_pair && register_index < 8)
0017143:                         {
0017144:                             register_index = (register_index + 1u) & ~1u;
0017145:                         }
0017146:                         if (!argument_type || ((argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION) && !aggregate))
0017147:                         {
0017148:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0017149:                             return result;
0017150:                         }
0017151:                         if (argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && !aarch64_windows_variadic)
0017152:                         {
0017153:                             if (argument_type->bit_width != 32 && argument_type->bit_width != 64)
0017154:                             {
0017155:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0017156:                                 return result;
0017157:                             }
0017158:                             if (float_register_index < 8)
0017159:                             {
0017160:                                 u32 scale = argument_type->bit_width == 32 ? 4 : 8;
0017161:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, float_register_index, result_offset, scale, true))
0017162:                                 {
0017163:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017164:                                     return result;
0017165:                                 }
0017166:                             }
0017167:                             else
0017168:                             {
0017169:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, 16 + prior_stack_parts * 8, 8, false, false, 29) ||
0017170:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset, 8, true, false))
0017171:                                 {
0017172:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017173:                                     return result;
0017174:                                 }
0017175:                             }
0017176:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017177:                             continue;
0017178:                         }
0017179:                         if (argument_hfa && !aarch64_windows_variadic)
0017180:                         {
0017181:                             if (float_register_index + argument_abi.part_count <= 8)
0017182:                             {
0017183:                                 for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0017184:                                 {
0017185:                                     codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, float_register_index + part,
0017186:                                                                                        result_offset + argument_abi.parts[part].value_offset,
0017187:                                                                                        argument_abi.parts[part].size, true);
0017188:                                 }
0017189:                             }
0017190:                             else
0017191:                             {
0017192:                                 prior_stack_parts = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(prior_stack_parts, argument_type->layout.alignment > 8);
0017193:                                 for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0017194:                                 {
0017195:                                     CodegenCanonicalAbiPart* abi_part = argument_abi.parts + part;
0017196:                                     u32 copied = 0;
0017197:                                     while (copied < abi_part->size)
0017198:                                     {
0017199:                                         u32 remaining = abi_part->size - copied;
0017200:                                         u32 chunk = remaining >= 8 ? 8 : 4;
0017201:                                         u32 source_offset = 16 + prior_stack_parts * 8 + abi_part->value_offset + copied;
0017202:                                         u32 destination_offset = result_offset + abi_part->value_offset + copied;
0017203:                                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, source_offset, chunk, false, false, 29) ||
0017204:                                             !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, destination_offset, chunk, true, false))
0017205:                                         {
0017206:                                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017207:                                             return result;
0017208:                                         }
0017209:                                         copied += chunk;
0017210:                                     }
0017211:                                 }
0017212:                             }
0017213:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017214:                             continue;
0017215:                         }
0017216:                         if (register_index + abi_part_count > 8)
0017217:                         {
0017218:                             if (indirect)
0017219:                             {
0017220:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 10, 16 + prior_stack_parts * 8, 8, false, false, 29))
0017221:                                 {
0017222:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017223:                                     return result;
0017224:                                 }
0017225:                                 for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0017226:                                 {
0017227:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, part_index * 8, 8, false, false, 10) ||
0017228:                                         !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + part_index * 8, 8, true, false))
0017229:                                     {
0017230:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017231:                                         return result;
0017232:                                     }
0017233:                                 }
0017234:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017235:                                 continue;
0017236:                             }
0017237:                             prior_stack_parts = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(prior_stack_parts, even_integer_pair);
0017238:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0017239:                             {
0017240:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, 16 + (prior_stack_parts + part_index) * 8, 8, false, false, 29) ||
0017241:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + part_index * 8, 8, true, false))
0017242:                                 {
0017243:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017244:                                     return result;
0017245:                                 }
0017246:                             }
0017247:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017248:                             continue;
0017249:                         }
0017250:                         if (indirect)
0017251:                         {
0017252:                             u32 source_register = register_index;
0017253:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0017254:                             {
0017255:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, part_index * 8, 8, false, false, source_register) ||
0017256:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + part_index * 8, 8, true, false))
0017257:                                 {
0017258:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017259:                                     return result;
0017260:                                 }
0017261:                             }
0017262:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017263:                             continue;
0017264:                         }
0017265:                         // AAPCS64 leaves the bits above a narrow integer
0017266:                         // argument's declared width unspecified in its
0017267:                         // register, and every canonical value lives
0017268:                         // zero-extended in its eightbyte slot, so the incoming
0017269:                         // register is normalized to its width before it is
0017270:                         // stored.  `mov w<n>, w<n>` clears the top half;
0017271:                         // `uxtb`/`uxth` clear all but the declared bits.
0017272:                         u16 argument_bits = abi_part_count == 1 && argument_type &&
0017273:                                                     (argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER || argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN)
0017274:                                                 ? (u16)(argument_type->layout.size * 8)
0017275:                                                 : (u16)64;
0017276:                         if (argument_bits && argument_bits < 64)
0017277:                         {
0017278:                             u32 narrow_register = register_index;
0017279:                             u32 encoding = argument_bits == 8    ? (0x53001c00u | (narrow_register << 5) | narrow_register)
0017280:                                            : argument_bits == 16 ? (0x53003c00u | (narrow_register << 5) | narrow_register)
0017281:                                                                  : (0x2a0003e0u | (narrow_register << 16) | narrow_register);
0017282:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoding);
0017283:                         }
0017284:                         for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < abi_part_count; part_index += 1)
0017285:                         {
0017286:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, register_index + part_index, result_offset + part_index * 8, 8, true,
0017287:                                                                              false))
0017288:                             {
0017289:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017290:                                 return result;
0017291:                             }
0017292:                         }
0017293:                     }
0017294:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FUNCTION)
0017295:                     {
0017296:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FUNCTION && direct_call_uses[instruction->result.value] == 1)
0017297:                         {
0017298:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa1f03e9);
0017299:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017300:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017301:                             continue;
0017302:                         }
0017303:                         IrSymbol* symbol = ir_symbol_from_id(&program->symbols, instruction->symbol);
0017304:                         bool is_thread_local = instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL && symbol && symbol->is_thread_local;
0017305:                         if (is_thread_local)
0017306:                         {
0017307:                             if (target.os == OPERATING_SYSTEM_WINDOWS)
0017308:                             {
0017309:                                 u32 index_high_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017310:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x90000009);
0017311:                                 u32 index_low_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017312:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xb9400129);
0017313:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf9402e4a);
0017314:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf8697949);
0017315:                                 u32 value_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017316:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000129);
0017317:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017318:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017319:                                     .offset = index_high_offset,
0017320:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_PE_TLS_INDEX_ADRP,
0017321:                                 };
0017322:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017323:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017324:                                     .offset = index_low_offset,
0017325:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_PE_TLS_INDEX_LO12,
0017326:                                 };
0017327:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017328:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017329:                                     .offset = value_offset,
0017330:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_PE_TLS_OFFSET12,
0017331:                                 };
0017332:                             }
0017333:                             else if (target.os == OPERATING_SYSTEM_MACOS || target.os == OPERATING_SYSTEM_IOS)
0017334:                             {
0017335:                                 u32 descriptor_high_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017336:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x90000000);
0017337:                                 u32 descriptor_low_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017338:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf9400000);
0017339:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf9400008);
0017340:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd63f0100);
0017341:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0003e9);
0017342:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017343:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017344:                                     .offset = descriptor_high_offset,
0017345:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_MACH_TLVP_PAGE21,
0017346:                                 };
0017347:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017348:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017349:                                     .offset = descriptor_low_offset,
0017350:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_MACH_TLVP_PAGEOFF12,
0017351:                                 };
0017352:                             }
0017353:                             else if (target.os != OPERATING_SYSTEM_LINUX && target.os != OPERATING_SYSTEM_ANDROID)
0017354:                             {
0017355:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0017356:                                 return result;
0017357:                             }
0017358:                             else
0017359:                             {
0017360:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd53bd049);
0017361:                                 u32 high_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017362:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91400129);
0017363:                                 u32 low_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017364:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000129);
0017365:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017366:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017367:                                     .offset = high_offset,
0017368:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_TLSLE_ADD_TPREL_HI12,
0017369:                                 };
0017370:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017371:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017372:                                     .offset = low_offset,
0017373:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_TLSLE_ADD_TPREL_LO12,
0017374:                                 };
0017375:                             }
0017376:                         }
0017377:                         else
0017378:                         {
0017379:                             if (target.os == OPERATING_SYSTEM_MACOS || target.os == OPERATING_SYSTEM_IOS)
0017380:                             {
0017381:                                 u32 high_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017382:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x90000009);
0017383:                                 u32 low_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017384:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000129);
0017385:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017386:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017387:                                     .offset = high_offset,
0017388:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_MACH_PAGE21,
0017389:                                 };
0017390:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017391:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017392:                                     .offset = low_offset,
0017393:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_MACH_PAGEOFF12,
0017394:                                 };
0017395:                             }
0017396:                             else
0017397:                             {
0017398:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x58000049);
0017399:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000003);
0017400:                                 u32 offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017401:                                 codegen_emit_u64(&buffer, 0);
0017402:                                 result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0017403:                                     .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0017404:                                     .offset = offset,
0017405:                                     .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_ABSOLUTE64,
0017406:                                 };
0017407:                             }
0017408:                         }
0017409:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset, 8, true, false))
0017410:                         {
0017411:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0017412:                             return result;
0017413:                         }
0017414:                     }
0017415:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOAD || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_LOAD)
0017416:                     {
0017417:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
0017418:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0017419:                         u32 aggregate_parts = 0;
0017420:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &aggregate_parts);
0017421:                         IrType* loaded_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0017422:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0017423:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
0017424:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
0017425:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_LOAD && aggregate)
0017426:                         {
0017427:                             // The access width is the place's promoted size (#731),
0017428:                             // not the loaded value's: three bytes are read through
0017429:                             // four. ldar zero-extends into the W form for every width
0017430:                             // below eight, so the eightbyte slot store leaves the
0017431:                             // value's padding zero rather than stack residue.
0017432:                             IrType* atomic_place_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, place->canonical_type);
0017433:                             u64 atomic_width = atomic_place_type && atomic_place_type->layout.resolved ? atomic_place_type->layout.size : 0;
0017434:                             bool aggregate_kind = loaded_type && (loaded_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || loaded_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION);
0017435:                             bool pair_kind =
0017436:                                 loaded_type && (aggregate_kind || (loaded_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && loaded_type->bit_width == 128));
0017437:                             if (atomic_width == 16 && pair_kind)
0017438:                             {
0017439:                                 // A sixteen-byte atomic load is the exclusive pair
0017440:                                 // loop — the load alone is not single-copy atomic,
0017441:                                 // so the pair writes itself back and retries until
0017442:                                 // the store-exclusive proves the read was whole.
0017443:                                 // Sequential consistency gives that successful
0017444:                                 // readback the release half through STLXP.
0017445:                                 // Both halves land in the slot, so a narrower
0017446:                                 // value's promoted padding reads back exactly what
0017447:                                 // the store side wrote.
0017448:                                 if (indirect)
0017449:                                 {
0017450:                                     c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017451:                                 }
0017452:                                 else
0017453:                                 {
0017454:                                     codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0017455:                                 }
0017456:                                 u32 pair_retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017457:                                 a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load_pair(&buffer, 9, 14, 10, instruction->memory_order != IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELAXED);
0017458:                                 a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store_pair(&buffer, 13, 9, 14, 10,
0017459:                                                                      instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL);
0017460:                                 a64_emit_exclusive_retry(&buffer, pair_retry_offset);
0017461:                                 c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017462:                                 c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 14, result_offset);
0017463:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017464:                                 continue;
0017465:                             }
0017466:                             if (!aggregate_kind || (atomic_width != 1 && atomic_width != 2 && atomic_width != 4 && atomic_width != 8))
0017467:                             {
0017468:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017469:                                 return result;
0017470:                             }
0017471:                             if (indirect)
0017472:                             {
0017473:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017474:                             }
0017475:                             else
0017476:                             {
0017477:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0017478:                             }
0017479:                             a64_emit_atomic_pointer(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)atomic_width, false);
0017480:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017481:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017482:                             continue;
0017483:                         }
0017484:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_LOAD && instruction->memory_order != IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELAXED)
0017485:                         {
0017486:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved ||
0017487:                                 (loaded_type->layout.size != 1 && loaded_type->layout.size != 2 && loaded_type->layout.size != 4 &&
0017488:                                  loaded_type->layout.size != 8))
0017489:                             {
0017490:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017491:                                 return result;
0017492:                             }
0017493:                             if (indirect)
0017494:                             {
0017495:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017496:                             }
0017497:                             else
0017498:                             {
0017499:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0017500:                             }
0017501:                             a64_emit_atomic_pointer(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)loaded_type->layout.size, false);
0017502:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017503:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017504:                             continue;
0017505:                         }
0017506:                         if (aggregate)
0017507:                         {
0017508:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved)
0017509:                             {
0017510:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0017511:                                 return result;
0017512:                             }
0017513:                             if (indirect)
0017514:                             {
0017515:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017516:                             }
0017517:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < aggregate_parts; part_index += 1)
0017518:                             {
0017519:                                 u32 part_offset = part_index * 8;
0017520:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, loaded_type->layout.size - part_offset);
0017521:                                 u64 part_copied = 0;
0017522:                                 while (part_copied < part_size)
0017523:                                 {
0017524:                                     u32 copy_offset = part_offset + (u32)part_copied;
0017525:                                     u64 remaining = part_size - part_copied;
0017526:                                     u32 chunk = codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(
0017527:                                         remaining, indirect ? copy_offset : value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset,
0017528:                                         result_offset + copy_offset);
0017529:                                     if (indirect)
0017530:                                     {
0017531:                                         a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 10, copy_offset, chunk);
0017532:                                     }
0017533:                                     else
0017534:                                     {
0017535:                                         a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
0017536:                                     }
0017537:                                     a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, result_offset + copy_offset, chunk);
0017538:                                     part_copied += chunk;
0017539:                                 }
0017540:                             }
0017541:                         }
0017542:                         else if (indirect)
0017543:                         {
0017544:                             if (!loaded_type || !loaded_type->layout.resolved ||
0017545:                                 (loaded_type->layout.size != 1 && loaded_type->layout.size != 2 && loaded_type->layout.size != 4 &&
0017546:                                  loaded_type->layout.size != 8))
0017547:                             {
0017548:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017549:                                 return result;
0017550:                             }
0017551:                             a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], 8);
0017552:                             a64_emit_load_pointer(&buffer, 9, 9, (u32)loaded_type->layout.size);
0017553:                         }
0017554:                         else
0017555:                         {
0017556:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0017557:                         }
0017558:                         if (!aggregate)
0017559:                         {
0017560:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017561:                         }
0017562:                     }
0017563:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX)
0017564:                     {
0017565:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
0017566:                         IrInstructionId base_definition_id = function->values[base.value].definition;
0017567:                         IrInstruction* base_definition = base_definition_id.value < function->instruction_count
0017568:                                                              ? function->instructions + base_definition_id.value
0017569:                                                              : 0;
0017570:                         IrType* base_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
0017571:                         u32 base_offset = value_offsets[base.value];
0017572:                         if ((base_definition && base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL) ||
0017573:                             (function->values[base.value].category == IR_VALUE_VALUE && base_type &&
0017574:                              (base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_ARRAY || base_type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)))
0017575:                         {
0017576:                             if (base_definition && base_definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
0017577:                             {
0017578:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
0017579:                             }
0017580:                             else
0017581:                             {
0017582:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, base_offset);
0017583:                             }
0017584:                         }
0017585:                         else
0017586:                         {
0017587:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
0017588:                         }
0017589:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
0017590:                         IrType* index_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
0017591:                         IrType* element = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0017592:                         if (!index_type || index_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER || !element || !element->layout.resolved)
0017593:                         {
0017594:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0017595:                             return result;
0017596:                         }
0017597:                         if (index_type->is_signed && index_type->bit_width < 64)
0017598:                         {
0017599:                             u32 sign_extend = index_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d4a
0017600:                                               : index_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d4a
0017601:                                               : index_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d4a
0017602:                                                                             : 0;
0017603:                             if (!sign_extend)
0017604:                             {
0017605:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017606:                                 return result;
0017607:                             }
0017608:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
0017609:                         }
0017610:                         // The stride is an arbitrary 64-bit constant, not a movz
0017611:                         // immediate: an element of 64 KiB or more needs the movk
0017612:                         // continuation, and used to be rejected outright.
0017613:                         a64_emit_constant_compact(&buffer, 11, element->layout.size);
0017614:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9b0b7d4a);
0017615:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
0017616:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017617:                     }
0017618:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ADDRESS_OF)
0017619:                     {
0017620:                         IrValueId object = instruction->operands[0];
0017621:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[object.value].definition.value;
0017622:                         u32 object_offset = value_offsets[object.value];
0017623:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
0017624:                         {
0017625:                             if (function->values[object.value].alignment > 16)
0017626:                             {
0017627:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, object);
0017628:                             }
0017629:                             else
0017630:                             {
0017631:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, object_offset);
0017632:                             }
0017633:                         }
0017634:                         else
0017635:                         {
0017636:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, object);
0017637:                         }
0017638:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017639:                     }
0017640:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE)
0017641:                     {
0017642:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0017643:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017644:                     }
0017645:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD)
0017646:                     {
0017647:                         IrValueId base = instruction->operands[0];
0017648:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[base.value].definition.value;
0017649:                         u32 base_offset = value_offsets[base.value];
0017650:                         if (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL)
0017651:                         {
0017652:                             if (function->values[base.value].alignment > 16)
0017653:                             {
0017654:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
0017655:                             }
0017656:                             else
0017657:                             {
0017658:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, base_offset);
0017659:                             }
0017660:                         }
0017661:                         else
0017662:                         {
0017663:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, base);
0017664:                         }
0017665:                         IrType* aggregate = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[base.value].canonical_type);
0017666:                         u64 field_index = instruction->immediates[0];
0017667:                         if (!aggregate || field_index >= aggregate->field_count)
0017668:                         {
0017669:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0017670:                             return result;
0017671:                         }
0017672:                         u64 field_offset = aggregate->fields[field_index].offset;
0017673:                         if (field_offset <= A64_IMM12_MAX)
0017674:                         {
0017675:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000129 | ((u32)field_offset << 10));
0017676:                         }
0017677:                         else
0017678:                         {
0017679:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, field_offset);
0017680:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
0017681:                         }
0017682:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017683:                     }
0017684:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CAST)
0017685:                     {
0017686:                         IrType* source_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0017687:                         IrType* target_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0017688:                         IrConversionOperation conversion = instruction->conversion_operation;
0017689:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0017690:                         if (!target_type || !source_type)
0017691:                         {
0017692:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0017693:                             return result;
0017694:                         }
0017695:                         bool source_integer128 = source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->bit_width == 128;
0017696:                         bool target_integer128 = target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->bit_width == 128;
0017697:                         if (target_integer128 && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
0017698:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
0017699:                         {
0017700:                             bool converted = codegen_canonical_a64_float_to_i128(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], result_offset,
0017701:                                                                                   source_type->bit_width,
0017702:                                                                                   conversion == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER);
0017703:                             if (!converted)
0017704:                             {
0017705:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0017706:                                 return result;
0017707:                             }
0017708:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017709:                             continue;
0017710:                         }
0017711:                         if (target_integer128)
0017712:                         {
0017713:                             if (source_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
0017714:                             {
0017715:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017716:                                 return result;
0017717:                             }
0017718:                             // x9 already holds the source's low eightbyte. A
0017719:                             // narrower source is widened in place first, then
0017720:                             // the second eightbyte is its sign or zero fill;
0017721:                             // a 128-bit source carries its own high half
0017722:                             // through unchanged.
0017723:                             if (source_integer128)
0017724:                             {
0017725:                                 c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017726:                             }
0017727:                             else if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND)
0017728:                             {
0017729:                                 u32 narrow_sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d29
0017730:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29
0017731:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d29
0017732:                                                                                         : 0;
0017733:                                 if (narrow_sign_extend)
0017734:                                 {
0017735:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, narrow_sign_extend);
0017736:                                 }
0017737:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x937ffd2a); // asr x10, x9, #63
0017738:                             }
0017739:                             else
0017740:                             {
0017741:                                 u32 narrow_zero_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x53001d29
0017742:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x53003d29
0017743:                                                          : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x2a0903e9
0017744:                                                                                         : 0;
0017745:                                 if (narrow_zero_extend)
0017746:                                 {
0017747:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, narrow_zero_extend);
0017748:                                 }
0017749:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa1f03ea); // mov x10, xzr
0017750:                             }
0017751:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017752:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
0017753:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017754:                             continue;
0017755:                         }
0017756:                         if (source_integer128 && target_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0017757:                             !(target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
0017758:                               (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT)))
0017759:                         {
0017760:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017761:                             return result;
0017762:                         }
0017763:                         if (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_SIGN_EXTEND && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
0017764:                         {
0017765:                             u32 sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8    ? 0x93401d29
0017766:                                               : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29
0017767:                                               : source_type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x93407d29
0017768:                                                                              : 0;
0017769:                             if (sign_extend)
0017770:                             {
0017771:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
0017772:                             }
0017773:                         }
0017774:                         else if ((conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_TRUNCATE ||
0017775:                                   conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET) &&
0017776:                                  source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER)
0017777:                         {
0017778:                             u32 effective_bit_width = conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_ZERO_EXTEND ? source_type->bit_width
0017779:                                                       : conversion == IR_CONVERSION_INTEGER_REINTERPRET && source_type->bit_width < target_type->bit_width
0017780:                                                           ? source_type->bit_width
0017781:                                                           : target_type->bit_width;
0017782:                             u32 zero_extend = effective_bit_width == 8    ? 0x53001d29
0017783:                                               : effective_bit_width == 16 ? 0x53003d29
0017784:                                               : effective_bit_width == 32 ? 0x2a0903e9
0017785:                                                                           : 0;
0017786:                             if (zero_extend)
0017787:                             {
0017788:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, zero_extend);
0017789:                             }
0017790:                         }
0017791:                         if (source_integer128 && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
0017792:                             (conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT || conversion == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
0017793:                         {
0017794:                             bool converted = codegen_canonical_a64_i128_to_float(&buffer, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value], result_offset,
0017795:                                                                                  target_type->bit_width,
0017796:                                                                                  conversion == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT);
0017797:                             if (!converted)
0017798:                             {
0017799:                                 result.error = buffer.error;
0017800:                                 return result;
0017801:                             }
0017802:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017803:                             continue;
0017804:                         }
0017805:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0017806:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ||
0017807:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_UNSIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT))
0017808:                         {
0017809:                             if (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT && source_type->bit_width < 64)
0017810:                             {
0017811:                                 u32 sign_extend = source_type->bit_width == 8 ? 0x93401d29 : source_type->bit_width == 16 ? 0x93403d29 : 0x93407d29;
0017812:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, sign_extend);
0017813:                             }
0017814:                             u32 encoded = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_SIGNED_INTEGER_TO_FLOAT ? 0x9e220000 : 0x9e230000;
0017815:                             if (target_type->bit_width == 64)
0017816:                             {
0017817:                                 encoded |= 0x00400000;
0017818:                             }
0017819:                             else if (target_type->bit_width != 32)
0017820:                             {
0017821:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017822:                                 return result;
0017823:                             }
0017824:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded | (9 << 5));
0017825:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, (u32)target_type->layout.size, true);
0017826:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017827:                             continue;
0017828:                         }
0017829:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
0017830:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND ||
0017831:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TRUNCATE))
0017832:                         {
0017833:                             bool extend = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_EXTEND;
0017834:                             if (source_type->float_format != IR_FLOAT_FORMAT_IEEE || target_type->float_format != IR_FLOAT_FORMAT_IEEE ||
0017835:                                 source_type->bit_width != (extend ? 32 : 64) || target_type->bit_width != (extend ? 64 : 32))
0017836:                             {
0017837:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017838:                                 return result;
0017839:                             }
0017840:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value],
0017841:                                                                                (u32)source_type->layout.size, false);
0017842:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, extend ? 0x1e22c000 : 0x1e624000);
0017843:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, (u32)target_type->layout.size, true);
0017844:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017845:                             continue;
0017846:                         }
0017847:                         if (target_type && source_type && target_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && source_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT &&
0017848:                             (instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ||
0017849:                              instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_UNSIGNED_INTEGER))
0017850:                         {
0017851:                             if (source_type->float_format != IR_FLOAT_FORMAT_IEEE ||
0017852:                                 (source_type->bit_width != 32 && source_type->bit_width != 64))
0017853:                             {
0017854:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017855:                                 return result;
0017856:                             }
0017857:                             codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value],
0017858:                                                                                (u32)source_type->layout.size, false);
0017859:                             u32 encoded = instruction->conversion_operation == IR_CONVERSION_FLOAT_TO_SIGNED_INTEGER ? 0x9e380000 : 0x9e390000;
0017860:                             if (source_type->bit_width == 64)
0017861:                             {
0017862:                                 encoded |= 0x00400000;
0017863:                             }
0017864:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded | 9);
0017865:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017866:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017867:                             continue;
0017868:                         }
0017869:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0017870:                     }
0017871:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_STORE || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE)
0017872:                     {
0017873:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
0017874:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0017875:                         u32 aggregate_parts = 0;
0017876:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type,
0017877:                                                                                    &aggregate_parts);
0017878:                         IrType* stored_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[1].value].canonical_type);
0017879:                         if (!aggregate && stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved && stored_type->layout.size > 8 &&
0017880:                             stored_type->layout.size <= (u64)UINT32_MAX * 8)
0017881:                         {
0017882:                             aggregate = true;
0017883:                             aggregate_parts = (u32)((stored_type->layout.size + 7) / 8);
0017884:                         }
0017885:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0017886:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
0017887:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
0017888:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && aggregate)
0017889:                         {
0017890:                             // See the atomic load above for the width. x10 carries the
0017891:                             // mask constant before it carries the address, so the
0017892:                             // padding is cleared first.
0017893:                             IrType* atomic_place_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, place->canonical_type);
0017894:                             u64 atomic_width = atomic_place_type && atomic_place_type->layout.resolved ? atomic_place_type->layout.size : 0;
0017895:                             u64 atomic_stored_size = stored_type && stored_type->layout.resolved ? stored_type->layout.size : 0;
0017896:                             bool aggregate_kind = stored_type && (stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION);
0017897:                             bool pair_kind =
0017898:                                 stored_type && (aggregate_kind || (stored_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && stored_type->bit_width == 128));
0017899:                             if (atomic_width == 16 && pair_kind && atomic_stored_size > 8)
0017900:                             {
0017901:                                 // The sixteen-byte store loop: the value pair
0017902:                                 // stages before the loop with its promoted
0017903:                                 // padding cleared, the discarded exclusive load
0017904:                                 // arms the monitor (acquire-formed only when
0017905:                                 // sequential, clang's answer), and the pair
0017906:                                 // store retries until it lands whole.
0017907:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
0017908:                                 c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
0017909:                                 a64_emit_keep_low_bytes(&buffer, 12, 13, atomic_stored_size - 8);
0017910:                                 if (indirect)
0017911:                                 {
0017912:                                     c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017913:                                 }
0017914:                                 else
0017915:                                 {
0017916:                                     codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0017917:                                 }
0017918:                                 bool pair_release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE ||
0017919:                                                     instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
0017920:                                                     instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0017921:                                 u32 pair_retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0017922:                                 a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load_pair(&buffer, 9, 14, 10, instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL);
0017923:                                 a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store_pair(&buffer, 13, 11, 12, 10, pair_release);
0017924:                                 a64_emit_exclusive_retry(&buffer, pair_retry_offset);
0017925:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017926:                                 continue;
0017927:                             }
0017928:                             if (!aggregate_kind || (atomic_width != 1 && atomic_width != 2 && atomic_width != 4 && atomic_width != 8))
0017929:                             {
0017930:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017931:                                 return result;
0017932:                             }
0017933:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[1]);
0017934:                             a64_emit_keep_low_bytes(&buffer, 9, 10, atomic_stored_size);
0017935:                             if (indirect)
0017936:                             {
0017937:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017938:                             }
0017939:                             else
0017940:                             {
0017941:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0017942:                             }
0017943:                             a64_emit_atomic_pointer(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)atomic_width, true);
0017944:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017945:                             continue;
0017946:                         }
0017947:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_STORE && instruction->memory_order != IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELAXED)
0017948:                         {
