0017949:                             if (!stored_type || !stored_type->layout.resolved ||
0017950:                                 (stored_type->layout.size != 1 && stored_type->layout.size != 2 && stored_type->layout.size != 4 &&
0017951:                                  stored_type->layout.size != 8))
0017952:                             {
0017953:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0017954:                                 return result;
0017955:                             }
0017956:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[1]);
0017957:                             if (indirect)
0017958:                             {
0017959:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017960:                             }
0017961:                             else
0017962:                             {
0017963:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0017964:                             }
0017965:                             a64_emit_atomic_pointer(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)stored_type->layout.size, true);
0017966:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0017967:                             continue;
0017968:                         }
0017969:                         if (aggregate)
0017970:                         {
0017971:                             if (!stored_type || !stored_type->layout.resolved)
0017972:                             {
0017973:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0017974:                                 return result;
0017975:                             }
0017976:                             if (indirect)
0017977:                             {
0017978:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0017979:                             }
0017980:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < aggregate_parts; part_index += 1)
0017981:                             {
0017982:                                 u32 part_offset = part_index * 8;
0017983:                                 u64 part_size = BUSTER_MIN((u64)8, stored_type->layout.size - part_offset);
0017984:                                 u64 part_copied = 0;
0017985:                                 while (part_copied < part_size)
0017986:                                 {
0017987:                                     u64 remaining = part_size - part_copied;
0017988:                                     u32 copy_offset = part_offset + (u32)part_copied;
0017989:                                     u32 chunk =
0017990:                                         codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(remaining, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value] + copy_offset, copy_offset);
0017991:                                     a64_emit_load_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
0017992:                                     if (indirect)
0017993:                                     {
0017994:                                         a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 10, copy_offset, chunk);
0017995:                                     }
0017996:                                     else
0017997:                                     {
0017998:                                         a64_emit_store_pointer_offset(&buffer, 9, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value] + copy_offset, chunk);
0017999:                                     }
0018000:                                     part_copied += chunk;
0018001:                                 }
0018002:                             }
0018003:                         }
0018004:                         else if (indirect)
0018005:                         {
0018006:                             if (!stored_type || !stored_type->layout.resolved ||
0018007:                                 (stored_type->layout.size != 1 && stored_type->layout.size != 2 && stored_type->layout.size != 4 &&
0018008:                                  stored_type->layout.size != 8))
0018009:                             {
0018010:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0018011:                                 return result;
0018012:                             }
0018013:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
0018014:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0018015:                             a64_emit_store_pointer(&buffer, 10, 9, (u32)stored_type->layout.size);
0018016:                         }
0018017:                         else
0018018:                         {
0018019:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[1]);
0018020:                             u32 place_offset = value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value];
0018021:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, place_offset, 8, true, false))
0018022:                             {
0018023:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018024:                                 return result;
0018025:                             }
0018026:                         }
0018027:                     }
0018028:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_READ_MODIFY_WRITE)
0018029:                     {
0018030:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
0018031:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0018032:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018033:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0018034:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
0018035:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
0018036:                         bool pointer_arithmetic = value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER &&
0018037:                                                   (instruction->atomic_operation == IR_ATOMIC_ADD || instruction->atomic_operation == IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT);
0018038:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->bit_width == 128 &&
0018039:                             value_type->layout.size == 16 && instruction->atomic_operation < IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT)
0018040:                         {
0018041:                             // The sixteen-byte read-modify-write: an exclusive
0018042:                             // pair loop whose operand pair reloads from its
0018043:                             // frame slot each retry, because the arithmetic
0018044:                             // writes the operand registers and a store inside
0018045:                             // the window could clear the monitor while a load
0018046:                             // cannot. The carry rides ADDS/ADC and SUBS/SBC;
0018047:                             // the old value survives in x9:x14 for the result.
0018048:                             if (indirect)
0018049:                             {
0018050:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0018051:                             }
0018052:                             else
0018053:                             {
0018054:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0018055:                             }
0018056:                             bool pair_acquire = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME ||
0018057:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
0018058:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
0018059:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018060:                             bool pair_release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE ||
0018061:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
0018062:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018063:                             u32 pair_retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018064:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
0018065:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
0018066:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load_pair(&buffer, 9, 14, 10, pair_acquire);
0018067:                             switch (instruction->atomic_operation)
0018068:                             {
0018069:                             case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
0018070:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xab0b012b));
0018071:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x9a0c01cc));
0018072:                                 break;
0018073:                             case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
0018074:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xeb0b012b));
0018075:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xda0c01cc));
0018076:                                 break;
0018077:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
0018078:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x8a0b012b));
0018079:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x8a0c01cc));
0018080:                                 break;
0018081:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
0018082:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xaa0b012b));
0018083:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xaa0c01cc));
0018084:                                 break;
0018085:                             case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
0018086:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xca0b012b));
0018087:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xca0c01cc));
0018088:                                 break;
0018089:                             case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
0018090:                                 break;
0018091:                             case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
0018092:                                 break;
0018093:                             }
0018094:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store_pair(&buffer, 13, 11, 12, 10, pair_release);
0018095:                             a64_emit_exclusive_retry(&buffer, pair_retry_offset);
0018096:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018097:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 14, result_offset);
0018098:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018099:                             continue;
0018100:                         }
0018101:                         if (!value_type ||
0018102:                             (!pointer_arithmetic && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0018103:                              (instruction->atomic_operation != IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE ||
0018104:                               (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER))) ||
0018105:                             !value_type->layout.resolved ||
0018106:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8) ||
0018107:                             instruction->atomic_operation >= IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT)
0018108:                         {
0018109:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0018110:                             return result;
0018111:                         }
0018112:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
0018113:                         if (indirect)
0018114:                         {
0018115:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0018116:                         }
0018117:                         else
0018118:                         {
0018119:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0018120:                         }
0018121:                         bool acquire = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
0018122:                                        instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018123:                         bool release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
0018124:                                        instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018125:                         u32 retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018126:                         a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)value_type->layout.size, acquire);
0018127:                         u32 operation = 0;
0018128:                         bool wide = value_type->layout.size == 8;
0018129:                         switch (instruction->atomic_operation)
0018130:                         {
0018131:                         case IR_ATOMIC_ADD:
0018132:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0x8b0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x0b0b012c);
0018133:                             break;
0018134:                         case IR_ATOMIC_SUBTRACT:
0018135:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xcb0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x4b0b012c);
0018136:                             break;
0018137:                         case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_AND:
0018138:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0x8a0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x0a0b012c);
0018139:                             break;
0018140:                         case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_OR:
0018141:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xaa0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x2a0b012c);
0018142:                             break;
0018143:                         case IR_ATOMIC_BITWISE_XOR:
0018144:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xca0b012c) : UINT32_C(0x4a0b012c);
0018145:                             break;
0018146:                         case IR_ATOMIC_EXCHANGE:
0018147:                             operation = wide ? UINT32_C(0xaa0b03ec) : UINT32_C(0x2a0b03ec);
0018148:                             break;
0018149:                         case IR_ATOMIC_OPERATION_COUNT:
0018150:                             break;
0018151:                         }
0018152:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, operation);
0018153:                         a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store(&buffer, 13, 12, 10, (u32)value_type->layout.size, release);
0018154:                         s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry_offset - (s64)buffer.count;
0018155:                         if (retry_displacement % 4 || retry_displacement / 4 < -INT64_C(0x40000) || retry_displacement / 4 > INT64_C(0x3ffff))
0018156:                         {
0018157:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018158:                             return result;
0018159:                         }
0018160:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x35000000) | (((u32)(retry_displacement / 4) & UINT32_C(0x7ffff)) << 5) | 13);
0018161:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018162:                     }
0018163:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_COMPARE_EXCHANGE)
0018164:                     {
0018165:                         IrValue* place = function->values + instruction->operands[0].value;
0018166:                         IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + place->definition.value;
0018167:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018168:                         bool indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0018169:                                         definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE ||
0018170:                                         (definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LOCAL && place->alignment > 16);
0018171:                         if (value_type && value_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->layout.resolved && value_type->bit_width == 128 &&
0018172:                             value_type->layout.size == 16)
0018173:                         {
0018174:                             // The sixteen-byte compare-exchange: the expected
0018175:                             // and desired pairs reload before each exclusive
0018176:                             // window, which contains no ordinary memory access.
0018177:                             // both halves compare through CCMP, and CSEL
0018178:                             // chooses the observed pair on mismatch. LDXP is
0018179:                             // not itself a single-copy atomic read: even a
0018180:                             // mismatch must complete STXP before returning.
0018181:                             // The loop returns the old value pair like every
0018182:                             // other atomic — the frontend owns the success
0018183:                             // comparison and the expected write-back.
0018184:                             if (indirect)
0018185:                             {
0018186:                                 c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0018187:                             }
0018188:                             else
0018189:                             {
0018190:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0018191:                             }
0018192:                             bool pair_acquire =
0018193:                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
0018194:                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL ||
0018195:                                 instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME ||
0018196:                                 instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
0018197:                                 instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018198:                             bool pair_release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE ||
0018199:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
0018200:                                                 instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018201:                             u32 pair_retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018202:                             // Stage every frame read before the exclusive window.
0018203:                             // X16 remains address scratch; X15/X17 retain desired.
0018204:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
0018205:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
0018206:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 15, instruction->operands[2]);
0018207:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 17, instruction->operands[2]);
0018208:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load_pair(&buffer, 9, 14, 10, pair_acquire);
0018209:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xeb0b013f));
0018210:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xfa4c01c0));
0018211:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x9a8901eb)); // csel x11, x15, x9, eq
0018212:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x9a8e022c)); // csel x12, x17, x14, eq
0018213:                             a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store_pair(&buffer, 13, 11, 12, 10, pair_release);
0018214:                             a64_emit_exclusive_retry(&buffer, pair_retry_offset);
0018215:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033f5f));
0018216:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018217:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 14, result_offset);
0018218:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018219:                             continue;
0018220:                         }
0018221:                         if (!value_type || (value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER) || !value_type->layout.resolved ||
0018222:                             (value_type->layout.size != 1 && value_type->layout.size != 2 && value_type->layout.size != 4 && value_type->layout.size != 8))
0018223:                         {
0018224:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0018225:                             return result;
0018226:                         }
0018227:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
0018228:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[2]);
0018229:                         if (indirect)
0018230:                         {
0018231:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0018232:                         }
0018233:                         else
0018234:                         {
0018235:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 10, 28, value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value]);
0018236:                         }
0018237:                         bool acquire =
0018238:                             instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
0018239:                             instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL ||
0018240:                             instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_CONSUME || instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE ||
0018241:                             instruction->failure_memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018242:                         bool release = instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELEASE || instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_ACQUIRE_RELEASE ||
0018243:                                        instruction->memory_order == IR_MEMORY_ORDER_SEQUENTIAL;
0018244:                         u32 retry_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018245:                         a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_load(&buffer, 9, 10, (u32)value_type->layout.size, acquire);
0018246:                         bool wide = value_type->layout.size == 8;
0018247:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, wide ? UINT32_C(0xeb0c013f) : UINT32_C(0x6b0c013f));
0018248:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x54000061));
0018249:                         a64_emit_atomic_exclusive_store(&buffer, 13, 11, 10, (u32)value_type->layout.size, release);
0018250:                         s64 retry_displacement = (s64)retry_offset - (s64)buffer.count;
0018251:                         if (retry_displacement % 4 || retry_displacement / 4 < -INT64_C(0x40000) || retry_displacement / 4 > INT64_C(0x3ffff))
0018252:                         {
0018253:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018254:                             return result;
0018255:                         }
0018256:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x35000000) | (((u32)(retry_displacement / 4) & UINT32_C(0x7ffff)) << 5) | 13);
0018257:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033f5f));
0018258:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018259:                     }
0018260:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ATOMIC_FENCE)
0018261:                     {
0018262:                         if (!instruction->atomic_signal_fence && instruction->memory_order != IR_MEMORY_ORDER_RELAXED)
0018263:                         {
0018264:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033bbf));
0018265:                         }
0018266:                     }
0018267:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CLEAR_INSTRUCTION_CACHE)
0018268:                     {
0018269:                         if (instruction->operand_count != 2)
0018270:                         {
0018271:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0018272:                             return result;
0018273:                         }
0018274:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0018275:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
0018276:                         // Visit every four-byte granule intersecting the
0018277:                         // range, including a final line after an unaligned begin.
0018278:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x927ef52b));
0018279:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xaa0b03e9));
0018280:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xeb0a013f));
0018281:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x54000082));
0018282:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd50b7b29));
0018283:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x91001129));
0018284:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x17fffffc));
0018285:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033b9f));
0018286:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xaa0b03e9));
0018287:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xeb0a013f));
0018288:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x54000082));
0018289:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd50b7529));
0018290:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x91001129));
0018291:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x17fffffc));
0018292:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033b9f));
0018293:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0xd5033fdf));
0018294:                     }
0018295:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER || instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_FLOAT)
0018296:                     {
0018297:                         u64 immediate = instruction->immediates[0];
0018298:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && instruction->immediate_is_negative)
0018299:                         {
0018300:                             immediate = 0 - immediate;
0018301:                         }
0018302:                         bool wide = codegen_canonical_register_is_64_bit(program, instruction->canonical_type);
0018303:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, (wide ? 0xd2800009 : 0x52800009) | ((u32)(immediate & 0xffff) << 5));
0018304:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, (wide ? 0xf2a00009 : 0x72a00009) | ((u32)((immediate >> 16) & 0xffff) << 5));
0018305:                         if (wide)
0018306:                         {
0018307:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2c00009 | ((u32)((immediate >> 32) & 0xffff) << 5));
0018308:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2e00009 | ((u32)((immediate >> 48) & 0xffff) << 5));
0018309:                         }
0018310:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018311:                         IrType* constant_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018312:                         if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CONSTANT_INTEGER && constant_type && constant_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER &&
0018313:                             constant_type->bit_width == 128)
0018314:                         {
0018315:                             // The second eightbyte of a 128-bit constant, the
0018316:                             // same selection the x86-64 canonical emitter
0018317:                             // makes: an explicit second immediate when the
0018318:                             // frontend recorded one, otherwise the negated
0018319:                             // low half's sign fill.
0018320:                             u64 immediate_high = instruction->immediate_count > 1 ? instruction->immediates[1]
0018321:                                                  : instruction->immediate_is_negative ? UINT64_MAX
0018322:                                                                                       : 0;
0018323:                             a64_emit_constant_compact(&buffer, 9, immediate_high);
0018324:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018325:                         }
0018326:                     }
0018327:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_START)
0018328:                     {
0018329:                         if (!canonical_variadic)
0018330:                         {
0018331:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0018332:                             return result;
0018333:                         }
0018334:                         u32 gp_count = 0;
0018335:                         u32 fp_count = 0;
0018336:                         u32 stack_parts = 0;
0018337:                         for (u32 parameter_index = 0; parameter_index < canonical_function_type->parameter_count; parameter_index += 1)
0018338:                         {
0018339:                             IrTypeId parameter_type = canonical_function_type->parameter_types[parameter_index];
0018340:                             IrType* parameter = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, parameter_type);
0018341:                             IrAbiValue parameter_abi = ir_type_abi_value(program, parameter_type, codegen_canonical_ir_abi_convention(result.abi), IR_ABI_USE_ARGUMENT);
0018342:                             if (!aarch64_windows_variadic && parameter_abi.part_count && codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(parameter_abi.parts[0].abi_class))
0018343:                             {
0018344:                                 if (fp_count + parameter_abi.part_count <= 8)
0018345:                                 {
0018346:                                     fp_count += parameter_abi.part_count;
0018347:                                 }
0018348:                                 else
0018349:                                 {
0018350:                                     fp_count = 8;
0018351:                                     stack_parts = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(stack_parts, parameter->layout.alignment > 8);
0018352:                                     stack_parts += (u32)((parameter->layout.size + 7) / 8);
0018353:                                 }
0018354:                                 continue;
0018355:                             }
0018356:                             u32 part_count = 1;
0018357:                             bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, parameter_type, &part_count);
0018358:                             bool parameter_indirect = aggregate && parameter && parameter->layout.size > 16;
0018359:                             if (parameter_indirect)
0018360:                             {
0018361:                                 part_count = 1;
0018362:                             }
0018363:                             bool parameter_even_integer_pair =
0018364:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_even_integer_pair(program, parameter_type, result.abi, false);
0018365:                             if (parameter_even_integer_pair && gp_count < 8)
0018366:                             {
0018367:                                 gp_count = (gp_count + 1u) & ~1u;
0018368:                             }
0018369:                             if (gp_count + part_count <= 8)
0018370:                             {
0018371:                                 gp_count += part_count;
0018372:                             }
0018373:                             else
0018374:                             {
0018375:                                 // AAPCS64 C.12: a spilled composite closes the
0018376:                                 // integer file; the overflow area starts after
0018377:                                 // every stack part this walk counts.
0018378:                                 if (aggregate && !parameter_indirect)
0018379:                                 {
0018380:                                     gp_count = 8;
0018381:                                 }
0018382:                                 stack_parts = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(stack_parts, parameter_even_integer_pair);
0018383:                                 stack_parts += part_count;
0018384:                             }
0018385:                         }
0018386:                         if (aarch64_darwin)
0018387:                         {
0018388:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 29, 16 + stack_parts * 8);
0018389:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset, 8, true, false))
0018390:                             {
0018391:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018392:                                 return result;
0018393:                             }
0018394:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018395:                             continue;
0018396:                         }
0018397:                         if (aarch64_windows_variadic)
0018398:                         {
0018399:                             if (gp_count < 8)
0018400:                             {
0018401:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, aarch64_va_save_offset + gp_count * 8);
0018402:                             }
0018403:                             else
0018404:                             {
0018405:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 29, 16 + stack_parts * 8);
0018406:                             }
0018407:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset, 8, true, false))
0018408:                             {
0018409:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018410:                                 return result;
0018411:                             }
0018412:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018413:                             continue;
0018414:                         }
0018415:                         codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 29, 16 + stack_parts * 8);
0018416:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + MACHINE_A64_VA_STACK_OFFSET, 8, true, false))
0018417:                         {
0018418:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018419:                             return result;
0018420:                         }
0018421:                         codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, aarch64_va_save_offset + MACHINE_A64_VA_GP_SAVE_BYTES);
0018422:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + MACHINE_A64_VA_GR_TOP_OFFSET, 8, true, false))
0018423:                         {
0018424:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018425:                             return result;
0018426:                         }
0018427:                         codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, aarch64_va_save_offset + MACHINE_A64_VA_SAVE_BYTES);
0018428:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + MACHINE_A64_VA_VR_TOP_OFFSET, 8, true, false))
0018429:                         {
0018430:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018431:                             return result;
0018432:                         }
0018433:                         s32 gp_offset = (s32)gp_count * 8 - (s32)MACHINE_A64_VA_GP_SAVE_BYTES;
0018434:                         s32 fp_offset = (s32)fp_count * 16 - (s32)MACHINE_A64_VA_FP_SAVE_BYTES;
0018435:                         a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 9, (u64)(u32)gp_offset | ((u64)(u32)fp_offset << 32));
0018436:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + MACHINE_A64_VA_GR_OFFS_OFFSET, 8, true, false))
0018437:                         {
0018438:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018439:                             return result;
0018440:                         }
0018441:                     }
0018442:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_COPY)
0018443:                     {
0018444:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0018445:                         if (aarch64_darwin || aarch64_windows_variadic)
0018446:                         {
0018447:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf9400149);
0018448:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset, 8, true, false))
0018449:                             {
0018450:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018451:                                 return result;
0018452:                             }
0018453:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018454:                             continue;
0018455:                         }
0018456:                         for (u32 component = 0; component < 4; component += 1)
0018457:                         {
0018458:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf9400149 | (component << 10));
0018459:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + component * 8, 8, true, false))
0018460:                             {
0018461:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018462:                                 return result;
0018463:                             }
0018464:                         }
0018465:                     }
0018466:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_END)
0018467:                     {
0018468:                         // Both cursor images are ordinary frame data; no runtime cleanup.
0018469:                     }
0018470:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_VA_ARG)
0018471:                     {
0018472:                         IrType* value_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018473:                         u32 part_count = 1;
0018474:                         bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &part_count);
0018475:                         IrAbiValue va_abi = ir_type_abi_value(program, instruction->canonical_type, codegen_canonical_ir_abi_convention(result.abi), IR_ABI_USE_VARIADIC_ARGUMENT);
0018476:                         bool indirect = aggregate && va_abi.indirect;
0018477:                         if (!value_type || !value_type->layout.size || value_type->layout.size > UINT32_MAX - 7u ||
0018478:                             ((aarch64_darwin || aarch64_windows_variadic) && value_type->layout.size > 16) ||
0018479:                             (!aarch64_darwin && !aarch64_windows_variadic && (va_abi.memory || (!aggregate && value_type->layout.size > 8))) ||
0018480:                             (!aggregate && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_INTEGER && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN && value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_POINTER &&
0018481:                              value_type->kind != IR_TYPE_FLOAT))
0018482:                         {
0018483:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0018484:                             return result;
0018485:                         }
0018486:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0018487:                         bool even_integer_pair =
0018488:                             ir_abi_value_is_aarch64_even_integer_pair(program, instruction->canonical_type,
0018489:                                                                       codegen_canonical_ir_abi_convention(result.abi),
0018490:                                                                       IR_ABI_USE_VARIADIC_ARGUMENT);
0018491:                         if (aarch64_darwin)
0018492:                         {
0018493:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf940014b);
0018494:                             if (even_integer_pair)
0018495:                             {
0018496:                                 // AAPCS64 rounds an overflow-area cursor to
0018497:                                 // the value's sixteen-byte alignment before
0018498:                                 // consuming a variadic INTEGER pair.
0018499:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91003d6b);
0018500:                                 a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 9, ~UINT64_C(15));
0018501:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a09016b);
0018502:                             }
0018503:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0018504:                             {
0018505:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, part_index * 8, 8, false, false, 11) ||
0018506:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + part_index * 8, 8, true, false))
0018507:                                 {
0018508:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018509:                                     return result;
0018510:                                 }
0018511:                             }
0018512:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 9, part_count * 8);
0018513:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b09016b);
0018514:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf900014b);
0018515:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018516:                             continue;
0018517:                         }
0018518:                         if (aarch64_windows_variadic)
0018519:                         {
0018520:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf940014b);
0018521:                             if (even_integer_pair)
0018522:                             {
0018523:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91003d6b);
0018524:                                 a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 9, ~UINT64_C(15));
0018525:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a09016b);
0018526:                             }
0018527:                             // Our ordinary frame keeps FP/LR between the
0018528:                             // saved X image and the caller's overflow area.
0018529:                             // Translate the one-past-save cursor to incoming
0018530:                             // SP before and after each read, preserving the
0018531:                             // public one-pointer Windows va_list shape.
0018532:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 12, 28, aarch64_va_save_offset + 64);
0018533:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 13, 29, 16);
0018534:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xeb0c017f);
0018535:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8b01abu);
0018536:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0018537:                             {
0018538:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, part_index * 8, 8, false, false, 11) ||
0018539:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, result_offset + part_index * 8, 8, true, false))
0018540:                                 {
0018541:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018542:                                     return result;
0018543:                                 }
0018544:                             }
0018545:                             u32 increment = part_count * 8;
0018546:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9100016b | (increment << 10));
0018547:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xeb0c017f);
0018548:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8b01abu);
0018549:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf900014b);
0018550:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018551:                             continue;
0018552:                         }
0018553:                         bool floating = va_abi.part_count && codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(va_abi.parts[0].abi_class);
0018554:                         if (floating || indirect) { part_count = va_abi.part_count; }
0018555:                         bool stack_aligned = !indirect && value_type->layout.alignment > 8;
0018556:                         u32 cursor_offset = floating ? MACHINE_A64_VA_VR_OFFS_OFFSET : MACHINE_A64_VA_GR_OFFS_OFFSET;
0018557:                         codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 11, cursor_offset, 4, false, true, 10);
0018558:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf100017f); // cmp x11, #0
0018559:                         u32 empty_patch = (u32)buffer.count;
0018560:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x5400000a); // b.ge: already exhausted
0018561:                         if (even_integer_pair)
0018562:                         {
0018563:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91003d6b);
0018564:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 9, ~UINT64_C(15));
0018565:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a09016b);
0018566:                         }
0018567:                         u32 increment = part_count * (floating ? 16u : 8u);
0018568:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000169 | (increment << 10)); // add x9, x11, #increment
0018569:                         codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, cursor_offset, 4, true, false, 10);
0018570:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf100013f); // cmp x9, #0
0018571:                         u32 overflow_patch = (u32)buffer.count;
0018572:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x5400000c); // b.gt: this value does not fit
0018573:                         codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 12,
0018574:                             floating ? MACHINE_A64_VA_VR_TOP_OFFSET : MACHINE_A64_VA_GR_TOP_OFFSET, 8, false, false, 10);
0018575:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0b018c);
0018576:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_va_value(&buffer, &va_abi, (u32)value_type->layout.size, part_count, result_offset, true))
0018577:                         {
0018578:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018579:                             return result;
0018580:                         }
0018581:                         u32 end_patch = (u32)buffer.count;
0018582:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0018583:                         u32 overflow_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018584:                         codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 12, MACHINE_A64_VA_STACK_OFFSET, 8, false, false, 10);
0018585:                         if (stack_aligned)
0018586:                         {
0018587:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91003d8c);
0018588:                             a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 9, ~UINT64_C(15));
0018589:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a09018c);
0018590:                         }
0018591:                         u32 stack_increment = indirect ? 8u : (u32)((value_type->layout.size + 7) & ~(u64)7);
0018592:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x91000189 | (stack_increment << 10));
0018593:                         codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, 9, MACHINE_A64_VA_STACK_OFFSET, 8, true, false, 10);
0018594:                         if (!codegen_canonical_a64_va_value(&buffer, &va_abi, (u32)value_type->layout.size, part_count, result_offset, false))
0018595:                         {
0018596:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018597:                             return result;
0018598:                         }
0018599:                         u32 end_offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018600:                         u32 empty = 0x5400000a | (((overflow_offset - empty_patch) / 4) << 5);
0018601:                         memcpy(buffer.bytes + empty_patch, &empty, sizeof(empty));
0018602:                         u32 conditional = 0x5400000c | (((overflow_offset - overflow_patch) / 4) << 5);
0018603:                         memcpy(buffer.bytes + overflow_patch, &conditional, sizeof(conditional));
0018604:                         u32 branch = 0x14000000 | ((end_offset - end_patch) / 4);
0018605:                         memcpy(buffer.bytes + end_patch, &branch, sizeof(branch));
0018606:                     }
0018607:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_CALL)
0018608:                     {
0018609:                         u32 argument_count = instruction->operand_count - 1;
0018610:                         IrType* callee_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0018611:                         IrType* callee_function_type = callee_type && callee_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER
0018612:                                                            ? ir_type_from_id(&program->types, callee_type->element_type)
0018613:                                                            : callee_type;
0018614:                         bool darwin_variadic_call = result.abi == CODEGEN_ABI_AARCH64_DARWIN && callee_function_type &&
0018615:                                                     callee_function_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FUNCTION && callee_function_type->is_variadic;
0018616:                         bool windows_variadic_call = windows_aarch64 && callee_function_type &&
0018617:                                                      callee_function_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FUNCTION && callee_function_type->is_variadic;
0018618:                         bool* argument_on_stack = arena_allocate(arena, bool, argument_count);
0018619:                         u32* argument_stack_offset = arena_allocate(arena, u32, argument_count);
0018620:                         bool* argument_indirect = arena_allocate(arena, bool, argument_count);
0018621:                         u8* argument_float_register = arena_allocate(arena, u8, argument_count);
0018622:                         memset(argument_on_stack, 0, sizeof(*argument_on_stack) * argument_count);
0018623:                         memset(argument_indirect, 0, sizeof(*argument_indirect) * argument_count);
0018624:                         memset(argument_float_register, UINT8_MAX, sizeof(*argument_float_register) * argument_count);
0018625:                         u32 simulated_registers = 0;
0018626:                         u32 simulated_float_registers = 0;
0018627:                         u32 stack_part_count = 0;
0018628:                         for (u32 argument_array_index = 0; argument_array_index < argument_count; argument_array_index += 1)
0018629:                         {
0018630:                             IrValueId argument = instruction->operands[argument_array_index + 1];
0018631:                             IrTypeId type_id = function->values[argument.value].canonical_type;
0018632:                             IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, type_id);
0018633:                             u32 part_count = 1;
0018634:                             bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, type_id, &part_count);
0018635:                             CodegenCanonicalAbiValue argument_abi = codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, type_id, result.abi, false, false);
0018636:                             bool argument_hfa = argument_abi.part_count != 0;
0018637:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0018638:                             {
0018639:                                 argument_hfa &= codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(argument_abi.parts[part].abi_class);
0018640:                             }
0018641:                             argument_hfa &= !windows_variadic_call;
0018642:                             if (!type || ((type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION) && !aggregate))
0018643:                             {
0018644:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0018645:                                 return result;
0018646:                             }
0018647:                             bool unnamed_variadic = darwin_variadic_call && argument_array_index >= callee_function_type->parameter_count;
0018648:                             if (type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && !windows_variadic_call)
0018649:                             {
0018650:                                 if (!unnamed_variadic && simulated_float_registers < 8)
0018651:                                 {
0018652:                                     argument_float_register[argument_array_index] = (u8)simulated_float_registers++;
0018653:                                 }
0018654:                                 else
0018655:                                 {
0018656:                                     argument_on_stack[argument_array_index] = true;
0018657:                                     argument_stack_offset[argument_array_index] = stack_part_count;
0018658:                                     stack_part_count += 1;
0018659:                                 }
0018660:                                 continue;
0018661:                             }
0018662:                             if (argument_hfa)
0018663:                             {
0018664:                                 if (!unnamed_variadic && simulated_float_registers + argument_abi.part_count <= 8)
0018665:                                 {
0018666:                                     argument_float_register[argument_array_index] = (u8)simulated_float_registers;
0018667:                                     simulated_float_registers += argument_abi.part_count;
0018668:                                 }
0018669:                                 else
0018670:                                 {
0018671:                                     if (!unnamed_variadic)
0018672:                                     {
0018673:                                         simulated_float_registers = 8;
0018674:                                     }
0018675:                                     argument_on_stack[argument_array_index] = true;
0018676:                                     stack_part_count = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(stack_part_count, type->layout.alignment > 8);
0018677:                                     argument_stack_offset[argument_array_index] = stack_part_count;
0018678:                                     stack_part_count += (u32)((type->layout.size + 7) / 8);
0018679:                                 }
0018680:                                 continue;
0018681:                             }
0018682:                             bool indirect = aggregate && type->layout.size > 16;
0018683:                             if (indirect)
0018684:                             {
0018685:                                 part_count = 1;
0018686:                                 argument_indirect[argument_array_index] = true;
0018687:                             }
0018688:                             bool even_integer_pair = codegen_canonical_a64_even_integer_pair(program, type_id, result.abi, false);
0018689:                             if (even_integer_pair && !unnamed_variadic && simulated_registers < 8)
0018690:                             {
0018691:                                 simulated_registers = (simulated_registers + 1u) & ~1u;
0018692:                             }
0018693:                             if (!unnamed_variadic && simulated_registers + part_count <= 8)
0018694:                             {
0018695:                                 simulated_registers += part_count;
0018696:                             }
0018697:                             else
0018698:                             {
0018699:                                 // AAPCS64 C.12: a composite that does not fit
0018700:                                 // the remaining integer registers closes the
0018701:                                 // file (NGRN becomes 8) as it goes to the
0018702:                                 // stack, so later scalars may not take the
0018703:                                 // registers it skipped. A scalar only lands
0018704:                                 // here with the file already full.
0018705:                                 if (!unnamed_variadic && aggregate && !indirect)
0018706:                                 {
0018707:                                     simulated_registers = 8;
0018708:                                 }
0018709:                                 argument_on_stack[argument_array_index] = true;
0018710:                                 stack_part_count = codegen_canonical_a64_align_stack_pair(stack_part_count, even_integer_pair);
0018711:                                 argument_stack_offset[argument_array_index] = stack_part_count;
0018712:                                 stack_part_count += part_count;
0018713:                             }
0018714:                         }
0018715:                         u32 stack_size = (stack_part_count * 8 + 15) & ~(u32)15;
0018716:                         if (stack_size)
0018717:                         {
0018718:                             codegen_canonical_a64_adjust_stack(&buffer, stack_size, true);
0018719:                         }
0018720:                         for (u32 argument_array_index = 0; argument_array_index < argument_count; argument_array_index += 1)
0018721:                         {
0018722:                             if (!argument_on_stack[argument_array_index])
0018723:                             {
0018724:                                 continue;
0018725:                             }
0018726:                             IrValueId argument = instruction->operands[argument_array_index + 1];
0018727:                             if (argument_indirect[argument_array_index])
0018728:                             {
0018729:                                 u32 source_offset = value_offsets[argument.value];
0018730:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 9, 28, source_offset);
0018731:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, argument_stack_offset[argument_array_index] * 8, 8, true, false))
0018732:                                 {
0018733:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018734:                                     return result;
0018735:                                 }
0018736:                                 continue;
0018737:                             }
0018738:                             u32 part_count = 1;
0018739:                             codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[argument.value].canonical_type, &part_count);
0018740:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0018741:                             {
0018742:                                 u32 source_offset = value_offsets[argument.value] + part_index * 8;
0018743:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, source_offset, 8, false, false) ||
0018744:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, (argument_stack_offset[argument_array_index] + part_index) * 8, 8, true,
0018745:                                                                             false))
0018746:                                 {
0018747:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018748:                                     return result;
0018749:                                 }
0018750:                             }
0018751:                         }
0018752:                         u32 register_index = 0;
0018753:                         for (u32 argument_index = 1; argument_index < instruction->operand_count; argument_index += 1)
0018754:                         {
0018755:                             IrValueId argument = instruction->operands[argument_index];
0018756:                             IrTypeId argument_type_id = function->values[argument.value].canonical_type;
0018757:                             IrType* argument_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, argument_type_id);
0018758:                             u32 part_count = 1;
0018759:                             bool aggregate = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, argument_type_id, &part_count);
0018760:                             CodegenCanonicalAbiValue argument_abi = codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, argument_type_id, result.abi, false, false);
0018761:                             bool argument_hfa = argument_abi.part_count != 0;
0018762:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0018763:                             {
0018764:                                 argument_hfa &= codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(argument_abi.parts[part].abi_class);
0018765:                             }
0018766:                             argument_hfa &= !windows_variadic_call;
0018767:                             bool indirect = aggregate && argument_type && argument_type->layout.size > 16;
0018768:                             if (indirect)
0018769:                             {
0018770:                                 part_count = 1;
0018771:                             }
0018772:                             bool even_integer_pair = codegen_canonical_a64_even_integer_pair(program, argument_type_id, result.abi, false);
0018773:                             if (argument_on_stack[argument_index - 1])
0018774:                             {
0018775:                                 if (aggregate && !indirect && !argument_hfa && argument_type && argument_type->kind != IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0018776:                                 {
0018777:                                     // A stack composite consumes the remaining
0018778:                                     // X file even though no register move is
0018779:                                     // emitted in this pass.
0018780:                                     register_index = 8;
0018781:                                 }
0018782:                                 continue;
0018783:                             }
0018784:                             if (even_integer_pair && register_index < 8)
0018785:                             {
0018786:                                 register_index = (register_index + 1u) & ~1u;
0018787:                             }
0018788:                             // The integer-file bound only exempts arguments
0018789:                             // that really ride the V file. Windows variadic
0018790:                             // calls carry float and HFA bits in X registers.
0018791:                             bool vector_register_argument =
0018792:                                 !windows_variadic_call && ((argument_type && argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT) || argument_hfa);
0018793:                             if (!argument_type || ((argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_STRUCT || argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_UNION) && !aggregate) ||
0018794:                                 (!vector_register_argument && register_index + part_count > 8))
0018795:                             {
0018796:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0018797:                                 return result;
0018798:                             }
0018799:                             if (argument_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT && !windows_variadic_call)
0018800:                             {
0018801:                                 u8 float_register = argument_float_register[argument_index - 1];
0018802:                                 if (float_register >= 8 || (argument_type->bit_width != 32 && argument_type->bit_width != 64))
0018803:                                 {
0018804:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0018805:                                     return result;
0018806:                                 }
0018807:                                 u32 source_offset = value_offsets[argument.value];
0018808:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, float_register, source_offset, argument_type->bit_width / 8,
0018809:                                                                                         false))
0018810:                                 {
0018811:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018812:                                     return result;
0018813:                                 }
0018814:                                 continue;
0018815:                             }
0018816:                             if (argument_hfa)
0018817:                             {
0018818:                                 u32 float_register = argument_float_register[argument_index - 1];
0018819:                                 for (u32 part = 0; part < argument_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0018820:                                 {
0018821:                                     CodegenCanonicalAbiPart* abi_part = argument_abi.parts + part;
0018822:                                     u32 source_offset = value_offsets[argument.value] + abi_part->value_offset;
0018823:                                     if (float_register + part >= 8)
0018824:                                     {
0018825:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0018826:                                         return result;
0018827:                                     }
0018828:                                     codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, float_register + part, source_offset, abi_part->size, false);
0018829:                                 }
0018830:                                 continue;
0018831:                             }
0018832:                             for (u32 part_index = 0; part_index < part_count; part_index += 1)
0018833:                             {
0018834:                                 u32 source_offset = value_offsets[argument.value] + part_index * 8;
0018835:                                 if (indirect)
0018836:                                 {
0018837:                                     codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, register_index, 28, source_offset);
0018838:                                 }
0018839:                                 else
0018840:                                 {
0018841:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, register_index, source_offset, 8, false, false))
0018842:                                     {
0018843:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018844:                                         return result;
0018845:                                     }
0018846:                                 }
0018847:                                 register_index += 1;
0018848:                             }
0018849:                         }
0018850:                         CodegenCanonicalAbiValue call_return_abi =
0018851:                             codegen_canonical_aggregate_abi(program, instruction->canonical_type, result.abi, true, false);
0018852:                         bool call_indirect_return = call_return_abi.indirect;
0018853:                         if (call_indirect_return)
0018854:                         {
0018855:                             u32 return_offset = result_offset;
0018856:                             codegen_canonical_a64_base_address(&buffer, 8, 28, return_offset);
0018857:                         }
0018858:                         bool indirect_call = callee_type && callee_type->kind == IR_TYPE_POINTER;
0018859:                         if (indirect_call)
0018860:                         {
0018861:                             u32 callee_offset = value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value];
0018862:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 16, callee_offset, 8, false, false))
0018863:                             {
0018864:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018865:                                 return result;
0018866:                             }
0018867:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd63f0200);
0018868:                         }
0018869:                         else
0018870:                         {
0018871:                             u32 offset = (u32)buffer.count;
0018872:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x94000000);
0018873:                             result.relocations[result.relocation_count++] = (CodegenModuleRelocation){
0018874:                                 .symbol = instruction->symbol,
0018875:                                 .offset = offset,
0018876:                                 .kind = CODEGEN_MODULE_RELOCATION_AARCH64_CALL26,
0018877:                             };
0018878:                         }
0018879:                         if (stack_size)
0018880:                         {
0018881:                             codegen_canonical_a64_adjust_stack(&buffer, stack_size, false);
0018882:                         }
0018883:                         if (instruction->result.value != IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID)
0018884:                         {
0018885:                             IrType* return_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018886:                             u32 widened_lane_count = 0;
0018887:                             u32 widened_element_bits = 0;
0018888:                             if (codegen_canonical_a64_widened_vector_result(program, return_type, &widened_lane_count, &widened_element_bits))
0018889:                             {
0018890:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, UINT32_C(0x9e660009)); // fmov x9, d0
0018891:                                 codegen_canonical_a64_pack_vector_result(&buffer, widened_lane_count, widened_element_bits, true);
0018892:                                 c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0018893:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018894:                                 continue;
0018895:                             }
0018896:                             if (return_type && return_type->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0018897:                             {
0018898:                                 if (return_type->bit_width != 32 && return_type->bit_width != 64)
0018899:                                 {
0018900:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_ABI;
0018901:                                     return result;
0018902:                                 }
0018903:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, return_type->bit_width / 8, true))
0018904:                                 {
0018905:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018906:                                     return result;
0018907:                                 }
0018908:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018909:                                 continue;
0018910:                             }
0018911:                             bool return_hfa = call_return_abi.part_count != 0;
0018912:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < call_return_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0018913:                             {
0018914:                                 return_hfa &= codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(call_return_abi.parts[part].abi_class);
0018915:                             }
0018916:                             if (return_hfa)
0018917:                             {
0018918:                                 for (u32 part = 0; part < call_return_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0018919:                                 {
0018920:                                     codegen_canonical_a64_frame_float_memory_operation(&buffer, part, result_offset + call_return_abi.parts[part].value_offset,
0018921:                                                                                        call_return_abi.parts[part].size, true);
0018922:                                 }
0018923:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0018924:                                 continue;
0018925:                             }
0018926:                             u32 return_parts = 0;
0018927:                             bool aggregate_return = codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, instruction->canonical_type, &return_parts);
0018928:                             if (!call_indirect_return)
0018929:                             {
0018930:                                 c_a64_store(&emitter, 0, result_offset);
0018931:                             }
0018932:                             if (!call_indirect_return && aggregate_return && return_parts == 2)
0018933:                             {
0018934:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 1, result_offset + 8, 8, true, false))
0018935:                                 {
0018936:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018937:                                     return result;
0018938:                                 }
0018939:                             }
0018940:                         }
0018941:                     }
0018942:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_ARRAY)
0018943:                     {
0018944:                         IrType* array = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018945:                         IrType* element = array ? ir_type_from_id(&program->types, array->element_type) : 0;
0018946:                         if (!array || !element || (array->kind != IR_TYPE_ARRAY && array->kind != IR_TYPE_VECTOR) ||
0018947:                             instruction->operand_count != array->element_count)
0018948:                         {
0018949:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0018950:                             return result;
0018951:                         }
0018952:                         for (u32 element_index = 0; element_index < instruction->operand_count; element_index += 1)
0018953:                         {
0018954:                             u64 copied = 0;
0018955:                             while (copied < element->layout.size)
0018956:                             {
0018957:                                 u64 remaining = element->layout.size - copied;
0018958:                                 u64 source_offset = (u64)value_offsets[instruction->operands[element_index].value] + copied;
0018959:                                 u64 destination_offset = (u64)value_offsets[instruction->result.value] + element_index * element->layout.size + copied;
0018960:                                 u32 chunk = codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(remaining, source_offset, destination_offset);
0018961:                                 if (source_offset > UINT32_MAX || destination_offset > UINT32_MAX ||
0018962:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, (u32)source_offset, chunk, false, false) ||
0018963:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, (u32)destination_offset, chunk, true, false))
0018964:                                 {
0018965:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018966:                                     return result;
0018967:                                 }
0018968:                                 copied += chunk;
0018969:                             }
0018970:                         }
0018971:                     }
