0018972:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_AGGREGATE)
0018973:                     {
0018974:                         IrType* aggregate = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0018975:                         if (!aggregate || instruction->operand_count != instruction->immediate_count)
0018976:                         {
0018977:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0018978:                             return result;
0018979:                         }
0018980:                         u64 aggregate_copied = 0;
0018981:                         while (aggregate_copied < aggregate->layout.size)
0018982:                         {
0018983:                             u64 remaining = aggregate->layout.size - aggregate_copied;
0018984:                             u64 destination_offset = (u64)result_offset + aggregate_copied;
0018985:                             u32 chunk = codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(remaining, destination_offset, destination_offset);
0018986:                             if (destination_offset > UINT32_MAX ||
0018987:                                 !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 31, (u32)destination_offset, chunk, true, false))
0018988:                             {
0018989:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0018990:                                 return result;
0018991:                             }
0018992:                             aggregate_copied += chunk;
0018993:                         }
0018994:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0018995:                         {
0018996:                             u64 field_index = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0018997:                             if (field_index >= aggregate->field_count)
0018998:                             {
0018999:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019000:                                 return result;
0019001:                             }
0019002:                             IrField* field = aggregate->fields + field_index;
0019003:                             IrType* field_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, field->type);
0019004:                             if (!field_type || !field_type->layout.resolved)
0019005:                             {
0019006:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019007:                                 return result;
0019008:                             }
0019009:                             u32 field_offset = result_offset + (u32)field->offset;
0019010:                             if (field->is_bit_field)
0019011:                             {
0019012:                                 if (!field->bit_width)
0019013:                                 {
0019014:                                     continue;
0019015:                                 }
0019016:                                 // The storage the field is written through,
0019017:                                 // which packing may have narrowed below the
0019018:                                 // declared type or left as a span no single
0019019:                                 // access covers; `field->offset` names its
0019020:                                 // first byte and the pieces walk it. One piece
0019021:                                 // is the ordinary case, and the rows it emits
0019022:                                 // are the rows a single unit always emitted.
0019023:                                 u32 field_size = (u32)ir_field_access_size(&program->types, field);
0019024:                                 IrFieldAccessPiece field_pieces[IR_FIELD_ACCESS_PIECE_CAPACITY];
0019025:                                 u32 field_piece_count = ir_field_access_pieces(field_size, field_pieces);
0019026:                                 if (!field_piece_count || field->bit_width > 64)
0019027:                                 {
0019028:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019029:                                     return result;
0019030:                                 }
0019031:                                 for (u32 piece_index = 0; piece_index < field_piece_count; piece_index += 1)
0019032:                                 {
0019033:                                     u32 piece_low = (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].offset * 8;
0019034:                                     u32 piece_high = piece_low + (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].size * 8;
0019035:                                     u32 low = BUSTER_MAX(piece_low, field->bit_offset);
0019036:                                     u32 high = BUSTER_MIN(piece_high, field->bit_offset + field->bit_width);
0019037:                                     if (low >= high)
0019038:                                     {
0019039:                                         continue;
0019040:                                     }
0019041:                                     u32 piece_offset = field_offset + (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].offset;
0019042:                                     c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[operand_index]);
0019043:                                     if (low > field->bit_offset)
0019044:                                     {
0019045:                                         // LSR is UBFM with the shift as the
0019046:                                         // rotate and 63 as the width.
0019047:                                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd3400000 | ((low - field->bit_offset) << 16) | (63 << 10) | (9 << 5) | 9);
0019048:                                     }
0019049:                                     if (high - low < 64)
0019050:                                     {
0019051:                                         a64_emit_constant(&buffer, 10, ((u64)1 << (high - low)) - 1);
0019052:                                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a0a0129);
0019053:                                     }
0019054:                                     if (low > piece_low)
0019055:                                     {
0019056:                                         u32 shift = low - piece_low;
0019057:                                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd3400000 | ((64 - shift) << 16) | ((63 - shift) << 10) | (9 << 5) | 9);
0019058:                                     }
0019059:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 10, piece_offset, (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].size,
0019060:                                                                                       false, false))
0019061:                                     {
0019062:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019063:                                         return result;
0019064:                                     }
0019065:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa09014a);
0019066:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 10, piece_offset, (u32)field_pieces[piece_index].size,
0019067:                                                                                       true, false))
0019068:                                     {
0019069:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019070:                                         return result;
0019071:                                     }
0019072:                                 }
0019073:                                 continue;
0019074:                             }
0019075:                             u64 field_copied = 0;
0019076:                             while (field_copied < field_type->layout.size)
0019077:                             {
0019078:                                 u64 remaining = field_type->layout.size - field_copied;
0019079:                                 u64 source_offset = (u64)value_offsets[instruction->operands[operand_index].value] + field_copied;
0019080:                                 u64 destination_offset = (u64)field_offset + field_copied;
0019081:                                 u32 chunk = codegen_canonical_copy_chunk(remaining, source_offset, destination_offset);
0019082:                                 if (source_offset > UINT32_MAX || destination_offset > UINT32_MAX ||
0019083:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, (u32)source_offset, chunk, false, false) ||
0019084:                                     !codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, 9, (u32)destination_offset, chunk, true, false))
0019085:                                 {
0019086:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019087:                                     return result;
0019088:                                 }
0019089:                                 field_copied += chunk;
0019090:                             }
0019091:                         }
0019092:                     }
0019093:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_UNARY)
0019094:                     {
0019095:                         IrType* canonical_unary_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0019096:                         if (canonical_unary_type && canonical_unary_type->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)
0019097:                         {
0019098:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_vector_operation(&buffer, program, function, instruction, value_offsets))
0019099:                             {
0019100:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019101:                                 return result;
0019102:                             }
0019103:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019104:                             continue;
0019105:                         }
0019106:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0019107:                         IrType* type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, instruction->canonical_type);
0019108:                         if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_BOOLEAN_NOT)
0019109:                         {
0019110:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x7100013f);
0019111:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x1a9f17e9);
0019112:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019113:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019114:                             continue;
0019115:                         }
0019116:                         if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_FLOAT_NEGATE)
0019117:                         {
0019118:                             if (!type || type->kind != IR_TYPE_FLOAT || (type->bit_width != 32 && type->bit_width != 64))
0019119:                             {
0019120:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019121:                                 return result;
0019122:                             }
0019123:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x52b0000a : 0xd2f0000a);
0019124:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, type->bit_width == 32 ? 0x4a0a0129 : 0xca0a0129);
0019125:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019126:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019127:                             continue;
0019128:                         }
0019129:                         if (type && type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && type->bit_width == 128)
0019130:                         {
0019131:                             // Pair forms over x9:x10; the 64-bit rows below
0019132:                             // would drop the high half. CLZ/CTZ select the
0019133:                             // non-zero half and add the crossed-word offset.
0019134:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0019135:                             if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_NEGATE)
0019136:                             {
0019137:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xeb0903e9); // negs x9, x9
0019138:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xda0a03ea); // ngc x10, x10
0019139:                             }
0019140:                             else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_NOT)
0019141:                             {
0019142:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa2903e9); // mvn x9, x9
0019143:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa2a03ea); // mvn x10, x10
0019144:                             }
0019145:                             else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_LEADING_ZEROS)
0019146:                             {
0019147:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xdac0114d); // clz x13, x10
0019148:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xdac0112e); // clz x14, x9
0019149:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x910101ce); // add x14, x14, #64
0019150:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf100015f); // cmp x10, #0
0019151:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8e11a9); // csel x9, x13, x14, ne
0019152:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa1f03ea); // mov x10, xzr
0019153:                                 c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019154:                                 c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
0019155:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019156:                                 continue;
0019157:                             }
0019158:                             else if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_TRAILING_ZEROS)
0019159:                             {
0019160:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xdac0012d); // rbit x13, x9
0019161:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xdac011ad); // clz x13, x13
0019162:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xdac0014e); // rbit x14, x10
0019163:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xdac011ce); // clz x14, x14
0019164:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x910101ce); // add x14, x14, #64
0019165:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf100013f); // cmp x9, #0
0019166:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8e11a9); // csel x9, x13, x14, ne
0019167:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa1f03ea); // mov x10, xzr
0019168:                                 c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019169:                                 c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
0019170:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019171:                                 continue;
0019172:                             }
0019173:                             else
0019174:                             {
0019175:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019176:                                 return result;
0019177:                             }
0019178:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019179:                             c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
0019180:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019181:                             continue;
0019182:                         }
0019183:                         u32 operation =
0019184:                             instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_NEGATE                 ? (type && type->bit_width > 32 ? 0xcb0903e9 : 0x4b0903e9)
0019185:                             : instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_NOT          ? (type && type->bit_width > 32 ? 0xaa2903e9 : 0x2a2903e9)
0019186:                             : instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_LEADING_ZEROS  ? (type && type->bit_width > 32 ? 0xdac01129 : 0x5ac01129)
0019187:                             : instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_TRAILING_ZEROS ? (type && type->bit_width > 32 ? 0xdac00129 : 0x5ac00129)
0019188:                                                                                                     : 0;
0019189:                         if (!operation)
0019190:                         {
0019191:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019192:                             return result;
0019193:                         }
0019194:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, operation);
0019195:                         if (instruction->unary_operation == IR_UNARY_INTEGER_COUNT_TRAILING_ZEROS)
0019196:                         {
0019197:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, type && type->bit_width > 32 ? 0xdac01129 : 0x5ac01129);
0019198:                         }
0019199:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019200:                     }
0019201:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_BINARY)
0019202:                     {
0019203:                         IrTypeId operand_type = function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type;
0019204:                         IrType* operand_type_value = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, operand_type);
0019205:                         if (operand_type_value && operand_type_value->kind == IR_TYPE_VECTOR)
0019206:                         {
0019207:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_vector_operation(&buffer, program, function, instruction, value_offsets))
0019208:                             {
0019209:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019210:                                 return result;
0019211:                             }
0019212:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019213:                             continue;
0019214:                         }
0019215:                         if (operand_type_value && operand_type_value->kind == IR_TYPE_FLOAT)
0019216:                         {
0019217:                             u32 width = operand_type_value->bit_width;
0019218:                             if (width != 32 && width != 64)
0019219:                             {
0019220:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019221:                                 return result;
0019222:                             }
0019223:                             u32 left_offset = value_offsets[instruction->operands[0].value];
0019224:                             u32 right_offset = value_offsets[instruction->operands[1].value];
0019225:                             u32 float_size = width == 32 ? 4 : 8;
0019226:                             if (!codegen_canonical_a64_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, left_offset, float_size, false) ||
0019227:                                 !codegen_canonical_a64_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 1, right_offset, float_size, false))
0019228:                             {
0019229:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019230:                                 return result;
0019231:                             }
0019232:                             IrBinaryOperation operation = instruction->binary_operation;
0019233:                             if (operation >= IR_BINARY_FLOAT_ADD && operation <= IR_BINARY_FLOAT_DIVIDE)
0019234:                             {
0019235:                                 u32 encoded = operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_ADD        ? 0x1e212800
0019236:                                               : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_SUBTRACT ? 0x1e213800
0019237:                                               : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_MULTIPLY ? 0x1e210800
0019238:                                                                                       : 0x1e211800;
0019239:                                 if (width == 64)
0019240:                                 {
0019241:                                     encoded |= 0x00400000;
0019242:                                 }
0019243:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, encoded);
0019244:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_float_memory_operation(&buffer, 0, result_offset, float_size, true))
0019245:                                 {
0019246:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019247:                                     return result;
0019248:                                 }
0019249:                                 instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019250:                                 continue;
0019251:                             }
0019252:                             u32 condition = operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_EQUAL           ? 0
0019253:                                             : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_NOT_EQUAL     ? 1
0019254:                                             : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_LESS          ? 4
0019255:                                             : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_LESS_EQUAL    ? 9
0019256:                                             : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_GREATER       ? 12
0019257:                                             : operation == IR_BINARY_FLOAT_GREATER_EQUAL ? 10
0019258:                                                                                          : UINT32_MAX;
0019259:                             if (condition == UINT32_MAX)
0019260:                             {
0019261:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019262:                                 return result;
0019263:                             }
0019264:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, width == 32 ? 0x1e212000 : 0x1e612000);
0019265:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x1a9f07e9 | ((condition ^ 1) << 12));
0019266:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019267:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019268:                             continue;
0019269:                         }
0019270:                         if (operand_type_value && operand_type_value->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && operand_type_value->bit_width == 128)
0019271:                         {
0019272:                             // 128-bit integers ride the pair x9:x10 (left) and
0019273:                             // x11:x12 (right), low eightbyte first, with x13
0019274:                             // and x14 as scratch. The variable shifts are
0019275:                             // branchless CSEL composites: the emitter places
0019276:                             // no labels inside one IR row, and LSLV/LSRV/ASRV
0019277:                             // wrap their amount modulo 64 only in lanes the
0019278:                             // CSEL then discards. The `mvn` spelling of
0019279:                             // 63 - amount keeps the amount-zero case exact.
0019280:                             // Shift amounts read only the right operand's low
0019281:                             // eightbyte, because the frontend may keep a
0019282:                             // shift's right operand at its promoted width and
0019283:                             // a narrow value's slot has no second eightbyte.
0019284:                             // Division and remainder use the same pair
0019285:                             // convention and a bounded long-division loop;
0019286:                             // the helper leaves its selected pair in x9:x10.
0019287:                             IrBinaryOperation operation128 = instruction->binary_operation;
0019288:                             bool shift128 = operation128 == IR_BINARY_SHIFT_LEFT || operation128 == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_SHIFT_RIGHT ||
0019289:                                             operation128 == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_SHIFT_RIGHT;
0019290:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0019291:                             c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[0]);
0019292:                             c_a64_load(&emitter, 11, instruction->operands[1]);
0019293:                             if (!shift128)
0019294:                             {
0019295:                                 c_a64_load_high(&emitter, 12, instruction->operands[1]);
0019296:                             }
0019297:                             bool store_pair = true;
0019298:                             switch (operation128)
0019299:                             {
0019300:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_ADD:
0019301:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xab0b0129); // adds x9, x9, x11
0019302:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a0c014a); // adc x10, x10, x12
0019303:                                 break;
0019304:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_SUBTRACT:
0019305:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xeb0b0129); // subs x9, x9, x11
0019306:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xda0c014a); // sbc x10, x10, x12
0019307:                                 break;
0019308:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_MULTIPLY:
0019309:                                 // hi = umulh(lo, lo') + lo * hi' + hi * lo';
0019310:                                 // the truncated product is signedness-blind.
0019311:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9bcb7d2d); // umulh x13, x9, x11
0019312:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9b0c352d); // madd x13, x9, x12, x13
0019313:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9b0b354a); // madd x10, x10, x11, x13
0019314:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9b0b7d29); // mul x9, x9, x11
0019315:                                 break;
0019316:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_AND:
0019317:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a0b0129); // and x9, x9, x11
0019318:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8a0c014a); // and x10, x10, x12
0019319:                                 break;
0019320:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_OR:
0019321:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0b0129); // orr x9, x9, x11
0019322:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0c014a); // orr x10, x10, x12
0019323:                                 break;
0019324:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_XOR:
0019325:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xca0b0129); // eor x9, x9, x11
0019326:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xca0c014a); // eor x10, x10, x12
0019327:                                 break;
0019328:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_DIVIDE:
0019329:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_REMAINDER:
0019330:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_DIVIDE:
0019331:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_REMAINDER:
0019332:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_i128_divide(&buffer,
0019333:                                                                        operation128 == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_DIVIDE || operation128 == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_REMAINDER,
0019334:                                                                        operation128 == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_REMAINDER || operation128 == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_REMAINDER))
0019335:                                 {
0019336:                                     result.error = buffer.error;
0019337:                                     return result;
0019338:                                 }
0019339:                                 break;
0019340:                             case IR_BINARY_SHIFT_LEFT:
0019341:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x2a2b03ed); // mvn w13, w11
0019342:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd341fd2e); // lsr x14, x9, #1
0019343:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acd25ce); // lsrv x14, x14, x13
0019344:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acb214a); // lslv x10, x10, x11
0019345:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0e014a); // orr x10, x10, x14
0019346:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x7101016d); // subs w13, w11, #64
0019347:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acd212e); // lslv x14, x9, x13
0019348:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8aa1ca); // csel x10, x14, x10, ge
0019349:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acb2129); // lslv x9, x9, x11
0019350:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a89a3e9); // csel x9, xzr, x9, ge
0019351:                                 break;
0019352:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_SHIFT_RIGHT:
0019353:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x2a2b03ed); // mvn w13, w11
0019354:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd37ff94e); // lsl x14, x10, #1
0019355:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acd21ce); // lslv x14, x14, x13
0019356:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acb2529); // lsrv x9, x9, x11
0019357:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0e0129); // orr x9, x9, x14
0019358:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x7101016d); // subs w13, w11, #64
0019359:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acd254e); // lsrv x14, x10, x13
0019360:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a89a1c9); // csel x9, x14, x9, ge
0019361:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acb254a); // lsrv x10, x10, x11
0019362:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8aa3ea); // csel x10, xzr, x10, ge
0019363:                                 break;
0019364:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_SHIFT_RIGHT:
0019365:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x2a2b03ed); // mvn w13, w11
0019366:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd37ff94e); // lsl x14, x10, #1
0019367:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acd21ce); // lslv x14, x14, x13
0019368:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acb2529); // lsrv x9, x9, x11
0019369:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0e0129); // orr x9, x9, x14
0019370:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x7101016d); // subs w13, w11, #64
0019371:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acd294e); // asrv x14, x10, x13
0019372:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a89a1c9); // csel x9, x14, x9, ge
0019373:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9acb294e); // asrv x14, x10, x11
0019374:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x937ffd4d); // asr x13, x10, #63
0019375:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x9a8ea1aa); // csel x10, x13, x14, ge
0019376:                                 break;
0019377:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_EQUAL:
0019378:                             case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_NOT_EQUAL:
0019379:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xca0b0129); // eor x9, x9, x11
0019380:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xca0c014a); // eor x10, x10, x12
0019381:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xaa0a0129); // orr x9, x9, x10
0019382:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf100013f); // cmp x9, #0
0019383:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, operation128 == IR_BINARY_INTEGER_EQUAL ? 0x1a9f17e9 : 0x1a9f07e9); // cset w9
0019384:                                 store_pair = false;
0019385:                                 break;
0019386:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS:
0019387:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0019388:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER:
0019389:                             case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0019390:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS:
0019391:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0019392:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER:
0019393:                             case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0019394:                             {
0019395:                                 // cmp low, then sbcs high: N/V carry the
0019396:                                 // signed answer and C the unsigned one, but Z
0019397:                                 // reflects the high subtraction alone, so the
0019398:                                 // Z-consuming conditions are unusable here.
0019399:                                 // LESS_EQUAL and GREATER therefore compare
0019400:                                 // with the operands swapped and answer with
0019401:                                 // the Z-free conditions of the mirrored
0019402:                                 // relation.
0019403:                                 bool swapped128 = operation128 == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER || operation128 == IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS_EQUAL ||
0019404:                                                   operation128 == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER || operation128 == IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS_EQUAL;
0019405:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, swapped128 ? 0xeb09017f : 0xeb0b013f); // cmp x11, x9 / cmp x9, x11
0019406:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, swapped128 ? 0xfa0a019f : 0xfa0c015f); // sbcs xzr, x12, x10 / sbcs xzr, x10, x12
0019407:                                 u32 condition_set;
0019408:                                 switch (operation128)
0019409:                                 {
0019410:                                 case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS:
0019411:                                 case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER:
0019412:                                     condition_set = 0x1a9fa7e9; // cset w9, lt
0019413:                                     break;
0019414:                                 case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0019415:                                 case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0019416:                                     condition_set = 0x1a9fb7e9; // cset w9, ge
0019417:                                     break;
0019418:                                 case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS:
0019419:                                 case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER:
0019420:                                     condition_set = 0x1a9f27e9; // cset w9, lo
0019421:                                     break;
0019422:                                 default:
0019423:                                     condition_set = 0x1a9f37e9; // cset w9, hs
0019424:                                 }
0019425:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, condition_set);
0019426:                                 store_pair = false;
0019427:                             }
0019428:                             break;
0019429:                             default:
0019430:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019431:                                 return result;
0019432:                             }
0019433:                             c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019434:                             if (store_pair)
0019435:                             {
0019436:                                 c_a64_store_high(&emitter, 10, result_offset);
0019437:                             }
0019438:                             instruction_id.value = instruction_id.value == emitted_block->last_instruction.value ? IR_ID_UNDERLYING_INVALID : instruction_id.value + 1;
0019439:                             continue;
0019440:                         }
0019441:                         u32 wide_mask = codegen_canonical_register_is_64_bit(program, operand_type) ? 0x80000000 : 0;
0019442:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0019443:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 10, instruction->operands[1]);
0019444:                         u32 operation = 0;
0019445:                         switch (instruction->binary_operation)
0019446:                         {
0019447:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_ADD:
0019448:                             operation = 0x0b0a0129;
0019449:                             break;
0019450:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_SUBTRACT:
0019451:                             operation = 0x4b0a0129;
0019452:                             break;
0019453:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_MULTIPLY:
0019454:                             operation = 0x1b0a7d29;
0019455:                             break;
0019456:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_DIVIDE:
0019457:                             operation = 0x1aca0d29;
0019458:                             break;
0019459:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_REMAINDER:
0019460:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, codegen_canonical_a64_remainder_divide_instruction(true, wide_mask != 0));
0019461:                             operation = 0x1b0aa569;
0019462:                             break;
0019463:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_DIVIDE:
0019464:                             operation = 0x1aca0929;
0019465:                             break;
0019466:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_REMAINDER:
0019467:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, codegen_canonical_a64_remainder_divide_instruction(false, wide_mask != 0));
0019468:                             operation = 0x1b0aa569;
0019469:                             break;
0019470:                         case IR_BINARY_SHIFT_LEFT:
0019471:                             operation = 0x1aca2129;
0019472:                             break;
0019473:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_SHIFT_RIGHT:
0019474:                             operation = 0x1aca2929;
0019475:                             break;
0019476:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_SHIFT_RIGHT:
0019477:                             operation = 0x1aca2529;
0019478:                             break;
0019479:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_AND:
0019480:                         case IR_BINARY_BOOLEAN_AND:
0019481:                             operation = 0x0a0a0129;
0019482:                             break;
0019483:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_OR:
0019484:                         case IR_BINARY_BOOLEAN_OR:
0019485:                             operation = 0x2a0a0129;
0019486:                             break;
0019487:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_BITWISE_XOR:
0019488:                             operation = 0x4a0a0129;
0019489:                             break;
0019490:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_EQUAL:
0019491:                         case IR_BINARY_POINTER_EQUAL:
0019492:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019493:                             operation = 0x1a9f17e9;
0019494:                             break;
0019495:                         case IR_BINARY_INTEGER_NOT_EQUAL:
0019496:                         case IR_BINARY_POINTER_NOT_EQUAL:
0019497:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019498:                             operation = 0x1a9f07e9;
0019499:                             break;
0019500:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS:
0019501:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019502:                             operation = 0x1a9fa7e9;
0019503:                             break;
0019504:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0019505:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019506:                             operation = 0x1a9fc7e9;
0019507:                             break;
0019508:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER:
0019509:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019510:                             operation = 0x1a9fd7e9;
0019511:                             break;
0019512:                         case IR_BINARY_SIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0019513:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019514:                             operation = 0x1a9fb7e9;
0019515:                             break;
0019516:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS:
0019517:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019518:                             operation = 0x1a9f27e9;
0019519:                             break;
0019520:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_LESS_EQUAL:
0019521:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019522:                             operation = 0x1a9f87e9;
0019523:                             break;
0019524:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER:
0019525:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019526:                             operation = 0x1a9f97e9;
0019527:                             break;
0019528:                         case IR_BINARY_UNSIGNED_GREATER_EQUAL:
0019529:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x6b0a013f | wide_mask);
0019530:                             operation = 0x1a9f37e9;
0019531:                             break;
0019532:                         default:
0019533:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019534:                             return result;
0019535:                         }
0019536:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, operation | wide_mask);
0019537:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019538:                     }
0019539:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_LABEL_ADDRESS)
0019540:                     {
0019541:                         if (instruction->target_count != 1)
0019542:                         {
0019543:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019544:                             return result;
0019545:                         }
0019546:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019547:                             .target = instruction->targets[0],
0019548:                             .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019549:                             .aarch64 = true,
0019550:                             .label_address = true,
0019551:                             }))
0019552:                         {
0019553:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019554:                             return result;
0019555:                         }
0019556:                         emitter.branch_patches[emitter.branch_patch_count - 1].secondary_offset = (u32)buffer.count + 4;
0019557:                         // Materialize the byte delta from this ADR to the
0019558:                         // target block.  Unlike ADRP, this remains correct
0019559:                         // when the text section is placed at a non-page
0019560:                         // aligned address or concatenated after another
0019561:                         // object: both addresses move by the same amount.
0019562:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x10000009);
0019563:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd280000a);
0019564:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2a0000a);
0019565:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2c0000a);
0019566:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2e0000a);
0019567:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x8b0a0129);
0019568:                         c_a64_store(&emitter, 9, result_offset);
0019569:                     }
0019570:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_BRANCH)
0019571:                     {
0019572:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019573:                             .target = instruction->targets[0],
0019574:                             .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019575:                             .aarch64 = true,
0019576:                             }))
0019577:                         {
0019578:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019579:                             return result;
0019580:                         }
0019581:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0019582:                     }
0019583:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDIRECT_BRANCH)
0019584:                     {
0019585:                         if (instruction->operand_count != 1)
0019586:                         {
0019587:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019588:                             return result;

0019590:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0019591:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd61f0120);
0019592:                     }
0019593:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_BRANCH_IF)
0019594:                     {
0019595:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0019596:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf100013f);
0019597:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x54000040);
0019598:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019599:                             .target = instruction->targets[0],
0019600:                             .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019601:                             .aarch64 = true,
0019602:                             }))
0019603:                         {
0019604:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019605:                             return result;
0019606:                         }
0019607:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0019608:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019609:                             .target = instruction->targets[1],
0019610:                             .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019611:                             .aarch64 = true,
0019612:                             }))
0019613:                         {
0019614:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019615:                             return result;
0019616:                         }
0019617:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0019618:                     }
0019619:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_SWITCH)
0019620:                     {
0019621:                         // A case immediate carries the switched type's own
0019622:                         // bits while a register may hold that value extended
0019623:                         // past them, so a 32-bit or narrower type is compared
0019624:                         // at 32 bits -- the same rule the x86-64 branch above
0019625:                         // and the AArch64 selector both apply.
0019626:                         bool compare_words = false;
0019627:                         if (instruction->operand_count && instruction->operands[0].value < function->value_count)
0019628:                         {
0019629:                             IrType* switch_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[instruction->operands[0].value].canonical_type);
0019630:                             compare_words = switch_type && (switch_type->kind == IR_TYPE_BOOLEAN ||
0019631:                                                             (switch_type->kind == IR_TYPE_INTEGER && switch_type->bit_width <= 32));
0019632:                         }
0019633:                         c_a64_load(&emitter, 9, instruction->operands[0]);
0019634:                         for (u32 case_index = 0; case_index < instruction->immediate_count; case_index += 1)
0019635:                         {
0019636:                             u64 immediate = instruction->immediates[case_index];
0019637:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd280000a | ((u32)(immediate & 0xffff) << 5));
0019638:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2a0000a | ((u32)((immediate >> 16) & 0xffff) << 5));
0019639:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2c0000a | ((u32)((immediate >> 32) & 0xffff) << 5));
0019640:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xf2e0000a | ((u32)((immediate >> 48) & 0xffff) << 5));
0019641:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, compare_words ? 0x6b0a013f : 0xeb0a013f);
0019642:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x54000041);
0019643:                             if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019644:                                 .target = instruction->targets[case_index],
0019645:                                 .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019646:                                 .aarch64 = true,
0019647:                                 }))
0019648:                             {
0019649:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019650:                                 return result;
0019651:                             }
0019652:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0019653:                         }
0019654:                         if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019655:                             .target = instruction->targets[instruction->target_count - 1],
0019656:                             .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019657:                             .aarch64 = true,
0019658:                             }))
0019659:                         {
0019660:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019661:                             return result;
0019662:                         }
0019663:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0019664:                     }
0019665:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INLINE_ASSEMBLY)
0019666:                     {
0019667:                         IrInstructionExtra asm_extra = ir_instruction_extra(function, ir_instruction_self_id(function, instruction));
0019668:                         bool empty = !asm_extra.literal.length;
0019669:                         bool brk = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("brk #0"));
0019670:                         u32 nop_count = codegen_canonical_a64_nop_count(asm_extra.literal);
0019671:                         bool nop = nop_count != 0;
0019672:                         bool yield = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("yield"));
0019673:                         bool wait_event = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("wfe"));
0019674:                         bool wait_interrupt = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("wfi"));
0019675:                         bool send_event = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("sev"));
0019676:                         bool send_event_local = string_equal(asm_extra.literal, S8("sevl"));
0019677:                         u32 jump_target_index = 0;
0019678:                         bool jump_label = codegen_inline_assembly_jump_target(function, instruction, asm_extra.literal, S8("b %l"), &jump_target_index);
0019679:                         if ((!empty && !brk && !nop && !yield && !wait_event && !wait_interrupt && !send_event && !send_event_local && !jump_label) ||
0019680:                             (jump_label && instruction->target_count < 2) || instruction->operand_count != instruction->immediate_count ||
0019681:                             asm_extra.operand_name_count != instruction->operand_count ||
0019682:                             (instruction->operand_count && (!instruction->operands || !instruction->immediates || !asm_extra.operand_names)) ||
0019683:                             (asm_extra.clobber_count && !asm_extra.clobbers))
0019684:                         {
0019685:                             result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019686:                             return result;
0019687:                         }
0019688:                         u32* asm_registers = arena_allocate(arena, u32, instruction->operand_count ? instruction->operand_count : 1);
0019689:                         bool* asm_indirect = arena_allocate(arena, bool, instruction->operand_count ? instruction->operand_count : 1);
0019690:                         bool used_asm_registers[8] = {0};
0019691:                         // x16 is a valid eighth operand register.  Keep the
0019692:                         // address scratch separate so an indirect eighth
0019693:                         // output cannot overwrite its own pointer.
0019694:                         u32 asm_address_register = 17;
0019695:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0019696:                         {
0019697:                             u64 constraint = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0019698:                             if (!codegen_inline_assembly_constraint_shape_valid(constraint, operand_index, instruction->operand_count, 0) ||
0019699:                                 (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) != IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_R)
0019700:                             {
0019701:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019702:                                 return result;
0019703:                             }
0019704:                             IrValueId operand = instruction->operands[operand_index];
0019705:                             if (operand.value >= function->value_count || function->values[operand.value].definition.value >= function->instruction_count)
0019706:                             {
0019707:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019708:                                 return result;
0019709:                             }
0019710:                             IrInstruction* definition = function->instructions + function->values[operand.value].definition.value;
0019711:                             asm_indirect[operand_index] = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0019712:                                                           definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE;
0019713:                             IrType* operand_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[operand.value].canonical_type);
0019714:                             if ((constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0)
0019715:                             {
0019716:                                 if (codegen_inline_assembly_type_class(operand_type) == IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_OPERAND_CLASS_INVALID)
0019717:                                 {
0019718:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019719:                                     return result;
0019720:                                 }
0019721:                                 u32 match_index = IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(constraint);
0019722:                                 IrValueId output = instruction->operands[match_index];
0019723:                                 u64 output_constraint = instruction->immediates[match_index];
0019724:                                 IrType* output_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[output.value].canonical_type);
0019725:                                 if ((output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) == 0 ||
0019726:                                     (output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0 ||
0019727:                                     (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) !=
0019728:                                         (output_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_CLASS_MASK) ||
0019729:                                     !codegen_inline_assembly_types_compatible(output_type, operand_type))
0019730:                                 {
0019731:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019732:                                     return result;
0019733:                                 }
0019734:                                 for (u32 previous_index = 0; previous_index < operand_index; previous_index += 1)
0019735:                                 {
0019736:                                     u64 previous_constraint = instruction->immediates[previous_index];
0019737:                                     if ((previous_constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH) != 0 &&
0019738:                                         IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_MATCH_INDEX(previous_constraint) == match_index)
0019739:                                     {
0019740:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019741:                                         return result;
0019742:                                     }
0019743:                                 }
0019744:                                 asm_registers[operand_index] = asm_registers[match_index];
0019745:                             }
0019746:                             else
0019747:                             {
0019748:                                 u32 register_index = 0;
0019749:                                 bool found = false;
0019750:                                 for (u32 candidate = 0; candidate < BUSTER_ARRAY_LENGTH(used_asm_registers); candidate += 1)
0019751:                                 {
0019752:                                     if (!used_asm_registers[candidate])
0019753:                                     {
0019754:                                         used_asm_registers[candidate] = true;
0019755:                                         register_index = 9 + candidate;
0019756:                                         found = true;
0019757:                                         break;
0019758:                                     }
0019759:                                 }
0019760:                                 if (!found)
0019761:                                 {
0019762:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019763:                                     return result;
0019764:                                 }
0019765:                                 asm_registers[operand_index] = register_index;
0019766:                             }
0019767:                             if (!operand_type || !operand_type->layout.resolved || operand_type->layout.size > UINT32_MAX ||
0019768:                                 !codegen_canonical_a64_asm_memory_width((u32)operand_type->layout.size))
0019769:                             {
0019770:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019771:                                 return result;
0019772:                             }
0019773:                             if ((constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) == 0 ||
0019774:                                 (constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_READ_WRITE) != 0)
0019775:                             {
0019776:                                 if (asm_indirect[operand_index])
0019777:                                 {
0019778:                                     if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, asm_address_register, value_offsets[operand.value], 8, false, false) ||
0019779:                                         !codegen_canonical_a64_memory_operation_base(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], 0, (u32)operand_type->layout.size, false, false,
0019780:                                                                                       asm_address_register))
0019781:                                     {
0019782:                                         result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019783:                                         return result;
0019784:                                     }
0019785:                                 }
0019786:                                 else if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], value_offsets[operand.value],
0019787:                                                                                          (u32)operand_type->layout.size, false, false))
0019788:                                 {
0019789:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019790:                                     return result;
0019791:                                 }
0019792:                             }
0019793:                         }
0019794:                         if (!empty)
0019795:                         {
0019796:                             if (nop)
0019797:                             {
0019798:                                 for (u32 nop_index = 0; nop_index < nop_count; nop_index += 1)
0019799:                                 {
0019800:                                     codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd503201f);
0019801:                                 }
0019802:                             }
0019803:                             else if (!jump_label)
0019804:                             {
0019805:                                 codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, brk              ? 0xd4200000

0019809:                                                           : send_event     ? 0xd503209f
0019810:                                                                            : 0xd50320bf);
0019811:                             }
0019812:                         }
0019813:                         for (u32 operand_index = 0; operand_index < instruction->operand_count; operand_index += 1)
0019814:                         {
0019815:                             u64 constraint = instruction->immediates[operand_index];
0019816:                             if ((constraint & IR_INLINE_ASSEMBLY_CONSTRAINT_OUTPUT) == 0)
0019817:                             {
0019818:                                 continue;
0019819:                             }
0019820:                             IrValueId place_id = instruction->operands[operand_index];
0019821:                             if (place_id.value >= function->value_count || function->values[place_id.value].definition.value >= function->instruction_count)
0019822:                             {
0019823:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_INVALID_IR;
0019824:                                 return result;

0019827:                             if (!output_type || !output_type->layout.resolved || output_type->layout.size > UINT32_MAX ||
0019828:                                 !codegen_canonical_a64_asm_memory_width((u32)output_type->layout.size))
0019829:                             {
0019830:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_UNSUPPORTED_INSTRUCTION;
0019831:                                 return result;
0019832:                             }

0019834:                             bool output_indirect = definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_GLOBAL || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_INDEX ||
0019835:                                                    definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_FIELD || definition->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEREFERENCE;
0019836:                             if (output_indirect)
0019837:                             {
0019838:                                 if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, asm_address_register, value_offsets[place_id.value], 8, false, false) ||

0019840:                                                                                    (u32)output_type->layout.size, true, false, asm_address_register))
0019841:                                 {
0019842:                                     result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019843:                                     return result;
0019844:                                 }
0019845:                             }
0019846:                             else if (!codegen_canonical_a64_frame_memory_operation(&buffer, asm_registers[operand_index], value_offsets[place_id.value],
0019847:                                                                                    (u32)output_type->layout.size, true, false))
0019848:                             {
0019849:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019850:                                 return result;
0019851:                             }
0019852:                         }

0019857:                                 .offset = (u32)buffer.count,

0019860:                             {

0019865:                         }
0019866:                         else if (instruction->target_count)
0019867:                         {
0019868:                             if (!c_branch_patch_push(&emitter, (CCanonicalBranchPatch){
0019869:                                 .target = instruction->targets[0],
0019870:                                 .offset = (u32)buffer.count,
0019871:                                 .aarch64 = true,
0019872:                                 }))
0019873:                             {
0019874:                                 result.error = CODEGEN_ERROR_CAPACITY;
0019875:                                 return result;
0019876:                             }
0019877:                             codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0x14000000);
0019878:                         }
0019879:                     }
0019880:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_DEBUG_TRAP)
0019881:                     {
0019882:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd4200000);
0019883:                     }
0019884:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_UNREACHABLE)
0019885:                     {
0019886:                         codegen_emit_u32(&buffer, 0xd4200000);
0019887:                     }
0019888:                     else if (instruction->opcode == IR_OPCODE_RETURN)
0019889:                     {
0019890:                         if (instruction->operand_count)
0019891:                         {
0019892:                             IrValueId return_value = instruction->operands[0];
0019893:                             IrType* return_type = ir_type_from_id(&program->types, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type);
0019894:                             u32 return_parts = 0;
0019895:                             bool aggregate_return =
0019896:                                 codegen_canonical_integer_aggregate_parts(program, function->values[return_value.value].canonical_type, &return_parts);

0019899:                             bool return_hfa = aggregate_return_abi.part_count != 0;
0019900:                             for (u32 part = 0; part < aggregate_return_abi.part_count; part += 1)
0019901:                             {
0019902:                                 return_hfa &= codegen_canonical_abi_part_is_float(aggregate_return_abi.parts[part].abi_class);
0019903:                             }
0019904:                             u32 widened_lane_count = 0;
